### Java Unit Tests and Outputs

```java
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonGenerator;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.util.JsonGeneratorDelegate;

public class DepthTrackingJsonGenerator extends JsonGeneratorDelegate {
    private int depth = 0;

    public DepthTrackingJsonGenerator(JsonGenerator d) {
        super(d);
    }

    @Override
    public void writeStartObject() {
        super.writeStartObject();
        depth++;
    }

    @Override
    public void writeEndObject() {
        super.writeEndObject();
        depth--;
    }

    /**
     * returns a 0 - based depth within the object graph of the current object being serialized.
     */
    public int getCurrentDepth() {
        return Math.max(0, depth - 1);
    }

    // Other overridden methods as needed

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        JsonGenerator mockJsonGen = new MockJsonGenerator();
        DepthTrackingJsonGenerator jsonGen = new DepthTrackingJsonGenerator(mockJsonGen);

        jsonGen.writeStartObject(); // depth should be 1
        System.out.println("Depth after writeStartObject: " + jsonGen.getCurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 1

        jsonGen.writeEndObject(); // depth should be 0
        System.out.println("Depth after writeEndObject: " + jsonGen.getCurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 0

        jsonGen.writeStartObject(); // depth should be 1
        jsonGen.writeStartObject(); // depth should be 2
        System.out.println("Depth after two writeStartObject calls: " + jsonGen.getCurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 2

        jsonGen.writeEndObject(); // depth should be 1
        System.out.println("Depth after one writeEndObject call: " + jsonGen.getCurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 1

        jsonGen.writeEndObject(); // depth should be 0
        System.out.println("Depth after another writeEndObject call: " + jsonGen.getCurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 0
    }
}

class MockJsonGenerator implements JsonGenerator {
    @Override
    public void close() throws IOException {}

    @Override
    public boolean isClosed() { return false; }

    @Override
    public Version version() { return null; }

    @Override
    public void flush() {}

    @Override
    public void writeStartArray() throws IOException {}

    @Override
    public void writeEndArray() throws IOException {}

    @Override
    public void writeStartObject() throws IOException {}

    @Override
    public void writeEndObject() throws IOException {}

    // Other methods not implemented for simplicity
}

```csharp
using System;
using System.Reflection;

public class ExampleClass
{
    private static readonly FieldInfo DEPTH_FIELD;
    private const int _NUM = 1;

    static ExampleClass()
    {
        // Assuming 'depthField' is a private field named 'DEPTH_FIELD' in this class or in a base class
        DEPTH_FIELD = typeof(ExampleClass).GetField("DEPTH_FIELD", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Instance);

        if (DEPTH_FIELD == null)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException("DEPTH_FIELD not found");
        }
    }

    // Placeholder field for demonstration purposes
    private int depthField;

    public ExampleClass(int depthValue)
    {
        this.depthField = depthValue;
    }

    public int CurrentDepth()
    {
        try
        {
            return depthField - _NUM;
        }
        catch (Exception e) when (e is TargetException || e is MethodAccessException)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(e);
        }
    }

    public static void Main()
    {
        ExampleClass obj = new ExampleClass(3);
        Console.WriteLine("Depth: " + obj.CurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 2

        ExampleClass obj2 = new ExampleClass(1);
        Console.WriteLine("Depth: " + obj2.CurrentDepth()); // Expected output: 0
    }
}

public static void main(String[] args) {
    Instances before = new Instances(5, 3); // Create a test instance set with 5 instances and 3 classes
    Instances after = new Instances(before); // Make a copy

    boolean altered = checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before, after);
    System.out.println("Test 1 - No changes: " + (altered == false));

    before.setClassIndex(2); // Change class index
    altered = checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before, after);
    System.out.println("Test 2 - Class index changed: " + (altered == true));

    Instances before2 = new Instances(5, 3);
    Instances after2 = new Instances(before2);
    after2.delete(); // Delete an instance
    altered = checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before2, after2);
    System.out.println("Test 3 - Instance deleted: " + (altered == true));

    before.set(0, new DenseInstance(before.numAttributes())); // Change first instance
    altered = checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before, after);
    System.out.println("Test 4 - Instance changed: " + (altered == true));

    Instances before3 = new Instances(5, 3);
    Instances after3 = new Instances(before3);
    after3.setWeight(0, 2.0); // Change weight of first instance
    altered = checkIfSchemeAltersTrainingData(before3, after3);
    System.out.println("Test 5 - Instance weight changed: " + (altered == true));
}

```java
import java.util.BitSet;

public class BitSetUnionSize {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit test 1
        BitSet set1 = new BitSet();
        BitSet set2 = new BitSet();
        set1.set(0);
        set1.set(2);
        set1.set(4);
        set2.set(1);
        set2.set(2);
        set2.set(5);
        BitSet union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        int unionSize = union.cardinality();
        if (unionSize != 6) {
            System.out.println("Unit test 1 failed. Expected 6, but got " + unionSize);
        } else {
            System.out.println("Unit test 1 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 2
        set1.clear();
        set2.clear();
        set1.set(10);
        set2.set(15);
        union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        unionSize = union.cardinality();
        if (unionSize != 2) {
            System.out.println("Unit test 2 failed. Expected 2, but got " + unionSize);
        } else {
            System.out.println("Unit test 2 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 3
        set1.clear();
        set2.clear();
        union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        unionSize = union.cardinality();
        if (unionSize != 0) {
            System.out.println("Unit test 3 failed. Expected 0, but got " + unionSize);
        } else {
            System.out.println("Unit test 3 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 4
        set1.clear();
        set2.clear();
        set1.set(3);
        set1.set(5);
        set1.set(7);
        set2.set(2);
        set2.set(4);
        union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        unionSize = union.cardinality();
        if (unionSize != 8) {
            System.out.println("Unit test 4 failed. Expected 8, but got " + unionSize);
        } else {
            System.out.println("Unit test 4 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 5
        set1.clear();
        set2.clear();
        set1.set(0);
        set2.set(1);
        union = (BitSet) set1.clone();
        union.or(set2);
        unionSize = union.cardinality();
        if (unionSize != 2) {
            System.out.println("Unit test 5 failed. Expected 2, but got " + unionSize);
        } else {
            System.out.println("Unit test 5 passed.");
        }
    }
}

```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;

class BitSetUtility
{
    private const int _NUM = 0;

    public static int UnionSize(long[] x, long[] y)
    {
        int lx = x.Length, ly = y.Length;
        int min = Math.Min(lx, ly);
        int i = _NUM, res = _NUM;
        for (; i < min; i++)
        {
            res += BitCount(x[i] | y[i]);
        }
        for (; i < lx; i++)
        {
            res += BitCount(x[i]);
        }
        for (; i < ly; i++)
        {
            res += BitCount(y[i]);
        }
        return res;
    }

    private static int BitCount(long value)
    {
        return Convert.ToString(value, 2).Count(c => c == '1');
    }

    static void Main()
    {
        // Unit test 1
        long[] bitset1 = { 0b10110 };
        long[] bitset2 = { 0b11001 };
        int union = UnionSize(bitset1, bitset2);
        if (union != 4) {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 1 failed. Expected 4, but got " + union);
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 1 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 2
        bitset1 = new long[] { 0b10000 };
        bitset2 = new long[] { 0b01000 };
        union = UnionSize(bitset1, bitset2);
        if (union != 3) {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 2 failed. Expected 3, but got " + union);
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 2 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 3
        bitset1 = new long[] { 0b00000 };
        bitset2 = new long[] { 0b00000 };
        union = UnionSize(bitset1, bitset2);
        if (union != 0) {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 3 failed. Expected 0, but got " + union);
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 3 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 4
        bitset1 = new long[] { 0b11110 };
        bitset2 = new long[] { 0b11011 };
        union = UnionSize(bitset1, bitset2);
        if (union != 7) {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 4 failed. Expected 7, but got " + union);
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 4 passed.");
        }

        // Unit test 5
        bitset1 = new long[] { 0b00100 };
        bitset2 = new long[] { 0b00010 };
        union = UnionSize(bitset1, bitset2);
        if (union != 3) {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 5 failed. Expected 3, but got " + union);
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Unit test 5 passed.");
        }
    }
}

```java
import java.util.*;

public class TestOrder {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Local entries
        Map<String, String> local = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        local.put("key1", "val1");
        local.put("key2", "localVal2");
        local.put("key3", "val3");

        // Remote entries (has a value for key2, in middle)
        Map<String, String> remote = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        remote.put("key2", "remoteVal2");

        // Test: for each local key, if there's a remote override, use it
        Map<String, String> merged = new LinkedHashMap<>();

        for (String key : local.keySet()) {
            if (remote.containsKey(key)) {
                merged.put(key, remote.get(key));
            } else {
                merged.put(key, local.get(key));
            }
        }

        // Unit tests
        System.out.println("Test 1: " + (merged.equals(local) ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
        System.out.println("Test 2: " + (merged.get("key1").equals("val1") && merged.get("key2").equals("remoteVal2") && merged.get("key3").equals("val3")) ? "Pass" : "Fail");
        System.out.println("Test 3: " + (merged.size() == local.size()) ? "Pass" : "Fail");
        System.out.println("Test 4: " + (merged.containsKey("key1") && merged.containsKey("key2") && merged.containsKey("key3")) ? "Pass" : "Fail");
        System.out.println("Test 5: " + (!merged.containsKey("nonExistentKey")) ? "Pass" : "Fail");

        // Output the merged map to check the order and values
        for (Map.Entry<String, String> entry : merged.entrySet()) {
            System.out.println(entry.getKey() + " = " + entry.getValue());
        }
    }
}

using System;

public class GridTest
{
    private const string _STR = "test";
    private const int _NUM = 1;
    private const bool _BOOL = true;

    private Grid grid = new Grid();

    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        GridTest test = new GridTest();
        test.TestReverseOrder4();
        Console.WriteLine("Test completed.");
    }

    // Helper method to create a version object
    private Version Version(int num)
    {
        return new Version(num, 0);
    }

    // Test method as described
    public void TestReverseOrder4()
    {
        Guid id = Guid.NewGuid();
        var cache = grid.InternalCache();
        var ctx = cache.Context();

        var entry1 = new GridCacheTestEntryEx(ctx, _STR);
        var entry2 = new GridCacheTestEntryEx(ctx, _STR);

        var ver1 = Version(_NUM);
        var ver2 = Version(_NUM);
        var ver3 = Version(_NUM);

        var v3k1 = entry1.AddLocal(_NUM, ver3, _NUM, _BOOL, _BOOL);
        var v3k2 = entry2.AddLocal(_NUM, ver3, _NUM, _BOOL, _BOOL);

        LinkCandidates(ctx, v3k1, v3k2);

        entry1.ReadyLocal(ver3);

        CheckLocal(v3k1, ver3, _BOOL, _BOOL, _BOOL);
        CheckLocal(v3k2, ver3, _BOOL, _BOOL, _BOOL);

        var v1k1 = entry1.AddLocal(_NUM, ver1, _NUM, _BOOL, _BOOL);
        var v1k2 = entry2.AddLocal(_NUM, ver1, _NUM, _BOOL, _BOOL);

        // Unit tests
        Console.WriteLine("Test 1: " + (v3k1.LinkedCandidate == v3k2 && v3k2.LinkedCandidate == v3k1 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
        Console.WriteLine("Test 2: " + (v1k1.LinkedCandidate == null && v1k2.LinkedCandidate == null ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
        Console.WriteLine("Test 3: " + (v3k1.Flag1 && v3k1.Flag2 && !v3k1.CandidateVersion.Equals(ver1) ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
        Console.WriteLine("Test 4: " + (!entry1.IsReadyForVersion(ver1) && entry1.IsReadyForVersion(ver3) ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
        Console.WriteLine("Test 5: " + (v3k1.Num == _NUM && v3k2.Num == _NUM ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
    }

    // Dummy implementation of LinkCandidates
    private void LinkCandidates(Context ctx, Candidate c1, Candidate c2)
    {
        c1.LinkedCandidate = c2;
        c2.LinkedCandidate = c1;
    }

    // Dummy implementation of CheckLocal
    private void CheckLocal(Candidate candidate, Version version, bool b1, bool b2, bool b3)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"CheckLocal: Candidate={candidate}, Version={version}, Flags={b1},{b2},{b3}");
    }
}

public class DialogHandler {

    // This method will be called before add to replace the .
    // Actions to perform when the user has closed the dialog with the OK button.
    public void onDialogOkClosed() {
        System.out.println("OK button clicked. Performing close actions...");
        // Place actual close actions here
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        DialogHandler handler = new DialogHandler();
        // Simulate user clicking the OK button in the dialog
        handler.onDialogOkClosed();

        // Unit tests
        testOnDialogOkClosed();
    }

    public static void testOnDialogOkClosed() {
        DialogHandler handler = new DialogHandler();
        String output = "";
        try {
            System.setOut(new java.io.PrintStream(new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()));
            handler.onDialogOkClosed();
            output = System.out.toString().trim();
        } finally {
            System.setOut(System.out);
        }
        assert "OK button clicked. Performing close actions...".equals(output) : "Test failed";
    }
}

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace MyApp
{
    public class AddUserFields
    {
        public class AttributeSpec
        {
            public string Name { get; set; }
            public AttributeSpec(string name)
            {
                Name = name;
            }
        }
    }

    public interface IModifyL
    {
        void SetModifiedStatus(object sender, bool modified);
    }

    public class ModifyL : IModifyL
    {
        public void SetModifiedStatus(object sender, bool modified)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Modified status set to: " + modified);
        }
    }

    public class Filter
    {
        public void SetAttributeSpecs(List<AddUserFields.AttributeSpec> specs)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Specs set with {0} attribute(s):", specs.Count);
            foreach (var spec in specs)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("- {0}", spec.Name);
            }
        }
    }

    public class AddUserFieldsCustomizer
    {
        protected const int _NUM = 1;   // example value for demonstration
        protected const bool _BOOL = true;

        protected List<AddUserFields.AttributeSpec> m_listModel;
        protected IModifyL m_modifyL;
        protected Filter m_filter;

        public AddUserFieldsCustomizer()
        {
            // For demonstration, initialize with 3 attributes
            m_listModel = new List<AddUserFields.AttributeSpec>
            {
                new AddUserFields.AttributeSpec("FirstName"),
                new AddUserFields.AttributeSpec("LastName"),
                new AddUserFields.AttributeSpec("Email")
            };
            m_modifyL = new ModifyL();
            m_filter = new Filter();
        }

        public virtual void ClosingOK()
        {
            List<AddUserFields.AttributeSpec> specs = new List<AddUserFields.AttributeSpec>();
            for (int i = _NUM; i < m_listModel.Count; i++)
            {
                AddUserFields.AttributeSpec a = (AddUserFields.AttributeSpec)m_listModel[i];
                specs.Add(a);
            }
            if (m_modifyL != null)
            {
                m_modifyL.SetModifiedStatus(this, _BOOL);
            }
            m_filter.SetAttributeSpecs(specs);
        }

        public static void Main(string[] args) {
            AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer();
            customizer.ClosingOK();

            // Unit tests
            testClosingOK();
        }

        public static void testClosingOK() {
            AddUserFieldsCustomizer customizer = new AddUserFieldsCustomizer();
            string output = "";
            try {
                System.setOut(new java.io.PrintStream(new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream()));
                customizer.ClosingOK();
                output = System.out.toString().Trim();
            } finally {
                System.setOut(System.out);
            }
            Console.WriteLine("Test passed");
        }
    }
}

using System;

public class ClassAttribute
{

    public ClassAttribute(string[] values)
    {
        _values = values;
    }

    public int NumValues()
    {
        return _values.Length;
    }

    public string Value(int i)
    {
        return _values[i];
    }
}

    public override string ToString()
    {
        return "DummyKernel";
    }

{
    public override string ToString()
    {
        return "LogisticModel";
    }

public class Classifier
{
    public Logistic m_logistic;

    public Classifier()
    {
        m_logistic = new Logistic();
    }

    public Classifier(bool hasLogistic)
    {
        m_logistic = hasLogistic ? new Logistic() : null;
    }

    public override string ToString()
    {
        return "Classifier";
    }

public class MyPrinter
{
    private string _STR = "|";
    private int _NUM = 0;

    public ClassAttribute m_classAttribute;
    public Kernel m_kernel;
    public bool m_fitLogisticModels;
    public Classifier[][] m_classifiers;

    public MyPrinter()
    {
        // Example Initialization
        m_classAttribute = new ClassAttribute(new string[] { "A", "B", "C" });
        m_kernel = new Kernel();
        m_fitLogisticModels = true;
        int n = m_classAttribute.NumValues();
        m_classifiers = new Classifier[n][];
        for (int i = 0; i < n; i++)
        {
            m_classifiers[i] = new Classifier[n];
            for (int j = 0; j < n; j++)
            {
                // add a Logistic only for i + j even
                m_classifiers[i][j] = new Classifier((i + j) % 2 == 0);
            }
        }
    }

    public override string ToString()
    {
        StringBuilder text = new StringBuilder();
        if (m_classAttribute == null)
        {
            return _STR;
        }
        try
        {
            text.Append(_STR);
            text.Append(_STR + m_kernel.ToString() + _STR);
            for (int i = _NUM; i < m_classAttribute.NumValues(); i++)
            {
                for (int j = i + _NUM; j < m_classAttribute.NumValues(); j++)
                {
                    text.Append(_STR + m_classAttribute.Value(i) + _STR + m_classAttribute.Value(j) + _STR);
                    text.Append(m_classifiers[i][j]);
                    if (m_fitLogisticModels)
                    {
                        text.Append(_STR);
                        if (m_classifiers[i][j].m_logistic == null)
                        {
                            text.Append(_STR);
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            text.Append(m_classifiers[i][j].m_logistic);
                        }
                    }
                    text.Append(_STR);
                }
            }
        }
        catch (Exception)
        {
            return _STR;
        }
        return text.ToString();
    }
}

class Program
{
    static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        MyPrinter printer = new MyPrinter();
        Console.WriteLine(printer.ToString());
    }
}

**Java Code with Unit Tests**

```java
import java.util.*;

public class Sector {
    private double minLatitude;
    private double maxLatitude;
    private double minLongitude;
    private double maxLongitude;

    public Sector(double minLatitude, double maxLatitude, double minLongitude, double maxLongitude) {
        this.minLatitude = minLatitude;
        this.maxLatitude = maxLatitude;
        this.minLongitude = minLongitude;
        this.maxLongitude = maxLongitude;
    }

    public Sector union(Sector sector) {
        double minLat = Math.min(this.minLatitude, sector.minLatitude);
        double maxLat = Math.max(this.maxLatitude, sector.maxLatitude);
        double minLon = Math.min(this.minLongitude, sector.minLongitude);
        double maxLon = Math.max(this.maxLongitude, sector.maxLongitude);
        return new Sector(minLat, maxLat, minLon, maxLon);
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return String.format("Sector[minLatitude=%.2f, maxLatitude=%.2f, minLongitude=%.2f, maxLongitude=%.2f]", 
                             minLatitude, maxLatitude, minLongitude, maxLongitude);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Sector sector1 = new Sector(-20.0, 10.0, 30.0, 70.0);
        Sector sector2 = new Sector(0.0, 40.0, 20.0, 50.0);

        Sector unionSector = sector1.union(sector2);

        System.out.println("Sector 1: " + sector1);
        System.out.println("Sector 2: " + sector2);
        System.out.println("Union Sector: " + unionSector);

        // Unit Tests
        Sector test1 = new Sector(0.0, 50.0, -90.0, 80.0);
        Sector test2 = new Sector(-30.0, 30.0, -70.0, 40.0);
        Sector expected1 = new Sector(-30.0, 50.0, -90.0, 80.0);
        Sector result1 = test1.union(test2);
        assert result1.equals(expected1) : "Test 1 failed";

        Sector test3 = new Sector(45.0, 60.0, 10.0, 30.0);
        Sector test4 = new Sector(30.0, 70.0, 20.0, 50.0);
        Sector expected2 = new Sector(30.0, 70.0, 10.0, 50.0);
        Sector result2 = test3.union(test4);
        assert result2.equals(expected2) : "Test 2 failed";

        Sector test5 = new Sector(-60.0, -30.0, -80.0, -70.0);
        Sector test6 = new Sector(-90.0, 0.0, -100.0, -60.0);
        Sector expected3 = new Sector(-90.0, 0.0, -100.0, -30.0);
        Sector result3 = test5.union(test6);
        assert result3.equals(expected3) : "Test 3 failed";

        System.out.println("All unit tests passed!");
    }
}

**C# Code with Unit Tests**

```csharp
using System;

public class Angle
{
    public double Degrees { get; }

    public Angle(double degrees)
    {
        Degrees = degrees;
    }

    public override string ToString()
    {
        return $"{Degrees}°";
    }
}

public class Sector
{
    public Angle minLatitude { get; }
    public Angle maxLatitude { get; }
    public Angle minLongitude { get; }
    public Angle maxLongitude { get; }

    public Sector(Angle minLat, Angle maxLat, Angle minLon, Angle maxLon)
    {
        this.minLatitude = minLat;
        this.maxLatitude = maxLat;
        this.minLongitude = minLon;
        this.maxLongitude = maxLon;
    }

    public Sector Union(Sector that)
    {
        if (that == null) return this;
        Angle minLat = this.minLatitude;
        Angle maxLat = this.maxLatitude;
        Angle minLon = this.minLongitude;
        Angle maxLon = this.maxLongitude;

        if (that.minLatitude.Degrees < this.minLatitude.Degrees)
            minLat = that.minLatitude;
        if (that.maxLatitude.Degrees > this.maxLatitude.Degrees)
            maxLat = that.maxLatitude;
        if (that.minLongitude.Degrees < this.minLongitude.Degrees)
            minLon = that.minLongitude;
        if (that.maxLongitude.Degrees > this.maxLongitude.Degrees)
            maxLon = that.maxLongitude;
        return new Sector(minLat, maxLat, minLon, maxLon);
    }

    public override string ToString()
    {
        return $"Sector: MinLat={minLatitude}, MaxLat={maxLatitude}, MinLon={minLongitude}, MaxLon={maxLongitude}";
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        Sector sector1 = new Sector(new Angle(-20), new Angle(10), new Angle(30), new Angle(70));
        Sector sector2 = new Sector(new Angle(0), new Angle(40), new Angle(20), new Angle(50));

        Sector unionSector = sector1.Union(sector2);

        Console.WriteLine("Sector 1: " + sector1);
        Console.WriteLine("Sector 2: " + sector2);
        Console.WriteLine("Union sector: " + unionSector);

        // Unit Tests
        Sector test1 = new Sector(new Angle(-30), new Angle(50), new Angle(-90), new Angle(80));
        Sector test2 = new Sector(new Angle(-30), new Angle(30), new Angle(-70), new Angle(40));
        Sector expected1 = new Sector(new Angle(-30), new Angle(50), new Angle(-90), new Angle(80));
        Sector result1 = test1.Union(test2);
        if (!result1.Equals(expected1))
            Console.WriteLine("Test 1 failed");

        Sector test3 = new Sector(new Angle(45), new Angle(60), new Angle(10), new Angle(30));
        Sector test4 = new Sector(new Angle(30), new Angle(70), new Angle(20), new Angle(50));
        Sector expected2 = new Sector(new Angle(30), new Angle(70), new Angle(10), new Angle(50));
        Sector result2 = test3.Union(test4);
        if (!result2.Equals(expected2))
            Console.WriteLine("Test 2 failed");

        Sector test5 = new Sector(new Angle(-60), new Angle(-30), new Angle(-80), new Angle(-70));
        Sector test6 = new Sector(new Angle(-90), new Angle(0), new Angle(-100), new Angle(-60));
        Sector expected3 = new Sector(new Angle(-90), new Angle(0), new Angle(-100), new Angle(-30));
        Sector result3 = test5.Union(test6);
        if (!result3.Equals(expected3))
            Console.WriteLine("Test 3 failed");

        Console.WriteLine("All unit tests passed!");
    }
}

```java
public class DoubleEqualityChecker {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        double a = 0.1 + 0.2;
        double b = 0.3;
        double epsilon = 1e-9;

        if (Math.abs(a - b) < epsilon) {
            System.out.println("a and b are equal (within tolerance).");
        } else {
            System.out.println("a and b are NOT equal.");
        }

        // Unit tests
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(0.1 + 0.2, 0.3, "Should be equal within tolerance", true);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(0.5, 0.5, "Equal values should be considered equal", true);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(0.1, 0.2, "Not equal values should not be considered equal", false);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(-0.1, -0.2, "-Equal values should be considered equal", true);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(1e-9, 0.0, "Values very close to zero should be considered equal within tolerance", true);
    }

    public static void testDoubleEqualityChecker(double a, double b, String message, boolean expectedResult) {
        boolean result = Math.abs(a - b) < 1e-9;
        System.out.println(message + ": " + (result == expectedResult ? "Passed" : "Failed"));
    }
}
```

using System;

class Program
{
    private const double _NUM = 1e-9;

    private static bool IsDoubleEqual(double value, double valueToCompare)
    {
        return (Math.Abs(value - valueToCompare) < _NUM);
    }

    static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        double a = 0.1 + 0.2;
        double b = 0.3;

        Console.WriteLine($"Is {a} equal to {b}? {IsDoubleEqual(a, b)}");

        // Unit tests
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(0.1 + 0.2, 0.3, "Should be equal within tolerance", true);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(0.5, 0.5, "Equal values should be considered equal", true);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(0.1, 0.2, "Not equal values should not be considered equal", false);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(-0.1, -0.2, "-Equal values should be considered equal", true);
        testDoubleEqualityChecker(1e-9, 0.0, "Values very close to zero should be considered equal within tolerance", true);
    }

    public static void testDoubleEqualityChecker(double a, double b, string message, bool expectedResult) {
        bool result = IsDoubleEqual(a, b);
        Console.WriteLine(message + ": " + (result == expectedResult ? "Passed" : "Failed"));
    }
}

### Java Code with Unit Tests

```java
import java.util.*;

class Tuple {
    private Object[] fields;

    public Tuple(Object[] fields) {
        this.fields = fields;
    }

    public Object getField(int i) {
        return fields[i];
    }
}

class TupleIterator implements Iterator<Tuple> {
    private List<Tuple> tuples;
    private int index = 0;

    public TupleIterator(List<Tuple> tuples) {
        this.tuples = tuples;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean hasNext() {
        return index < tuples.size();
    }

    @Override
    public Tuple next() {
        return tuples.get(index++);
    }
}

public class SortedFieldValues {
    // get a sorted array containing all column values for a given tuple iterator and field
    public static Comparable[] getSortedFieldValues(Iterator<Tuple> it, int field) {
        List<Comparable> values = new ArrayList<>();
        while (it.hasNext()) {
            Object val = it.next().getField(field);
            if (val instanceof Comparable) {
                values.add((Comparable) val);
            }
        }
        Collections.sort(values);
        return values.toArray(new Comparable[0]);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<Tuple> tuples = Arrays.asList(
            new Tuple(new Object[] {1, "Alice", 4.5}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {3, "Bob", 2.1}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {2, "Carol", 7.8}),
            new Tuple(new Object[] {4, "Dave", 5.3})
        );

        TupleIterator it = new TupleIterator(tuples);

        // Test Case 1
        Comparable[] sortedField0 = getSortedFieldValues(it, 0);
        System.out.println("Sorted values in field 0: " + Arrays.toString(sortedField0));
        assert Arrays.equals(sortedField0, new Integer[]{1, 2, 3, 4});

        // Test Case 2
        Comparable[] sortedField2 = getSortedFieldValues(it, 2);
        System.out.println("Sorted values in field 2: " + Arrays.toString(sortedField2));
        assert Arrays.equals(sortedField2, new Double[]{2.1, 4.5, 5.3, 7.8});

        // Test Case 3
        Comparable[] sortedField1 = getSortedFieldValues(it, 1);
        System.out.println("Sorted values in field 1: " + Arrays.toString(sortedField1));
        assert Arrays.equals(sortedField1, new String[]{"Alice", "Bob", "Carol", "Dave"});

        // Test Case 4
        Comparable[] sortedEmpty = getSortedFieldValues(new TupleIterator(Collections.emptyList()), 0);
        System.out.println("Sorted values in empty field: " + Arrays.toString(sortedEmpty));
        assert Arrays.equals(sortedEmpty, new Object[]{});

        // Test Case 5
        Comparable[] sortedNull = getSortedFieldValues(it, -1); // Invalid field index
        System.out.println("Sorted values in invalid field: " + Arrays.toString(sortedNull));
        assert Arrays.equals(sortedNull, new Comparable[0]);
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class TupleSet : IEnumerable<Dictionary<string, object>>
{
    private List<Dictionary<string, object>> tuples = new List<Dictionary<string, object>>();

    public void Add(Dictionary<string, object> tuple)
    {
        tuples.Add(tuple);
    }

    public IEnumerator<Dictionary<string, object>> GetEnumerator()
    {
        return tuples.GetEnumerator();
    }

    IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
    {
        return GetEnumerator();
    }
}

public class DefaultLiteralComparator : IComparer<object>
{
    public static readonly DefaultLiteralComparator Instance = new DefaultLiteralComparator();

    public int Compare(object x, object y)
    {
        return Comparer<object>.Default.Compare(x, y);
    }
}

public class Program
{
    // get a sorted array containing all column values for a given tuple iterator and field
    public static object[] OrdinalArray(TupleSet tuples, string field)
    {
        return OrdinalArray(tuples, field, DefaultLiteralComparator.Instance);
    }

    public static object[] OrdinalArray(TupleSet tuples, string field, IComparer<object> comparer)
    {
        List<object> values = new List<object>();
        foreach (var tuple in tuples)
        {
            if (tuple.ContainsKey(field))
                values.Add(tuple[field]);
        }
        values.Sort(comparer);
        return values.ToArray();
    }

    static void Main()
    {
        TupleSet tuples = new TupleSet();
        tuples.Add(new Dictionary<string, object> { { "id", 3 }, { "name", "Alice" } });
        tuples.Add(new Dictionary<string, object> { { "id", 1 }, { "name", "Bob" } });
        tuples.Add(new Dictionary<string, object> { { "id", 2 }, { "name", "Charlie" } });

        object[] sortedIds = OrdinalArray(tuples, "id");
        Console.WriteLine("Sorted IDs:");
        foreach (var id in sortedIds)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(id);
        }

        // Test Case 1
        object[] sortedField0 = OrdinalArray(tuples, "id");
        Console.WriteLine("Sorted values in field 'id': " + string.Join(", ", sortedField0));
        Assert.AreEqual(new object[]{1, 2, 3}, sortedField0);

        // Test Case 2
        object[] sortedField1 = OrdinalArray(tuples, "name");
        Console.WriteLine("Sorted values in field 'name': " + string.Join(", ", sortedField1));
        Assert.AreEqual(new object[]{"Alice", "Bob", "Charlie"}, sortedField1);

        // Test Case 3
        object[] sortedEmpty = OrdinalArray(new TupleSet(), "id");
        Console.WriteLine("Sorted values in empty field: " + string.Join(", ", sortedEmpty));
        Assert.AreEqual(new object[]{}, sortedEmpty);

        // Test Case 4
        try
        {
            object[] sortedInvalid = OrdinalArray(tuples, null); // Invalid field index
            Console.WriteLine("Sorted values in invalid field: " + string.Join(", ", sortedInvalid));
        }
        catch (NullReferenceException ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Caught NullReferenceException for invalid field");
        }

        // Test Case 5
        try
        {
            object[] sortedNonExistent = OrdinalArray(tuples, "age"); // Non-existent field
            Console.WriteLine("Sorted values in non-existent field: " + string.Join(", ", sortedNonExistent));
        }
        catch (KeyNotFoundException ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Caught KeyNotFoundException for non-existent field");
        }
    }

    public static void Assert<T>(T actual, T expected)
    {
        if (!actual.Equals(expected))
        {
            throw new Exception($"Expected {expected}, but got {actual}");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.math.BigDecimal;

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("3");
        try {
            // Divide with an invalid rounding mode (-1 is not a valid RoundingMode)
            BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, 2, -1);
            System.out.println("Result: " + result);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            System.out.println("Exception caught: " + e);
        }
    }

    public static void TestDivideExceptionInvalidRM() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("3");
        try {
            // Divide with an invalid rounding mode (-1 is not a valid RoundingMode)
            BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, 2, -1);
            assert false : "Expected exception was not thrown";
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            assert true : "Exception caught: " + e;
        }
    }

    public static void TestDivideWithValidRM() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("3");
        try {
            // Divide with a valid rounding mode
            BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, 2, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);
            assert true : "Result: " + result;
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            assert false : "Unexpected exception was thrown";
        }
    }

    public static void TestDivideWithZero() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("10");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("0");
        try {
            // Divide by zero
            BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, 2, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);
            assert false : "Expected exception was not thrown";
        } catch (ArithmeticException e) {
            assert true : "Exception caught: " + e;
        }
    }

    public static void TestDivideWithLargeNumber() {
        BigDecimal a = new BigDecimal("12345678901234567890");
        BigDecimal b = new BigDecimal("98765432109876543210");
        try {
            // Divide large numbers
            BigDecimal result = a.divide(b, 2, BigDecimal.ROUND_HALF_UP);
            assert true : "Result: " + result;
        } catch (ArithmeticException e) {
            assert false : "Unexpected exception was thrown";
        }
    }
}

```csharp
using System;
using System.Numerics;

namespace BigDecimalTest
{
    public class BigDecimal
    {
        private BigInteger _unscaledValue;
        private int _scale;

        public BigDecimal(BigInteger unscaledValue, int scale)
        {
            _unscaledValue = unscaledValue;
            _scale = scale;
        }

        public BigDecimal Divide(BigDecimal other, int roundingMode)
        {
            // For test: only allow roundingMode == 0
            if (roundingMode != 0)
                throw new ArgumentException("Invalid rounding mode");

            // Dummy implementation
            return new BigDecimal(_unscaledValue / other._unscaledValue, _scale - other._scale);
        }
    }

    public static class Assert
    {
        public static void AreEqual(string expected, string actual, string message)
        {
            if (!string.Equals(expected, actual))
            {
                throw new Exception("Assert.AreEqual failed. " + message);
            }
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static readonly string _STR = "123";
        static readonly int _NUM = 1;

        static void Fail(string message)
        {
            throw new Exception("Test failed: " + message);
        }

        public static void TestDivideExceptionInvalidRM()
        {
            string a = _STR;
            int aScale = _NUM;
            string b = _STR;
            int bScale = _NUM;
            BigDecimal aNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(a), aScale);
            BigDecimal bNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(b), bScale);
            try
            {
                aNumber.Divide(bNumber, _NUM);
                Fail(_STR);
            }
            catch (ArgumentException e)
            {
                Assert.AreEqual(_STR, _STR, e.Message);
            }
        }

        public static void TestDivideWithValidRM()
        {
            string a = "10";
            int aScale = 1;
            string b = "3";
            int bScale = 1;
            BigDecimal aNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(a), aScale);
            BigDecimal bNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(b), bScale);
            try
            {
                // Divide with a valid rounding mode
                BigDecimal result = aNumber.Divide(bNumber, 0);
                Assert.AreEqual("3.33", result.ToString(), "Result: " + result);
            }
            catch (ArgumentException e)
            {
                Fail("Unexpected exception was thrown");
            }
        }

        public static void TestDivideWithZero()
        {
            string a = "10";
            int aScale = 1;
            string b = "0";
            int bScale = 1;
            BigDecimal aNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(a), aScale);
            BigDecimal bNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(b), bScale);
            try
            {
                // Divide by zero
                BigDecimal result = aNumber.Divide(bNumber, 0);
                Fail("Expected exception was not thrown");
            }
            catch (ArithmeticException e)
            {
                Assert.AreEqual("/ by zero", e.Message, "Exception caught: " + e);
            }
        }

        public static void TestDivideWithLargeNumber()
        {
            string a = "12345678901234567890";
            int aScale = 1;
            string b = "98765432109876543210";
            int bScale = 1;
            BigDecimal aNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(a), aScale);
            BigDecimal bNumber = new BigDecimal(new BigInteger(b), bScale);
            try
            {
                // Divide large numbers
                BigDecimal result = aNumber.Divide(bNumber, 0);
                Assert.AreEqual("0.1248596732269991", result.ToString(), "Result: " + result);
            }
            catch (ArithmeticException e)
            {
                Fail("Unexpected exception was thrown");
            }
        }

        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            TestDivideExceptionInvalidRM();
            TestDivideWithValidRM();
            TestDivideWithZero();
            TestDivideWithLargeNumber();
            Console.WriteLine("Test completed.");
        }
    }
}

using System;

public interface IScheduler {
    void Initialize(Config config, Config runtime);
    bool OnSchedule(PackingPlan packing);
    void Close();

}

    // Packing plan properties can be added here

public static class SchedulerUtils {
    public static bool SetLibSchedulerLocation(Config runtime, IScheduler scheduler, bool flag) {
        // Simulation of setting location
        return flag;
    }
}

    public static void Severe(string message) {
        Console.WriteLine("SEVERE: " + message);
    }
}

public class SchedulerExample : IScheduler {
    public void Initialize(Config config, Config runtime) {
        // Simulate initialization
        Console.WriteLine("Scheduler initialized with configs.");
    }

    public bool OnSchedule(PackingPlan packing) {
        // Simulate scheduling
        Console.WriteLine("Scheduler OnSchedule invoked.");
        return true;
    }

    public void Close() {
        Console.WriteLine("Scheduler closed.");
    }
}

public class Program {
    // Assumed placeholders for _BOOL and _STR
    private const bool _BOOL = true;
    private const string _STR = "Scheduling failed";

    public bool OnScheduleAsLibrary(Config config, Config runtime, IScheduler scheduler, PackingPlan packing) {
        bool ret = _BOOL;
        try {
            scheduler.Initialize(config, runtime);
            ret = scheduler.OnSchedule(packing);
            if (ret) {
                ret = SchedulerUtils.SetLibSchedulerLocation(runtime, scheduler, _BOOL);
            } else {
                LOG.Severe(_STR);
            }
        } finally {
            scheduler.Close();
        }
        return ret;
    }

    public static void Main(string[] args) {
        var config = new Config();
        var runtime = new Config();
        var scheduler = new SchedulerExample();
        var packing = new PackingPlan();
        var program = new Program();

        bool result = program.OnScheduleAsLibrary(config, runtime, scheduler, packing);
        Console.WriteLine("Result: " + result);

        // Unit tests
        IScheduler isScheduler = (IScheduler) scheduler;
        
        // Run unit tests and calculate pass rate
        bool[] testResults = {isScheduler != null};
        int passedTests = (int) System.Linq.Enumerable.Count(testResults, result => result);
        double passRate = ((double) passedTests / testResults.Length) * 100;
        
        Console.WriteLine("Unit Tests Pass Rate: " + passRate + "%");
    }
}

using System;

namespace CodeSnippetRunner
{
    public class RefType
    {
        private Type type;

        private RefType(Type type)
        {
            this.type = type;
        }

        public static RefType v(Type t)
        {
            return new RefType(t);
        }

        public bool Equals(Type t)
        {
            return type == t;
        }
    }

    public class Program
    {
        public static bool IsStringType(Type t)
        {
            return t.Equals(RefType.v(typeof(string)));
        }

        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(IsStringType(typeof(string))); // Expected: True
            Console.WriteLine(IsStringType(typeof(int)));    // Expected: False

            // Unit Tests
            Assert.IsTrue(IsStringType(typeof(string)), "Test 1 Failed");
            Assert.IsFalse(IsStringType(typeof(int)), "Test 2 Failed");
            Assert.IsTrue(IsStringType(typeof(object)), "Test 3 Failed");
            Assert.IsFalse(IsStringType(typeof(bool)), "Test 4 Failed");
            Assert.IsTrue(IsStringType(typeof(Class)), "Test 5 Failed");

            Console.WriteLine("All tests passed.");
        }
    }

    public static class Assert
    {
        public static void IsTrue(bool condition, string message)
        {
            if (!condition) throw new Exception(message);
        }

        public static void IsFalse(bool condition, string message)
        {
            if (condition) throw new Exception(message);
        }
    }
}

### Java Code with Unit Tests

```java
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class ApiExample {

    static class Entry {
        String user;
        String url;
        String data;

        Entry(String user, String url, String data) {
            this.user = user;
            this.url = url;
            this.data = data;
        }

        @Override
        public String toString() {
            return "Entry{" +
                    "user='" + user + '\'' +
                    ", url='" + url + '\'' +
                    ", data='" + data + '\'' +
                    '}';
        }
    }

    private final Map<String, Map<String, Entry>> entries = new HashMap<>();

    /**
     * API method. Returns an entry for the source indexed by the user and the url.
     * This method creates it if it didn't already exist.
     */
    public Entry updateDataFor(String user, String url, String newData) {
        entries.putIfAbsent(user, new HashMap<>());
        Map<String, Entry> urlMap = entries.get(user);

        Entry entry = urlMap.get(url);
        if (entry == null) {
            entry = new Entry(user, url, newData);
            urlMap.put(url, entry);
        } else {
            entry.data = newData; // Update data
        }
        return entry;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        ApiExample api = new ApiExample();

        // Unit Test 1: Create a new entry
        Entry e1 = api.updateDataFor("alice", "http://example.com/source1", "first update");
        assert e1 != null : "Entry should not be null";
        System.out.println(e1);

        // Unit Test 2: Update an existing entry
        Entry e2 = api.updateDataFor("alice", "http://example.com/source1", "second update");
        assert e2 != null && e2.data.equals("second update") : "Entry data should be updated";
        System.out.println(e2);

        // Unit Test 3: Create an entry for a new user
        Entry e3 = api.updateDataFor("bob", "http://example.com/source1", "bob's data");
        assert e3 != null : "Entry should not be null";
        System.out.println(e3);

        // Unit Test 4: Create an entry for a new URL
        Entry e4 = api.updateDataFor("alice", "http://example.com/source2", "new url data");
        assert e4 != null : "Entry should not be null";
        System.out.println(e4);

        // Unit Test 5: Handle duplicate entries
        Entry e5 = api.updateDataFor("alice", "http://example.com/source1", "third update");
        assert e5 != null && e5.data.equals("third update") : "Entry data should be updated";
        System.out.println(e5);
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Program
{
    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        var errors = new Errors();
        var user1 = new User("user1");
        var user2 = new User("user2");
        string id = "source1";
        string url = "http://example.com";
        string title = "Example Source";
        long? modTime = DateTime.Now.Ticks;
        long? createTime = DateTime.Now.Ticks;
        bool isAdmin = false;

        var service = new EntryService();

        // Unit Test 1: Create a new entry
        var entry1 = service.UpdateOrCreateSource(user1, id, url, title, modTime, createTime, isAdmin, errors);
        if (entry1 != null)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Entry created or updated:");
            Console.WriteLine($"Id: {entry1.Id}, Url: {entry1.Url}, Title: {entry1.Title}");
            assert entry1 != null : "Entry should not be null";
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Failed to create/update entry:");
            foreach (var error in errors.ErrorList)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  {error}");
            }
        }

        // Unit Test 2: Update an existing entry
        var sameEntry1 = service.UpdateOrCreateSource(user1, id, url, "New Title", modTime, createTime, isAdmin, errors);
        if (sameEntry1 != null)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"Entry id: {sameEntry1.Id}, title: {sameEntry1.Title}");
            assert sameEntry1.data.Equals("New Title") : "Entry data should be updated";
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Failed to create/update entry:");
            foreach (var error in errors.ErrorList)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  {error}");
            }
        }

        // Unit Test 3: Create an entry for a new user
        var entry2 = service.UpdateOrCreateSource(user2, id, url, title, modTime, createTime, isAdmin, errors);
        if (entry2 != null)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Entry created or updated:");
            Console.WriteLine($"Id: {entry2.Id}, Url: {entry2.Url}, Title: {entry2.Title}");
            assert entry2 != null : "Entry should not be null";
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Failed to create/update entry:");
            foreach (var error in errors.ErrorList)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  {error}");
            }
        }

        // Unit Test 4: Create an entry for a new URL
        var entry3 = service.UpdateOrCreateSource(user1, "source2", url, title, modTime, createTime, isAdmin, errors);
        if (entry3 != null)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Entry created or updated:");
            Console.WriteLine($"Id: {entry3.Id}, Url: {entry3.Url}, Title: {entry3.Title}");
            assert entry3 != null : "Entry should not be null";
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Failed to create/update entry:");
            foreach (var error in errors.ErrorList)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  {error}");
            }
        }

        // Unit Test 5: Handle duplicate entries
        var entry4 = service.UpdateOrCreateSource(user1, id, url, "Updated Again", modTime, createTime, isAdmin, errors);
        if (entry4 != null)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"Entry id: {entry4.Id}, title: {entry4.Title}");
            assert entry4.data.Equals("Updated Again") : "Entry data should be updated";
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Failed to create/update entry:");
            foreach (var error in errors.ErrorList)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"  {error}");
            }
        }
    }
}

public class Entry
{
    public string Id { get; set; }
    public string UserId { get; set; }
    public string Url { get; set; }
    public string Title { get; set; }
    public long ModTime { get; set; }
    public long CreateTime { get; set; }
}

public class User
{
    private string id;
    public User(string id)
    {
        this.id = id;
    }
    public string GetId()
    {
        return id;
    }
}

public class Errors
{
    public List<string> ErrorList { get; } = new List<string>();
    public static void Add(Errors errors, string error)
    {
        if (errors != null && error != null)
        {
            errors.ErrorList.Add(error);
        }
    }
}

public static class errorMessages
{
    public static string ErrorUserIsNull() => "User is null.";
    public static string ErrorUrlIsNull() => "URL is null.";
    public static string ErrorUrlIsEmpty() => "URL is empty.";
    public static string ErrorTitleIsNull() => "Title is null.";
    public static string ErrorTitleIsEmpty() => "Title is empty.";
    public static string ErrorModTimeIsNull() => "ModTime is null.";
    public static string ErrorCreateTimeIsNull() => "CreateTime is null.";
}

public class EntryService
{
    // Simulate storage with a static dictionary
    private static Dictionary<string, Entry> entries = new Dictionary<string, Entry>();

    public Entry UpdateOrCreateSource(User user, string id, string url, string title, long? modTime, long? createTime, bool isAdmin, Errors errors)
    {
        if (user == null)
        {
            Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorUserIsNull());
            return null;
        }
        if (url == null)
        {
            Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorUrlIsNull());
            return null;
        }
        Entry source = GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl(user.GetId(), url);
        if (source == null)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(url))
            {
                Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorUrlIsEmpty());
                return null;
            }
            if (title == null)
            {
                Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorTitleIsNull());
                return null;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(title))
            {
                Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorTitleIsEmpty());
                return null;
            }
            if (!modTime.HasValue)
            {
                Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorModTimeIsNull());
                return null;
            }
            if (!createTime.HasValue)
            {
                Errors.Add(errors, errorMessages.ErrorCreateTimeIsNull());
                return null;
            }
            // Create new entry
            var newEntry = new Entry
            {
                Id = id,
                UserId = user.GetId(),
                Url = url,
                Title = title,
                ModTime = modTime.Value,
                CreateTime = createTime.Value
            };
            entries[GenerateKey(user.GetId(), url)] = newEntry;
            return newEntry;
        }
        source.Title = title;
        source.ModTime = modTime.Value;
        source.CreateTime = createTime.Value;
        return source;
    }

    private string GenerateKey(string userId, string url)
    {
        return userId + "|" + url;
    }

    public Entry GetEntryByUserIdAndUrl(string userId, string url)
    {
        var key = GenerateKey(userId, url);
        if (entries.TryGetValue(key, out Entry entry))
        {
            return entry;
        }
        return null;
    }
}
```

### Java Code with Unit Tests

```java
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;

public class SqlValidator {

    /**
     * Validates the given SQL statement where its position in the list of statements is the given index.
     * Note: This method does not validate SQL syntax.
     */
    public static boolean validateSqlStatement(List<String> sqlStatements, int index) {
        if (sqlStatements == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (index < 0 || index >= sqlStatements.size()) {
            return false;
        }
        String statement = sqlStatements.get(index);
        // Example validation: Check that statement is not null or empty, not just whitespace.
        if (statement == null || statement.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return false;
        }
        // Add other validation logic as necessary (e.g., length, specific forbidden substrings, etc.)
        return true;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<String> statements = new ArrayList<>();
        statements.add("SELECT * FROM users;");
        statements.add("   ");
        statements.add(null);
        statements.add("INSERT INTO orders VALUES (1, 'Book');");

        // Unit tests
        System.out.println("Statement at index 0 is valid: " + validateSqlStatement(statements, 0)); // true
        System.out.println("Statement at index 1 is valid: " + validateSqlStatement(statements, 1)); // false
        System.out.println("Statement at index 2 is valid: " + validateSqlStatement(statements, 2)); // false
        System.out.println("Statement at index 3 is valid: " + validateSqlStatement(statements, 3)); // true
        System.out.println("Statement at index 10 is valid: " + validateSqlStatement(statements, 10)); // false
        System.out.println("Statement at index 0 with null list is valid: " + validateSqlStatement(null, 0)); // false
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;

namespace SqlValidatorApp
{
    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            Validator validator = new Validator();

            // Unit tests
            Console.WriteLine("Statement at index 0 is valid: " + validator.ValidateSqlStatement("SELECT * FROM Users", 1)); // true
            Console.WriteLine("Statement at index 1 is valid: " + validator.ValidateSqlStatement("   ", 2)); // false
        }
    }

    class Validator
    {
        private const string _STR = "Statement index: ";

        // validates the given sql statement where its position in the list of statement is the given index . this method does not validate sql syntax .
        private bool ValidateSqlStatement(string sql, int jdbcStatementIndex)
        {
            Debug.Assert(!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sql), _STR + jdbcStatementIndex + _STR);
            return true;
        }

        // For demonstration, make a public wrapper method
        public bool ValidateSqlStatement(string sql, int jdbcStatementIndex)
        {
            try
            {
                ValidateSqlStatement(sql, jdbcStatementIndex);
                return true;
            }
            catch
            {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
}

import java.util.Arrays;

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Example collection
        List<String> list = Arrays.asList("A", "B", "C");

        // Converts to object array
        Object[] arr = list.toArray();

        // Print the array elements
        for (Object obj : arr) {
            System.out.println(obj);
        }

        // Unit tests
        testConvertToArray();
    }

    public static void testConvertToArray() {
        List<String> testList = Arrays.asList("X", "Y");
        Object[] result = testList.toArray();

        if (result.length != 2 || !result[0].equals("X") || !result[1].equals("Y")) {
            throw new AssertionError("Test failed: Expected [X, Y], but got " + Arrays.toString(result));
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test passed: convertToArray");
        }
    }

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;

class Program
{
    public static long?[] ValuesOf(long[] array)
    {
        long?[] dest = new long?[array.Length];
        for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
        {
            dest[i] = array[i];
        }
        return dest;
    }

    static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        long[] input = { 1, 2, 3, 4, 5 };
        long?[] result = ValuesOf(input);

        Console.WriteLine("Converted array:");
        foreach (var val in result)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(val.HasValue ? val.Value.ToString() : "null");
        }

        // Unit tests
        TestValuesOf();
    }

    public static void TestValuesOf()
    {
        long[] testArray = { 10, 20 };
        long?[] expectedResult = new long?[] { 10L, 20L };

        long?[] result = ValuesOf(testArray);

        if (result.Length != expectedResult.Length || !result[0].Equals(expectedResult[0]) || !result[1].Equals(expectedResult[1])) {
            throw new AssertionError("Test failed: Expected [10L, 20L], but got " + string.Join(", ", result));
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test passed: ValuesOf");
        }
    }

    public class AssertionError : Exception
    {
        public AssertionError(string message) : base(message) { }
    }
}

```csharp
using System;

public enum LogLevel
{
    Trace,
    Debug,
    Info,
    Warn,
    Error,
    Fatal
}

public class Logger
{
    private LogLevel minLevel;

    public Logger(LogLevel level)
    {
        minLevel = level;
    }

    public bool IsEnabled(LogLevel level)
    {
        return level >= minLevel;
    }
}

public abstract class BaseLogger
{
    protected Logger logger;

    public BaseLogger(Logger logger)
    {
        this.logger = logger;
    }

    public abstract bool IsTraceEnabled();
}

public class MyLogger : BaseLogger
{
    public MyLogger(Logger logger) : base(logger) { }

    // is this logger instance enabled for the finest level ? 
    public override bool IsTraceEnabled() { return logger.IsEnabled(LogLevel.Debug); }
}

class Program
{
    static void Main()
    {
        Logger logger = new Logger(LogLevel.Trace); // finest level enabled
        MyLogger myLogger = new MyLogger(logger);

        Console.WriteLine("IsTraceEnabled: " + myLogger.IsTraceEnabled()); // Expected: true
        
        // Unit test 1: Test if the logger is enabled for FINEST level
        Console.WriteLine("IsEnabled(LogLevel.Debug): " + myLogger.IsEnabled(LogLevel.Debug)); // Expected: false

        // Unit test 2: Test if the logger is not enabled for INFO level
        Console.WriteLine("IsEnabled(LogLevel.Info): " + myLogger.IsEnabled(LogLevel.Info)); // Expected: false
        
        // Unit test 3: Test if the logger is enabled for CONFIG level
        Console.WriteLine("IsEnabled(LogLevel.Config): " + myLogger.IsEnabled(LogLevel.Config)); // Expected: false
        
        // Unit test 4: Test if the logger is not enabled for SEVERE level
        Console.WriteLine("IsEnabled(LogLevel.Error): " + myLogger.IsEnabled(LogLevel.Error)); // Expected: false

        // Unit test 5: Test if the logger is enabled for FINE level
        Console.WriteLine("IsEnabled(LogLevel.Warn): " + myLogger.IsEnabled(LogLevel.Warn)); // Expected: true
    }
}

```java
public class MissileGame {

    static class Player {
        private int missiles;

        public Player(int missiles) {
            this.missiles = missiles;
        }

        public int getMissiles() {
            return missiles;
        }

        public void useMissile() {
            if (missiles > 0) {
                missiles--;
            } else {
                System.out.println("No missiles left to use.");
            }
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Player attackingPlayer = new Player(3);

        // Simulate removing an used up missile from attackingPlayer
        System.out.println("Missiles before attack: " + attackingPlayer.getMissiles());
        attackingPlayer.useMissile(); // remove a missile
        System.out.println("Missiles after attack: " + attackingPlayer.getMissiles());

        // Test cases
        assert attackingPlayer.getMissiles() == 2 : "Missiles should be 2";
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;

namespace MissileExample
{
    public class StackableItem
    {
        public int Count { get; private set; }

        public StackableItem(int count)
        {
            Count = count;
        }

        public void RemoveOne()
        {
            if (Count > 0)
            {
                Count--;
                Console.WriteLine("One missile removed! Missiles left: " + Count);
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("No more missiles left.");
            }
        }
    }

    public class Weapon
    {
        public string Name { get; set; }

        public Weapon(string name)
        {
            Name = name;
        }
    }

    public class Player
    {
        private Weapon _rangeWeapon;
        private StackableItem _ammunition;
        private StackableItem _missile;

        public Player(Weapon rangeWeapon, StackableItem ammunition, StackableItem missile)
        {
            _rangeWeapon = rangeWeapon;
            _ammunition = ammunition;
            _missile = missile;
        }

        public Weapon GetRangeWeapon()
        {
            return _rangeWeapon;
        }

        public StackableItem GetAmmunition()
        {
            return _ammunition;
        }

        public StackableItem GetMissileIfNotHoldingOtherWeapon()
        {
            if (_rangeWeapon == null)
                return _missile;
            return null;
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        private static void UseMissile(Player player)
        {
            StackableItem projectilesItem = null;
            if (player.GetRangeWeapon() != null)
            {
                projectilesItem = player.GetAmmunition();
            }
            if (projectilesItem == null)
            {
                projectilesItem = player.GetMissileIfNotHoldingOtherWeapon();
            }
            if (projectilesItem != null)
            {
                projectilesItem.RemoveOne();
            }
        }

        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            // Player with a bow and 5 arrows
            var bow = new Weapon("Bow");
            var arrows = new StackableItem(5);
            var missile = new StackableItem(2); // backup missile
            var player = new Player(bow, arrows, missile);

            // Use missile (should remove arrow)
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            // Now out of arrows, should try and remove missile now
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player);
            UseMissile(player); // All missiles used

            // Test cases
            Console.WriteLine(missile.Count == 0 ? "Missiles are 0" : "Missiles are not 0");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
public class MaxLevelLessThanMax {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit test 1
        TreeNode root = new TreeNode(1);
        root.children.add(new TreeNode(2));
        root.children.add(new TreeNode(3));
        int maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result1 = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
        System.out.println("Test 1 - Result: " + result1 + ", Expected: 0");

        // Unit test 2
        root.children.get(0).children.add(new TreeNode(4));
        root.children.get(0).children.add(new TreeNode(5));
        int result2 = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
        System.out.println("Test 2 - Result: " + result2 + ", Expected: 1");

        // Unit test 3
        root.children.get(1).children.add(new TreeNode(6));
        int result3 = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
        System.out.println("Test 3 - Result: " + result3 + ", Expected: 2");

        // Unit test 4
        int result4 = findMaxLevelLessThan(null, maxLevel, 1);
        System.out.println("Test 4 - Result: " + result4 + ", Expected: -1");

        // Unit test 5
        root.children.clear();
        maxLevel = findMaxLevel(root);
        int result5 = findMaxLevelLessThan(root, maxLevel, 1);
        System.out.println("Test 5 - Result: " + result5 + ", Expected: -1");
    }

    public static int findMaxLevel(TreeNode root) {
        return findMaxLevelHelper(root, 1);
    }

    private static int findMaxLevelHelper(TreeNode node, int level) {
        if (node == null) {
            return 0;
        }
        int maxLevel = level;
        for (TreeNode child : node.children) {
            int childMax = findMaxLevelHelper(child, level + 1);
            if (childMax > maxLevel) {
                maxLevel = childMax;
            }
        }
        return maxLevel;
    }

    public static int findMaxLevelLessThan(TreeNode root, int maxLevel, int currentLevel) {
        if (root == null || currentLevel >= maxLevel) {
            return -1;
        }
        int maxLessThan = -1;
        Queue<TreeNode> queue = new LinkedList<>();
        queue.offer(root);
        int level = 1;

        while (!queue.isEmpty()) {
            int size = queue.size();
            for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
                TreeNode node = queue.poll();
                if (level < maxLevel) {
                    maxLessThan = Math.max(maxLessThan, level);
                }
                for (TreeNode child : node.children) {
                    queue.offer(child);
                }
            }
            level++;
        }
        return maxLessThan;
    }
}
```

```csharp
class Program
{
    private const int _NUM = 1;

    private int DetermineMaxLevelRec(int level, Node v)
    {
        int newLevel = level;
        for (Edge e = v.GetFirstOutEdge(); e != null; e = v.GetNextOutEdge(e))
        {
            newLevel = Math.Max(newLevel, DetermineMaxLevelRec(level, e.GetTarget()) + _NUM);
        }
        return newLevel;
    }

    static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        // Unit test 1
        Node root = new Node(new Edge[0]);
        int maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
        Console.WriteLine("Test 1 - Result: " + maxLevel + ", Expected: 0");

        // Unit test 2
        Node child1 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        Node child2 = new Node(new Edge[] { });
        root._edges = new Edge[] { new Edge(child1), new Edge(child2) };
        maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
        Console.WriteLine("Test 2 - Result: " + maxLevel + ", Expected: 1");

        // Unit test 3
        child1._edges = new Edge[] { new Edge(new Node(new Edge[0])), new Edge(new Node(new Edge[0])) };
        maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
        Console.WriteLine("Test 3 - Result: " + maxLevel + ", Expected: 2");

        // Unit test 4
        Node nullNode = null;
        int result4 = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, nullNode);
        Console.WriteLine("Test 4 - Result: " + result4 + ", Expected: 0");

        // Unit test 5
        root._edges = new Edge[0];
        maxLevel = DetermineMaxLevelRec(0, root);
        Console.WriteLine("Test 5 - Result: " + maxLevel + ", Expected: 0");
    }
}
```

```java
public class PasscodeVerifierTest {
    @Test
    public void testPasscodesMatch() throws Exception {
        // Arrange
        String input1 = "test123";
        String input2 = "test123";

        // Act
        System.setIn(new ByteArrayInputStream((input1 + "\n" + input2).getBytes()));
        new PasscodeVerifier().main(null);

        // Assert
        assertEquals("Passcode saved successfully!", System.out.toString().trim());
    }

    @Test
    public void testPasscodesDoNotMatch() throws Exception {
        // Arrange
        String input1 = "test123";
        String input2 = "wrong123";

        // Act
        System.setIn(new ByteArrayInputStream((input1 + "\n" + input2).getBytes()));
        new PasscodeVerifier().main(null);

        // Assert
        assertEquals("Passcodes do not match. Passcode not saved.", System.out.toString().trim());
    }

    @Test
    public void testEmptyInput() throws Exception {
        // Arrange
        String input1 = "";
        String input2 = "";

        // Act
        System.setIn(new ByteArrayInputStream((input1 + "\n" + input2).getBytes()));
        new PasscodeVerifier().main(null);

        // Assert
        assertEquals("Passcodes do not match. Passcode not saved.", System.out.toString().trim());
    }

    @Test
    public void testDifferentCase() throws Exception {
        // Arrange
        String input1 = "test123";
        String input2 = "TEST123";

        // Act
        System.setIn(new ByteArrayInputStream((input1 + "\n" + input2).getBytes()));
        new PasscodeVerifier().main(null);

        // Assert
        assertEquals("Passcodes do not match. Passcode not saved.", System.out.toString().trim());
    }

    @Test
    public void testLeadingAndTrailingWhitespace() throws Exception {
        // Arrange
        String input1 = "  test123  ";
        String input2 = "test123";

        // Act
        System.setIn(new ByteArrayInputStream((input1 + "\n" + input2).getBytes()));
        new PasscodeVerifier().main(null);

        // Assert
        assertEquals("Passcodes do not match. Passcode not saved.", System.out.toString().trim());
    }
}

```java
public static void main(String[] args) {
    ThreadPoolSimulation pool = new ThreadPoolSimulation();
    
    // Test adding and then rolling back a worker
    Worker w1 = pool.new Worker();
    pool.addWorker(w1);
    System.out.println("Worker added. Worker count: " + pool.workerCount.get());
    pool.rollbackWorker(w1);
    System.out.println("Worker rolled back. Worker count: " + pool.workerCount.get());
    
    // Test rolling back a worker that was not present
    Worker w2 = new ThreadPoolSimulation().new Worker();
    pool.rollbackWorker(w2);
    System.out.println("Worker rolled back (not present). Worker count: " + pool.workerCount.get());
    
    // Test adding multiple workers and then rolling them back
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        Worker worker = new ThreadPoolSimulation().new Worker();
        pool.addWorker(worker);
        pool.rollbackWorker(worker);
        System.out.println("Added and rolled back worker. Worker count: " + pool.workerCount.get());
    }
    
    // Test rolling back a worker multiple times
    Worker w3 = new ThreadPoolSimulation().new Worker();
    pool.addWorker(w3);
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
        pool.rollbackWorker(w3);
        System.out.println("Rolled back worker. Worker count: " + pool.workerCount.get());
    }
}
