### Java Code

```java
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Statement;

public class InsertFunctionalityTest {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        try {
            Class.forName("org.h2.Driver");
            Connection conn = DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:h2:mem:testdb;DB_CLOSE_DELAY=-1");
            Statement stmt = conn.createStatement();

            // Create source table and insert some data
            stmt.execute("CREATE TABLE source_table (a INT, b INT)");
            stmt.execute("INSERT INTO source_table VALUES (1, 10), (2, 20), (3, 30)");

            // Create destination table
            stmt.execute("CREATE TABLE dest_table (x INT, y INT)");

            // Insert into dest_table using INSERT...SELECT with expressions
            stmt.execute("INSERT INTO dest_table (x, y) SELECT a+100, b*2 FROM source_table");

            // Query dest_table to check the inserts
            ResultSet rs = stmt.executeQuery("SELECT x, y FROM dest_table ORDER BY x");

            System.out.println("Contents of dest_table after insert:");
            while(rs.next()) {
                int x = rs.getInt("x");
                int y = rs.getInt("y");
                System.out.println("x=" + x + ", y=" + y);
            }

            rs.close();
            stmt.close();
            conn.close();

        } catch (ClassNotFoundException | SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
```

### C# Code

```csharp
using System;
using System.Data;
using System.Data.SQLite;

public class DatabaseCreator
{
    public const string SIMPLE_TABLE1 = "SimpleTable1";
    public const string SIMPLE_TABLE2 = "SimpleTable2";
    public static void FillSimpleTable1(SQLiteConnection conn)
    {
        using (var cmd = conn.CreateCommand())
        {
            cmd.CommandText = $"CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS {SIMPLE_TABLE1} (Id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, Value INTEGER);";
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
            cmd.CommandText = $"INSERT INTO {SIMPLE_TABLE1} (Value) VALUES (42), (52), (62);";
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
    }
}

public static class Assert
{
    public static void AreEqual(int expected, int actual)
    {
        if (expected != actual)
            throw new Exception($"Assert Failed. Expected: {expected}, Actual: {actual}");
    }
}

public class TestClass
{
    const string _STR = "INSERT INTO SimpleTable2(Value) SELECT Value FROM ";
    private SQLiteConnection conn;
    private SQLiteCommand statement;

    public TestClass()
    {
        conn = new SQLiteConnection("Data Source=:memory:;Version=3;New=True;");
        conn.Open();
        using (var cmd = conn.CreateCommand())
        {
            cmd.CommandText = $"CREATE TABLE {DatabaseCreator.SIMPLE_TABLE2} (Id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, Value INTEGER);";
            cmd.ExecuteNonQuery();
        }
        statement = new SQLiteCommand(conn);
    }

    public void TestInsert4()
    {
        DatabaseCreator.FillSimpleTable1(conn);
        statement.CommandText = _STR + DatabaseCreator.SIMPLE_TABLE1;
        statement.ExecuteNonQuery();

        statement.CommandText = $"SELECT COUNT(Value) FROM {DatabaseCreator.SIMPLE_TABLE2}";
        using (var r = statement.ExecuteReader())
        {
            r.Read();
            Assert.AreEqual(3, r.GetInt32(0));
        }
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        statement.Dispose();
        conn.Dispose();
    }
}

class Program
{
    static void Main()
    {
        var test = new TestClass();
        try
        {
            test.TestInsert4();
            Console.WriteLine("TestInsert4 passed.");
        }
        finally
        {
            test.Dispose();
        }
    }
}
```

### Unit Tests

```java
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.sql.ResultSet;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.sql.Statement;

public class InsertFunctionalityTest {

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        try {
            Class.forName("org.h2.Driver");
            Connection conn = DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:h2:mem:testdb;DB_CLOSE_DELAY=-1");
            Statement stmt = conn.createStatement();

            // Create source table and insert some data
            stmt.execute("CREATE TABLE source_table (a INT, b INT)");
            stmt.execute("INSERT INTO source_table VALUES (42), (52), (62)");

            // Create destination table
            stmt.execute("CREATE TABLE dest_table (x INT, y INT)");

            // Insert into dest_table using INSERT...SELECT with expressions
            stmt.execute("INSERT INTO dest_table (x, y) SELECT a+100, b*2 FROM source_table");

            // Query dest_table to check the inserts
            ResultSet rs = stmt.executeQuery("SELECT COUNT(x), SUM(y) FROM dest_table");

            System.out.println("Contents of dest_table after insert:");
            while(rs.next()) {
                int count = rs.getInt("COUNT(x)");
                int sum = rs.getInt("SUM(y)");
                System.out.println("Count=" + count + ", Sum=" + sum);
            }

            Assert.AreEqual(3, count);
            Assert.AreEqual(420, sum);

        } catch (ClassNotFoundException | SQLException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
```

### Java Code

```java
import java.io.*;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;

public class LocalIdMap {
    private static final String FILE_NAME = "idmap.dat";
    private Map<String, String> idMap;

    public LocalIdMap() {
        idMap = loadMap();
    }

    // writes one entry to the local id map on disk
    public void putEntry(String id, String entry) {
        idMap.put(id, entry);
        saveMap();
    }

    public String getEntry(String id) {
        return idMap.get(id);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private Map<String, String> loadMap() {
        File file = new File(FILE_NAME);
        if (!file.exists()) {
            return new HashMap<>();
        }
        try (ObjectInputStream ois = new ObjectInputStream(new FileInputStream(file))) {
            return (Map<String, String>) ois.readObject();
        } catch (IOException | ClassNotFoundException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            return new HashMap<>();
        }
    }

    private void saveMap() {
        try (ObjectOutputStream oos = new ObjectOutputStream(new FileOutputStream(FILE_NAME))) {
            oos.writeObject(idMap);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        LocalIdMap idMap = new LocalIdMap();
        // Example of putting an entry into the local file
        idMap.putEntry("12345", "Sample Entry");
        System.out.println("Entry for 12345: " + idMap.getEntry("12345"));
    }
}
```

```java
import java.io.File;

public class LocalIdMapTest {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        File file = new File("idmap.dat");
        if (file.exists()) {
            file.delete();
        }

        LocalIdMap idMap = new LocalIdMap();

        // Test 1: Put and get an entry
        idMap.putEntry("12345", "Sample Entry");
        String retrievedEntry = idMap.getEntry("12345");
        System.out.println("Test 1 - Passed: " + (retrievedEntry != null && retrievedEntry.equals("Sample Entry")));

        // Test 2: Get non-existent entry
        String nonExistentEntry = idMap.getEntry("98765");
        System.out.println("Test 2 - Passed: " + (nonExistentEntry == null));

        // Test 3: Put an empty ID and get it
        try {
            idMap.putEntry("", "Empty Entry");
            System.out.println("Test 3 - Failed: Should have thrown exception");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 3 - Passed: Threw expected exception");
        }

        // Test 4: Put a null ID and get it
        try {
            idMap.putEntry(null, "Null Entry");
            System.out.println("Test 4 - Failed: Should have thrown exception");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 4 - Passed: Threw expected exception");
        }

        // Test 5: Put an entry and verify it is saved
        idMap.putEntry("12346", "New Sample Entry");
        try (ObjectInputStream ois = new ObjectInputStream(new FileInputStream(file))) {
            Map<String, String> loadedMap = (Map<String, String>) ois.readObject();
            System.out.println("Test 5 - Passed: " + loadedMap.containsKey("12346") && loadedMap.get("12346").equals("New Sample Entry"));
        } catch (IOException | ClassNotFoundException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
            System.out.println("Test 5 - Failed: Unable to load map");
        }
    }
```

### C# Code

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;
using Newtonsoft.Json;
using Newtonsoft.Json.Linq;

public class MapEntry
{
    public int RetainCount { get; set; }
    public string ObjectId { get; set; }
}

public class DiskPath
{
    public string Path { get; private set; }

    public DiskPath(string path)
    {
        Path = path;
    }

    public bool Exists()
    {
        return Directory.Exists(Path);
    }

    public void Mkdirs()
    {
        Directory.CreateDirectory(Path);
    }
}

public static class ParseFileUtils
{
    public static void WriteJSONObjectToFile(FileInfo file, JObject json)
    {
        using (var writer = new StreamWriter(file.FullName))
        {
            writer.Write(json.ToString());
        }
    }
}

public class EntryMapHandler
{
    private const string _STR = "_STR";
    private readonly DiskPath diskPath;
    private readonly string _STR_VALUE = "_STR";

    public EntryMapHandler(string diskPath)
    {
        this.diskPath = new DiskPath(diskPath);
    }

    private bool IsLocalId(string localId)
    {
        // Placeholder for actual validation logic
        return !string.IsNullOrEmpty(localId);
    }

    public void PutMapEntry(string localId, MapEntry entry)
    {
        if (!IsLocalId(localId))
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(_STR + localId + _STR);
        }

        var json = new JObject();
        try
        {
            json.Put(_STR, entry.RetainCount);
            if (entry.ObjectId != null)
            {
                json.Put(_STR, entry.ObjectId);
            }
        }
        catch (JsonException je)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(_STR, je);
        }

        var file = new FileInfo(Path.Combine(diskPath.Path, localId));
        if (!diskPath.Exists())
        {
            diskPath.Mkdirs();
        }
        try
        {
            ParseFileUtils.WriteJSONObjectToFile(file, json);
        }
        catch (IOException)
        {
            // Handle the IOException as needed
        }
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        var handler = new EntryMapHandler("path/to/disk");
        var entry = new MapEntry { RetainCount = 5, ObjectId = "obj123" };
        handler.PutMapEntry("localId1", entry);
    }
}
```

```java
import org.xml.sax.Attributes;
import org.xml.sax.SAXException;
import org.xml.sax.helpers.DefaultHandler;

public class EndElementNotification {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        MyHandler handler = new MyHandler();
        try {
            handler.endElement("namespaceURI", "localName", "elementName");
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
        
        // Unit Tests
        MyHandler testHandler1 = new MyHandler();
        try {
            testHandler1.endElement("", "", "testElement");
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 1 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        MyHandler testHandler2 = new MyHandler();
        try {
            testHandler2.endElement(null, null, "nullElement");
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 2 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        MyHandler testHandler3 = new MyHandler();
        try {
            testHandler3.endElement("namespaceURI", "localName", null);
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 3 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        MyHandler testHandler4 = new MyHandler();
        try {
            testHandler4.endElement("", "", "");
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 4 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        MyHandler testHandler5 = new MyHandler();
        try {
            testHandler5.endElement(null, null, null);
        } catch (SAXException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 5 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    static class MyHandler extends DefaultHandler {
        @Override
        public void endElement(String uri, String localName, String qName) throws SAXException {
            System.out.println("End of element: " + qName);
        }
    }
}
```

**C# Code with Unit Tests**
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace XmlEndElementDemo
{
    public class Base { }

    public class SAXException : Exception
    {
        public SAXException(Exception e) : base("SAXException", e) { }
    }

    public class Attributes { }

    public class PrefixMap { }

    public class ElemContext { }

    public class Tracer { }

    public class EndElementMock : Base
    {
        private const int _NUM = 2;
        private const string _STR = "/>";

        private bool m_inEntityRef = false;
        private PrefixMap m_prefixMap = new PrefixMap();
        private ElemContext m_elemContext = new ElemContext();
        private Tracer m_tracer = new Tracer();
        private Attributes m_attributes = new Attributes();
        private bool m_spaceBeforeClose = false;
        private bool m_cdataTagOpen = false;
        private bool m_doIndent = true;

        public void EndElement(string namespaceURI, string localName, string name)
        {
            if (m_inEntityRef) return;
            m_prefixMap.PopNamespaces(m_elemContext.m_currentElemDepth, null);
            try
            {
                var writer = Console.Out;
                if (m_elemContext.m_startTagOpen)
                {
                    int nAttrs = m_attributes.Length;
                    if (nAttrs > _NUM)
                    {
                        ProcessAttributes(writer, nAttrs);
                        m_attributes.Clear();
                    }
                    if (m_spaceBeforeClose)
                        writer.Write(_STR);
                    else
                        writer.Write(_STR);
                }
                else
                {
                    if (m_cdataTagOpen)
                        CloseCDATA();
                    if (ShouldIndent())
                        Indent(m_elemContext.m_currentElemDepth - _NUM);
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    writer.Write(name);
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                }
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                throw new SAXException(e);
            }

        }

        private void ProcessAttributes(TextWriter writer, int nAttrs) { }
        private void CloseCDATA() { }
        private bool ShouldIndent() { return true; }
        private void Indent(int depth) { }
    }

    // Unit Tests
    public class Program
    {
        public static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            EndElementMock mock = new EndElementMock();
            
            // Test with start tag open and more than _NUM attributes
            mock.m_elemContext.m_startTagOpen = true;
            mock.m_attributes.Add("attr1");
            mock.m_attributes.Add("attr2");
            mock.m_attributes.Add("attr3");
            try {
                mock.EndElement("", "", "testElement");
            } catch (SAXException e) {
                Console.WriteLine("Test 1 Failed: " + e.Message);
            }

            // Reset and test with start tag closed (not open)
            mock = new EndElementMock();
            mock.m_elemContext.m_startTagOpen = false;
            mock.m_cdataTagOpen = true;
            try {
                mock.EndElement("uri2", "local2", "elem2");
            } catch (SAXException e) {
                Console.WriteLine("Test 2 Failed: " + e.Message);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.logging.Level;
import java.util.logging.Logger;

public class Main {
    private static final Logger logger = Logger.getLogger(Main.class.getName());

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // send a fatal error log message
        logger.log(Level.SEVERE, "A fatal error occurred!");

        // Unit tests
        testLogFatalError();
    }

    private static void testLogFatalError() {
        // Setup
        Logger mockLogger = new MockLogger();

        // Test
        Main.logger = mockLogger;
        Main.main(new String[]{});
        
        // Check
        if (mockLogger.logMessages.contains("A fatal error occurred!")) {
            System.out.println("Test passed: Log message 'A fatal error occurred!' was logged.");
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test failed: Log message 'A fatal error occurred!' was not logged.");
        }
    }
}

class MockLogger extends Logger {
    List<String> logMessages = new ArrayList<>();

    public MockLogger() {
        super(Main.class.getName(), null);
    }

    @Override
    public void log(Level level, String msg) {
        if (level == Level.SEVERE) {
            logMessages.add(msg);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public boolean isLoggable(Level level) {
        return true;
    }
}
```

### C# Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Log
{
    public static void Wtf(string tag, string msg)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"WTF: [{tag}] {msg}");
    }
}

public static class Program
{
    private const int LEVEL_FATAL = 5;
    private static int sLevel = 5;
    private static bool allowWtf = true;
    private const int _NUM = 0;

    public static void F(string msg, params object[] args)
    {
        if (!allowWtf) return;
        var caller = GetCallerStackTraceElement();
        string tag = GenerateTag(caller);
        if (sLevel > LEVEL_FATAL) return;
        if (args.Length > _NUM)
        {
            msg = string.Format(msg, args);
        }
        Log.Wtf(tag, msg);
    }

    private static string GetCallerStackTraceElement()
    {
        // For simplicity in this example, just return a dummy string
        return "Program.Main";
    }

    private static string GenerateTag(string caller)
    {
        // For simplicity in this example, just return caller name as the tag
        return caller;
    }

    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        F("A fatal error occurred: {0}", "NullReferenceException");
        F("Simple fatal error message with no args.");

        // Unit tests
        TestLogFatalError();
    }

    private static void TestLogFatalError()
    {
        // Setup
        List<string> logMessages = new List<string>();

        // Mock Log.Wtf
        Action<string, string> mockWtf = (tag, msg) => logMessages.Add($"[{tag}] {msg}");

        // Redirect Log.Wtf to mock
        Type type = typeof(Log);
        FieldInfo field = type.GetField("wtf", BindingFlags.NonPublic | BindingFlags.Static);
        MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("Wtf", BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.Static);
        Delegate originalWtf = (Delegate)method.CreateDelegate(typeof(Action<string, string>), Log.Wtf);

        Action<string, string> backup = (tag, msg) => mockWtf(tag, msg);
        field.SetValue(null, backup);

        // Test
        F("A fatal error occurred: {0}", "NullReferenceException");
        F("Simple fatal error message with no args.");

        // Check
        if (logMessages.Contains("[Program.Main] A fatal error occurred: NullReferenceException") &&
            logMessages.Contains("[Program.Main] Simple fatal error message with no args."))
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Test passed: Log messages were logged correctly.");
        }
        else
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Test failed: Log messages were not logged correctly.");
        }

        // Restore original Wtf method
        field.SetValue(null, originalWtf);
    }
}
```

### Java Code with Unit Tests

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class Main {
    // Removes the given element from the list
    public static <T> void removeFromList(List<T> list, T element) {
        list.remove(element);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<String> leaves = new ArrayList<>();
        leaves.add("leaf1");
        leaves.add("leaf2");
        leaves.add("leaf3");

        System.out.println("Before removal: " + leaves);
        removeFromList(leaves, "leaf2");
        System.out.println("After removal: " + leaves);

        // Unit Tests
        List<String> testLeaves = new ArrayList<>();
        testLeaves.add("test1");
        testLeaves.add("test2");
        removeFromList(testLeaves, "test1");
        assert testLeaves.size() == 1 && !testLeaves.contains("test1");

        testLeaves.clear();
        testLeaves.add("test3");
        testLeaves.add("test4");
        removeFromList(testLeaves, "test5");
        assert testLeaves.size() == 2 && testLeaves.contains("test3") && testLeaves.contains("test4");

        testLeaves.clear();
        removeFromList(testLeaves, "test6");
        assert testLeaves.isEmpty();

        List<Integer> intList = new ArrayList<>();
        intList.add(1);
        intList.add(2);
        intList.add(3);
        removeFromList(intList, 2);
        assert intList.size() == 2 && !intList.contains(2);

        List<String> duplicateLeaves = new ArrayList<>();
        duplicateLeaves.add("duplicate");
        duplicateLeaves.add("duplicate");
        removeFromList(duplicateLeaves, "duplicate");
        assert duplicateLeaves.isEmpty();
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace CatalogExample
{
    public class CatalogTreeLeaf
    {
        private string _name;

        public CatalogTreeLeaf(string name)
        {
            _name = name;
        }

        public string GetName()
        {
            return _name;
        }
    }

    public class Catalog
    {
        private List<CatalogTreeLeaf> _leaves = new List<CatalogTreeLeaf>();
        private int _NUM = 0; // You can set _NUM as needed

        public void AddLeaf(CatalogTreeLeaf leaf)
        {
            _leaves.Add(leaf);
        }

        // leaves can be used for many - to - many relations
        public void DeleteLeaves(string name)
        {
            for (int i = _NUM; i < _leaves.Count; i++)
            {
                CatalogTreeLeaf leaf = _leaves[i];
                if (name.Equals(leaf.GetName()))
                {
                    _leaves.RemoveAt(i);
                    i--; // Adjust index after removal
                }
            }
        }

        public void PrintLeaves()
        {
            foreach (var leaf in _leaves)
            {
                Console.WriteLine(leaf.GetName());
            }
        }
    }

    public class Program
    {
        public static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            Catalog catalog = new Catalog();
            catalog.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Apple"));
            catalog.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Banana"));
            catalog.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Apple"));
            catalog.AddLeaf(new CatalogTreeLeaf("Cherry"));

            Console.WriteLine("Before deletion:");
            catalog.PrintLeaves();

            catalog.DeleteLeaves("Apple");

            Console.WriteLine("\nAfter deletion of 'Apple':");
            catalog.PrintLeaves();

            // Unit Tests
            List<CatalogTreeLeaf> testLeaves = new List<CatalogTreeLeaf>();
            testLeaves.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("test1"));
            testLeaves.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("test2"));
            DeleteLeaves(testLeaves, "test1");
            Assert.AreEqual(1, testLeaves.Count);
            Assert.IsFalse(testLeaves.Exists(leaf => leaf.GetName() == "test1"));

            testLeaves.Clear();
            testLeaves.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("test3"));
            testLeaves.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("test4"));
            DeleteLeaves(testLeaves, "test5");
            Assert.AreEqual(2, testLeaves.Count);
            Assert.IsTrue(testLeaves.Exists(leaf => leaf.GetName() == "test3"));
            Assert.IsTrue(testLeaves.Exists(leaf => leaf.GetName() == "test4"));

            testLeaves.Clear();
            DeleteLeaves(testLeaves, "test6");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, testLeaves.Count);

            List<CatalogTreeLeaf> intList = new List<CatalogTreeLeaf>();
            intList.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("1"));
            intList.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("2"));
            intList.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("3"));
            DeleteLeaves(intList, "2");
            Assert.AreEqual(2, testLeaves.Count);
            Assert.IsFalse(testLeaves.Exists(leaf => leaf.GetName() == "2"));

            List<CatalogTreeLeaf> duplicateLeaves = new List<CatalogTreeLeaf>();
            duplicateLeaves.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("duplicate"));
            duplicateLeaves.Add(new CatalogTreeLeaf("duplicate"));
            DeleteLeaves(duplicateLeaves, "duplicate");
            Assert.AreEqual(0, duplicateLeaves.Count);
        }

        private static void DeleteLeaves(List<CatalogTreeLeaf> leaves, string name)
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < leaves.Count; i++)
            {
                if (name.Equals(leaves[i].GetName()))
                {
                    leaves.RemoveAt(i);
                    i--; // Adjust index after removal
                }
            }
        }

        private static void Assert(bool condition)
        {
            if (!condition)
            {
                throw new Exception("Assertion failed");
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Java Code with Unit Tests

```java
import java.util.BitSet;

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        BitSet vector = new BitSet();
        vector.set(0);
        vector.set(2);
        vector.set(4);
        System.out.println(bitSetToString(vector, 5)); // Expected Output: 10100
    }

    /**
     * Returns a string representation of this vector.
     * Note that this string representation shows the bit of index 0 at the leftmost position.
     */
    public static String bitSetToString(BitSet bitSet, int length) {
        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
        for (int i = 0; i < length; i++) {
            sb.append(bitSet.get(i) ? "1" : "0");
        }
        return sb.toString();
    }

    // Unit Tests
    public static void runTests() {
        assert bitSetToString(new BitSet(), 5).equals("00000") : "Test 1 Failed";
        BitSet testVector1 = new BitSet();
        testVector1.set(2);
        testVector1.set(3);
        assert bitSetToString(testVector1, 4).equals("0110") : "Test 2 Failed";
        BitSet testVector2 = new BitSet();
        testVector2.set(0);
        testVector2.set(5);
        testVector2.set(9);
        assert bitSetToString(testVector2, 10).equals("100000100") : "Test 3 Failed";
        BitSet testVector3 = new BitSet();
        testVector3.set(4);
        testVector3.set(7);
        assert bitSetToString(testVector3, 8).equals("00001001") : "Test 4 Failed";

        System.out.println("All tests passed.");
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        runTests();
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Text;

public class MyVector
{
    private int[] _data;
    private const int _NUM = 0;

    public MyVector(int[] data)
    {
        _data = data;
    }

    public int Size()
    {
        return _data.Length;
    }

    // Mock GetInt method, assuming each element is either 0 or 1 for bit representation
    public int GetInt(int index)
    {
        if (index >= 0 && index < _data.Length)
            return _data[index];
        return 0;
    }

    // returns a string representation of this vector
    // note: this string representation shows the bit of index 0 at the leftmost position.
    public override string ToString()
    {
        var s = new StringBuilder();
        int size = Size();
        for (int i = _NUM; i < size; i++)
        {
            s.Append(GetInt(i));
        }
        return s.ToString();
    }

    // Unit Tests
    public static void runTests() {
        MyVector testVector1 = new MyVector(new int[]{0, 0, 0, 0, 0});
        assert testVector1.ToString().Equals("00000") : "Test 1 Failed";

        MyVector testVector2 = new MyVector(new int[]{0, 1, 1, 0});
        assert testVector2.ToString().Equals("0110") : "Test 2 Failed";

        MyVector testVector3 = new MyVector(new int[]{1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1, 0, 0});
        assert testVector3.ToString().Equals("100000100") : "Test 3 Failed";

        MyVector testVector4 = new MyVector(new int[]{0, 0, 0, 1, 0});
        assert testVector4.ToString().Equals("00010") : "Test 4 Failed";

        Console.WriteLine("All tests passed.");
    }

    public static void Main()
    {
        runTests();
    }
}
```

Java Code with Unit Tests:
```java
public class UriValidator {

    /**
     * Checks whether the specified character is valid within a URI component.
     * The validation should be performed after escape encoding, or the component doesn't include escaped characters.
     * This validation is generous, not strict.
     *
     * @param ch         the character to check
     * @param component  the name of the component (not used in logic, but to match original intent)
     * @return           true if the character is valid, false otherwise
     */
    public static boolean isValidUriChar(char ch, String component) {
        // Allow unreserved characters: ALPHA / DIGIT / "-" / "." / "_" / "~"
        if ((ch >= 'A' && ch <= 'Z')
                || (ch >= 'a' && ch <= 'z')
                || (ch >= '0' && ch <= '9')
                || ch == '-'
                || ch == '.'
                || ch == '_'
                || ch == '~') {
            return true;
        }
        // Allow sub-delims: "!" / "$" / "&" / "'" / "(" / ")"
        //                    "*" / "+" / "," / ";" / "="
        if ("!$&'()*+,;=".indexOf(ch) >= 0) {
            return true;
        }
        // Allow ':' and '@'
        if (ch == ':' || ch == '@') {
            return true;
        }
        // Allow percent (escaped encoding "%")
        if (ch == '%') {
            return true;
        }
        // Generous: allow '/' and '?', depending on component
        if (component != null) {
            if (component.equalsIgnoreCase("path") && ch == '/') {
                return true;
            }
            if (component.equalsIgnoreCase("query") && ch == '?') {
                return true;
            }
            if (component.equalsIgnoreCase("fragment") && (ch == '/' || ch == '?')) {
                return true;
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Test examples
        char[] testChars = { 'A', 'z', '0', '-', '.', '_', '~', '!', '$', '&', '\'', '(', ')', '*', '+', ',', ';', '=', ':', '@', '%', '/', '?', ' ' };
        String[] components = { "path", "query", "fragment", "unknown" };

        for (String component : components) {
            System.out.println("Component: " + component);
            for (char ch : testChars) {
                boolean valid = isValidUriChar(ch, component);
                System.out.printf("  '%c' (%d): %s\n", ch, (int) ch, valid ? "valid" : "invalid");
            }
            System.out.println();
        }

        // Unit Tests
        System.out.println("\nUnit Tests for Java Code:");
        assert isValidUriChar('A', "path") == true;
        assert isValidUriChar('/', "query") == true;
        assert isValidUriChar('!', "unknown") == true;
        assert isValidUriChar(' ', "fragment") == false;
        assert isValidUriChar('%', "path") == true;
    }
}
```
C# Code with Unit Tests:
```csharp
using System;

namespace UriValidationExample
{
    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            // Example usage of UriValidator
            char[] component = { 'A', 'z', '0', '-', '.', '_', '~', '!', '$', '&', '\'', '(', ')', '*', '+', ',', ';', '=', ':', '@', '%', '/', '?', ' ' };
            int soffset = 0;
            int eoffset = -1;
            BitArray generous = new BitArray(128);

            // Set allowed chars (allow uppercase and lowercase alphabets)
            for (int i = 0; i < generous.Length; i++)
            {
                generous[i] = (i >= 'A' && i <= 'Z') || (i >= 'a' && i <= 'z');
            }

            bool result1 = new UriValidator().Validate(component, soffset, eoffset, generous);
            Console.WriteLine("Validation Result 1: " + result1);

            // Unit Tests
            Console.WriteLine("\nUnit Tests for C# Code:");
            UriValidator validator = new UriValidator();
            assert(validator.Validate(new char[] { 'A' }, 0, -1, generous) == true);
            assert(validator.Validate(new char[] { '/' }, 0, -1, generous) == false);
            assert(validator.Validate(new char[] { '!' }, 0, -1, generous) == true);
            assert(validator.Validate(new char[] { ' ' }, 0, -1, generous) == false);
            assert(validator.Validate(new char[] { '%' }, 0, -1, generous) == true);
        }
    }

    public class UriValidator
    {
        public const int _NUM = 1;
        public const bool _BOOL = false;

        // validate the uri characters within a specific component . 
        // the component must be performed after escape encoding .
        // or it doesn't include escaped characters .
        // it's not that much strict , generous . 
        // the strict validation might be performed before being called this method .
        protected internal bool Validate(char[] component, int soffset, int eoffset, BitArray generous)
        {
            if (eoffset == -_NUM)
            {
                eoffset = component.Length - _NUM;
            }
            for (int i = soffset; i <= eoffset; i++)
            {
                if (component[i] >= generous.Length || !generous[component[i]])
                {
                    return _BOOL;
                }
            }
            return !_BOOL;
        }
    }

    public static void assert(bool condition)
    {
        if (!condition) throw new Exception("Assertion failed");
    }
}
```

```java
import java.awt.Color;
import java.awt.Graphics2D;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;

public class BuildFinalBitmap {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int width = 400;
        int height = 400;
        BufferedImage finalBitmap = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);

        // Example: Suppose we have four pieces (sub-images)
        BufferedImage[] pieces = new BufferedImage[4];
        int pieceWidth = 200;
        int pieceHeight = 200;
        Color[] colors = { Color.RED, Color.GREEN, Color.BLUE, Color.ORANGE };
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            pieces[i] = new BufferedImage(pieceWidth, pieceHeight, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
            Graphics2D g = pieces[i].createGraphics();
            g.setColor(colors[i]);
            g.fillRect(0, 0, pieceWidth, pieceHeight);
            g.dispose();
        }

        // Build the final bitmap by drawing each piece
        Graphics2D g2d = finalBitmap.createGraphics();
        g2d.drawImage(pieces[0], 0, 0, null); // Top-left
        g2d.drawImage(pieces[1], 200, 0, null); // Top-right
        g2d.drawImage(pieces[2], 0, 200, null); // Bottom-left
        g2d.drawImage(pieces[3], 200, 200, null); // Bottom-right
        g2d.dispose();

        // Save the resulting bitmap to a file (for verification)
        try {
            javax.imageio.ImageIO.write(finalBitmap, "png", new java.io.File("final_bitmap.png"));
            System.out.println("Final bitmap created and saved as final_bitmap.png");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    // Unit Tests
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int width = 400;
        int height = 400;
        BufferedImage finalBitmap = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);

        // Test case 1: Create a single piece and draw it on the bitmap
        BufferedImage[] pieces = new BufferedImage[1];
        int pieceWidth = 200;
        int pieceHeight = 200;
        Color color = Color.RED;
        pieces[0] = new BufferedImage(pieceWidth, pieceHeight, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        Graphics2D g = pieces[0].createGraphics();
        g.setColor(color);
        g.fillRect(0, 0, pieceWidth, pieceHeight);
        g.dispose();

        // Build the final bitmap by drawing each piece
        Graphics2D g2d = finalBitmap.createGraphics();
        g2d.drawImage(pieces[0], 0, 0, null); // Top-left
        g2d.dispose();

        int pixelValue = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)finalBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(0);
        if (pixelValue == color.getRGB()) {
            System.out.println("Test Case 1 Passed");
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test Case 1 Failed");
        }

        // Test case 2: Create multiple pieces and draw them on the bitmap
        int NUM_PIECES = 4;
        BufferedImage[] testPieces = new BufferedImage[NUM_PIECES];
        Color[] testColors = { Color.RED, Color.GREEN, Color.BLUE, Color.ORANGE };
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PIECES; i++) {
            testPieces[i] = new BufferedImage(pieceWidth, pieceHeight, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
            Graphics2D gTest = testPieces[i].createGraphics();
            gTest.setColor(testColors[i]);
            gTest.fillRect(0, 0, pieceWidth, pieceHeight);
            gTest.dispose();
        }

        // Build the final bitmap by drawing each piece
        g2d = finalBitmap.createGraphics();
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PIECES; i++) {
            int x = (i % 2 == 0) ? 0 : pieceWidth;
            int y = (i / 2 == 0) ? 0 : pieceHeight;
            g2d.drawImage(testPieces[i], x, y, null);
        }
        g2d.dispose();

        boolean testPassed = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PIECES; i++) {
            int x = (i % 2 == 0) ? 0 : pieceWidth;
            int y = (i / 2 == 0) ? 0 : pieceHeight;
            int testPixelValue = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)finalBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(y * finalBitmap.getWidth() + x);
            if (testPixelValue != testColors[i].getRGB()) {
                testPassed = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (testPassed) {
            System.out.println("Test Case 2 Passed");
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test Case 2 Failed");
        }

        // Test case 3: Create a bitmap with no pieces and verify it is transparent
        BufferedImage emptyBitmap = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        g2d = finalBitmap.createGraphics();
        for (int x = 0; x < width; x++) {
            for (int y = 0; y < height; y++) {
                int pixelValueEmpty = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)emptyBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(y * emptyBitmap.getWidth() + x);
                g2d.setColor(new Color(pixelValueEmpty));
                g2d.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1);
            }
        }
        g2d.dispose();

        boolean isEmpty = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < height; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < width; j++) {
                int pixelValueEmpty = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)emptyBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(i * emptyBitmap.getWidth() + j);
                if (pixelValueEmpty != 0) {
                    isEmpty = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (isEmpty) {
            System.out.println("Test Case 3 Passed");
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test Case 3 Failed");
        }

        // Test case 4: Create a bitmap with partial transparency and verify it
        BufferedImage transparentBitmap = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        g2d = finalBitmap.createGraphics();
        for (int x = 0; x < width / 2; x++) {
            for (int y = 0; y < height / 2; y++) {
                int pixelValueTransparent = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)transparentBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(y * transparentBitmap.getWidth() + x);
                g2d.setColor(new Color(pixelValueTransparent, true));
                g2d.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1);
            }
        }
        g2d.dispose();

        boolean isTransparent = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < height / 2; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < width / 2; j++) {
                int pixelValueTransparent = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)transparentBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(i * transparentBitmap.getWidth() + j);
                if (pixelValueTransparent != 0) {
                    isTransparent = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (isTransparent) {
            System.out.println("Test Case 4 Passed");
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test Case 4 Failed");
        }

        // Test case 5: Create a bitmap with all pixels set to the same color and verify it
        BufferedImage sameColorBitmap = new BufferedImage(width, height, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_ARGB);
        g2d = finalBitmap.createGraphics();
        Color setColor = Color.YELLOW;
        for (int x = 0; x < width; x++) {
            for (int y = 0; y < height; y++) {
                int pixelValueSameColor = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)sameColorBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(y * sameColorBitmap.getWidth() + x);
                g2d.setColor(new Color(pixelValueSameColor));
                g2d.fillRect(x, y, 1, 1);
            }
        }
        g2d.dispose();

        boolean isSameColor = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < height; i++) {
            for (int j = 0; j < width; j++) {
                int pixelValueSameColor = ((java.awt.image.DataBufferInt)sameColorBitmap.getRaster().getDataBuffer()).get(i * sameColorBitmap.getWidth() + j);
                if (pixelValueSameColor != setColor.getRGB()) {
                    isSameColor = false;
                    break;
                }
            }
        }

        if (isSameColor) {
            System.out.println("Test Case 5 Passed");
        } else {
            System.out.println("Test Case 5 Failed");
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
    public class RectF

        public float Left;

        public float Bottom;
        public float Width() => Right - Left;
        public float Height() => Bottom - Top;

    public class Bitmap
    {

        public int Width { get; set; }

        {
            return rand.Next(a, b+1);
        }
        public static int Dp2Px(int x) => x * 2; // Dummy
        public static Bitmap CreateBitmapSafely(int width, int height, Bitmap.Config config, int count)
        {
            if (width < 0 || height < 0) return null;
            return new Bitmap(width, height);
        }

    public class Point
    {
        public int x, y;
        public Point(int x, int y) { this.x = x; this.y = y; }
    }

    public class Piece
    {
        public int X, Y;
        public Bitmap Bitmap;
        public int Shadow;
        public Piece(int x, int y, Bitmap bmp, int shadow)
        {
            X = x; Y = y; Bitmap = bmp; Shadow = shadow;
        }
    }

    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        // Test Case 1: Create a single piece and draw it on the bitmap
        int width = 400;
        int height = 400;
        Bitmap finalBitmap = new Bitmap(width, height);

        // Create a piece with a specific color
        Color color = System.Drawing.Color.Red;
        Piece[] pieces = { new Piece(0, 0, new Bitmap(color), 0) };

        // Draw the piece on the bitmap
        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(finalBitmap))
        {
            foreach (Piece piece in pieces)
                g2d.DrawImageUnscaled(piece.Bitmap, piece.X, piece.Y);
        }

        int pixelValue = ((System.Drawing.Imaging.BitmapData)finalBitmap.LockBits(new System.Drawing.Rectangle(0, 0, width, height), System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageLockMode.ReadOnly, System.Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat.Format32bppArgb)).Scan0.ToInt32();
        if (pixelValue == color.ToArgb()) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 1 Passed");
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 1 Failed");
        }

        // Test Case 2: Create multiple pieces and draw them on the bitmap
        int NUM_PIECES = 4;
        Piece[] testPieces = new Piece[NUM_PIECES];
        Color[] testColors = { System.Drawing.Color.Red, System.Drawing.Color.Green, System.Drawing.Color.Blue, System.Drawing.Color.Orange };
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PIECES; i++)
            testPieces[i] = new Piece((i % 2 == 0) ? 0 : width / 2, (i / 2 == 0) ? 0 : height / 2, new Bitmap(testColors[i]), 0);

        // Draw the pieces on the bitmap
        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(finalBitmap))
        {
            foreach (Piece piece in testPieces)
                g2d.DrawImageUnscaled(piece.Bitmap, piece.X, piece.Y);
        }

        bool testPassed = true;
        for (int i = 0; i < NUM_PIECES; i++)
        {
            int x = (i % 2 == 0) ? 0 : width / 2;
            int y = (i / 2 == 0) ? 0 : height / 2;
            Color testPixelColor = ((System.Drawing.Imaging.BitmapData)finalBitmap.LockBits(new System.Drawing.Rectangle(x, y, 1, 1), System.Drawing.Imaging.ImageLockMode.ReadOnly, System.Drawing.Imaging.PixelFormat.Format32bppArgb)).Scan0.ToInt32();
            if (testPixelColor != testColors[i])
            {
                testPassed = false;
                break;
            }
        }

        if (testPassed) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 2 Passed");
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 2 Failed");
        }

        // Test Case 3: Create a bitmap with no pieces and verify it is transparent
        Bitmap emptyBitmap = new Bitmap(width, height);

        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(finalBitmap))
        {
            for (int x = 0; x < width; x++)
                for (int y = 0; y < height; y++)
                    g2d.SetPixel(x, y, Color.Transparent);
        }

        bool isEmpty = true;
        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(emptyBitmap))
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < height; i++)
                for (int j = 0; j < width; j++)
                    if (!g2d.GetPixel(j, i).Equals(Color.Transparent))
                    {
                        isEmpty = false;
                        break;
                    }
        }

        if (isEmpty) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 3 Passed");
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 3 Failed");
        }

        // Test Case 4: Create a bitmap with partial transparency and verify it
        Bitmap transparentBitmap = new Bitmap(width, height);

        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(transparentBitmap))
        {
            for (int x = 0; x < width / 2; x++)
                for (int y = 0; y < height / 2; y++)
                    g2d.SetPixel(x, y, Color.FromArgb(128, 255, 255, 0));
        }

        bool isTransparent = true;
        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(transparentBitmap))
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < height / 2; i++)
                for (int j = 0; j < width / 2; j++)
                    if (!g2d.GetPixel(j, i).Equals(Color.FromArgb(128, 255, 255, 0)))
                    {
                        isTransparent = false;
                        break;
                    }
        }

        if (isTransparent) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 4 Passed");
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 4 Failed");
        }

        // Test Case 5: Create a bitmap with all pixels set to the same color and verify it
        Bitmap sameColorBitmap = new Bitmap(width, height);

        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(sameColorBitmap))
        {
            for (int x = 0; x < width; x++)
                for (int y = 0; y < height; y++)
                    g2d.SetPixel(x, y, Color.Yellow);
        }

        bool isSameColor = true;
        using (Graphics g2d = Graphics.FromImage(sameColorBitmap))
        {
            for (int i = 0; i < height; i++)
                for (int j = 0; j < width; j++)
                    if (!g2d.GetPixel(j, i).Equals(Color.Yellow))
                    {
                        isSameColor = false;
                        break;
                    }
        }

        if (isSameColor) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 5 Passed");
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 5 Failed");
        }
    }
}
```

In these test cases, we create a `Bitmap` object and draw different shapes on it using the `Graphics` class. We then verify that the pixels on the bitmap have been set correctly based on our expectations.

To run the tests, simply call the `Main` method in each code snippet. The output will indicate whether each test case passed or failed based on whether the pixels on the bitmap match the expected colors and transparency levels.

```java
import java.lang.Thread;

public class Main {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Thread shutdownHook = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook executed."));
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(shutdownHook);
        boolean removed = Runtime.getRuntime().removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook);
        System.out.println("Shutdown hook removed: " + removed);

        // Unit Tests
        testRemoveShutdownHook();
    }

    public static void testRemoveShutdownHook() {
        Thread shutdownHook1 = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook 1 executed."));
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        boolean removed1 = Runtime.getRuntime().removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook1);
        assert removed1 : "Should have been able to remove the shutdown hook";

        Thread shutdownHook2 = new Thread(() -> System.out.println("Shutdown hook 2 executed."));
        Runtime.getRuntime().addShutdownHook(shutdownHook2);
        boolean removed2 = Runtime.getRuntime().removeShutdownHook(shutdownHook3); // Intentional error to test failure
        assert !removed2 : "Should not have been able to remove an unregistered shutdown hook";
    }
}
```

C# Code with Unit Tests:
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading;

namespace ShutdownHookExample
{
    public class RuntimePermission
    {
        private string name;
        public RuntimePermission(string name)
        {
            this.name = name;
        }
    }

    public class SecurityManager
    {
        public static SecurityManager Current { get; } = new SecurityManager();

        public void CheckPermission(RuntimePermission permission)
        {
        }
    }

    public class ShutdownHookManager
    {
        private List<Thread> hooksList = new List<Thread>();
        private int VMState = 0;
        private const int _NUM = 3;

        public void RegisterShutdownHook(Thread hook)
        {
            lock (hooksList)
            {
                hooksList.Add(hook);
            }
        }

        public bool RemoveShutdownHook(Thread hook)
        {
            SecurityManager sm = SecurityManager.Current;
            if (sm != null)
            {
                sm.CheckPermission(new RuntimePermission("shutdownHooks"));
            }
            if (hook == null)
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(hook));
            if (VMState > _NUM)
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException();
            }
            lock (hooksList)
            {
                return hooksList.Remove(hook);
            }
        }

        public void PrintHooks()
        {
            lock (hooksList)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("Current hooks:");
                foreach (var h in hooksList)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine(h.Name);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            ShutdownHookManager manager = new ShutdownHookManager();
            Thread hook1 = new Thread(() => Console.WriteLine("Hook 1"));
            hook1.Name = "Hook1";
            Thread hook2 = new Thread(() => Console.WriteLine("Hook 2"));
            hook2.Name = "Hook2";

            manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook1);
            manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook2);

            Console.WriteLine("Before removal:");
            manager.PrintHooks();

            bool removed = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook1);
            Console.WriteLine($"Removed hook1: {removed}");

            Console.WriteLine("After removal:");
            manager.PrintHooks();

            // Unit Tests
            testRemoveShutdownHook(manager);
        }

        public static void testRemoveShutdownHook(ShutdownHookManager manager)
        {
            Thread hook3 = new Thread(() => Console.WriteLine("Shutdown hook 3 executed."));
            manager.RegisterShutdownHook(hook3);

            bool removed1 = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook3);
            if (!removed1) throw new Exception("Should have been able to remove the shutdown hook");

            Thread hook4 = new Thread(() => Console.WriteLine("Shutdown hook 4 executed.")); // Intentional error to test failure
            try
            {
                bool removed2 = manager.RemoveShutdownHook(hook4);
            }
            catch (ArgumentException)
            {
                // Expected exception for unregistered shutdown hook
            }
        }
    }
}
```

**Java Code with Unit Tests**

```java
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;
import java.util.concurrent.Callable;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.FutureTask;

public class ReadRequestDemo {

    static class InputReader implements Callable<String> {
        @Override
        public String call() throws Exception {
            BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in));
            return reader.readLine();
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        FutureTask<String> futureRead = null;
        Thread inputThread = null;

        System.out.println("Press enter (in another thread) to continue, or type 'quit' to exit.");
        boolean running = true;
        while (running) {
            futureRead = new FutureTask<>(new InputReader());
            inputThread = new Thread(futureRead);
            inputThread.setDaemon(true);
            inputThread.start();

            try {
                String input = futureRead.get();
                if ("quit".equalsIgnoreCase(input)) {
                    System.out.println("Quit received. Exiting.");
                    running = false;
                } else {
                    System.out.println("You entered: " + input);
                }
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                System.out.println("Main thread interrupted!");
                running = false;
            } catch (ExecutionException e) {
                System.out.println("Input task failed: " + e.getMessage());
                running = false;
            }
        }
        System.out.println("Exited main loop.");
    }

    public static void runTests() throws Exception {
        // Unit test 1
        System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(("hello\n").getBytes()));
        ReadRequestDemo.main(null);
        
        // Unit test 2
        System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(("quit\n").getBytes()));
        ReadRequestDemo.main(null);

        // Unit test 3
        System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(("Hello World!\n").getBytes()));
        ReadRequestDemo.main(null);

        // Unit test 4
        System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(("1234567890\n").getBytes()));
        ReadRequestDemo.main(null);

        // Unit test 5
        System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream(("abcde\n").getBytes()));
        ReadRequestDemo.main(null);
    }
}
```

**C# Code with Unit Tests**

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;
using System.Threading;

public class KeyboardInputCollector
{
    private volatile bool m_KeyboardReadRequest = false;
    private StreamReader m_KeyboardStream;
    private List<string> m_KeyboardInput = new List<string>();
    private const int _NUM = 100; // milliseconds
    private Thread m_Thread;
    private volatile bool m_Running = true;

    public KeyboardInputCollector()
    {
        m_KeyboardStream = new StreamReader(Console.OpenStandardInput());
        m_Thread = new Thread(Run);
        m_Thread.IsBackground = true;
        m_Thread.Start();
    }

    public void RequestKeyboardInput()
    {
        m_KeyboardReadRequest = true;
        lock (this)
        {
            while (m_KeyboardReadRequest)
            {
                Monitor.Wait(this);
            }
        }
    }

    public List<string> GetKeyboardInput()
    {
        lock (m_KeyboardInput)
        {
            return new List<string>(m_KeyboardInput);
        }
    }

    public void Stop()
    {
        m_Running = false;
        m_KeyboardStream = null;
    }

    public void Run()
    {
        while (m_KeyboardStream != null && m_Running)
        {
            try
            {
                if (m_KeyboardReadRequest)
                {
                    string s = m_KeyboardStream.ReadLine();
                    lock (m_KeyboardInput)
                    {
                        m_KeyboardInput.Add(s);
                    }
                    m_KeyboardReadRequest = false;
                    lock (this)
                    {
                        Monitor.PulseAll(this);
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    Thread.Sleep(_NUM);
                }
            }
            catch (IOException)
            {
                // Handle IOException
            }
        }
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        KeyboardInputCollector collector = new KeyboardInputCollector();
        Console.WriteLine("Press Enter to send an input request, or type 'quit' to exit.");

        while (true)
        {
            Console.Write("Type 'request' to get input: ");
            string cmd = Console.ReadLine();
            if (cmd == "quit")
            {
                break;
            }
            if (cmd == "request")
            {
                collector.RequestKeyboardInput();
                var inputs = collector.GetKeyboardInput();
                Console.WriteLine("Keyboard Inputs Collected:");
                foreach (var input in inputs)
                {
                    Console.WriteLine(input);
                }
            }
        }

        collector.Stop();
        Console.WriteLine("Exiting.");
    }

    public static void runTests()
    {
        // Unit test 1
        Console.SetIn(new StringReader("hello\n"));
        Program.Main(null);

        // Unit test 2
        Console.SetIn(new StringReader("quit\n"));
        Program.Main(null);

        // Unit test 3
        Console.SetIn(new StringReader("Hello World!\n"));
        Program.Main(null);

        // Unit test 4
        Console.SetIn(new StringReader("1234567890\n"));
        Program.Main(null);

        // Unit test 5
        Console.SetIn(new StringReader("abcde\n"));
        Program.Main(null);
    }
}
```

```java
public class ScrollBounderTest {

        ScrollBounder scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(150, 0, 100);
        System.out.println("Before bounding: " + scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        System.out.println("After bounding: " + scrollBounder.getScrollPosition());

        // Test case 1
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(120, 0, 100);
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assert scrollBounder.getScrollPosition() == 100 : "Test case 1 failed";

        // Test case 2
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(-50, 0, 100);
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assert scrollBounder.getScrollPosition() == 0 : "Test case 2 failed";

        // Test case 3
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(50, 0, 100);
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assert scrollBounder.getScrollPosition() == 50 : "Test case 3 failed";

        // Test case 4
        scrollBounder = new ScrollBounder(-20, 0, 100);
        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
        assert scrollBounder.getScrollPosition() == 0 : "Test case 4 failed";

        // Test case 5

        scrollBounder.boundScroll();
```

```csharp
using System;

public class ScrollManagerTest
{
    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        ScrollManager sm = new ScrollManager();
        sm.SetStackScroll(120.0f);
        Console.WriteLine($"Before bounding: {sm.GetStackScroll()}");

        bool changed = sm.BoundScroll();

        Console.WriteLine($"After bounding: {sm.GetStackScroll()}");
        Console.WriteLine($"Scroll position changed? {changed}");

        sm.SetStackScroll(-10.0f);
        Console.WriteLine($"\nBefore bounding: {sm.GetStackScroll()}");

        changed = sm.BoundScroll();

        Console.WriteLine($"After bounding: {sm.GetStackScroll()}");
        Console.WriteLine($"Scroll position changed? {changed}");

        sm.SetStackScroll(50.0f);
        Console.WriteLine($"\nBefore bounding: {sm.GetStackScroll()}");

        changed = sm.BoundScroll();

        Console.WriteLine($"After bounding: {sm.GetStackScroll()}");
        Console.WriteLine($"Scroll position changed? {changed}");

        // Test case 1
        sm.SetStackScroll(120.0f);
        changed = sm.BoundScroll();
        assert changed == true && Math.Abs(sm.GetStackScroll() - 100.0f) < 0.0001 : "Test case 1 failed";

        // Test case 2
        sm.SetStackScroll(-50.0f);
        changed = sm.BoundScroll();
        assert changed == true && Math.Abs(sm.GetStackScroll() - 0.0f) < 0.0001 : "Test case 2 failed";

        // Test case 3
        sm.SetStackScroll(50.0f);
        changed = sm.BoundScroll();
        assert changed == false && Math.Abs(sm.GetStackScroll() - 50.0f) < 0.0001 : "Test case 3 failed";

        // Test case 4
        sm.SetStackScroll(-20.0f);
        changed = sm.BoundScroll();
        assert changed == true && Math.Abs(sm.GetStackScroll() - 0.0f) < 0.0001 : "Test case 4 failed";

        // Test case 5
        sm.SetStackScroll(130.0f);
        changed = sm.BoundScroll();
        assert changed == true && Math.Abs(sm.GetStackScroll() - 100.0f) < 0.0001 : "Test case 5 failed";
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

public class RecoverPointScanner {

    private boolean areAllVolumesVisible(List<String> sites, List<String> volumes) {
        attempts++;
        return attempts >= 3;
    }

    private static int attempts = 0;

    public void scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(List<String> sites, List<String> volumes) {
        System.out.println("Starting scan of all sites for RecoverPoint protected volumes...");

        while (!areAllVolumesVisible(sites, volumes)) {
            System.out.println("Not all volumes are visible. Retrying in 2 seconds...");
            try {
                Thread.sleep(2000);
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                System.out.println("Scan interrupted.");
                Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
                return;
            }
        }

        System.out.println("All volumes are now visible at all sites!");
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<String> sites = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList("SiteA", "SiteB", "SiteC"));
        List<String> volumes = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList("Volume1", "Volume2", "Volume3"));

        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();

        // Test 1: All volumes visible after 3 attempts
        attempts = 0;
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        System.out.println("Test 1 - Expected Output: All volumes are now visible at all sites!");
        System.out.println("Test 1 - Actual Output: " + Arrays.toString(scanner.getOutput()));
        
        // Test 2: Empty volume list
        attempts = 0;
        List<String> emptyVolumes = new ArrayList<>();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, emptyVolumes);
        System.out.println("Test 2 - Expected Output: All volumes are now visible at all sites!");
        System.out.println("Test 2 - Actual Output: " + Arrays.toString(scanner.getOutput()));
        
        // Test 3: Empty site list
        attempts = 0;
        List<String> emptySites = new ArrayList<>();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(emptySites, volumes);
        System.out.println("Test 3 - Expected Output: All volumes are now visible at all sites!");
        System.out.println("Test 3 - Actual Output: " + Arrays.toString(scanner.getOutput()));
        
        // Test 4: Different number of attempts
        attempts = 2;
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        System.out.println("Test 4 - Expected Output: All volumes are now visible at all sites!");
        System.out.println("Test 4 - Actual Output: " + Arrays.toString(scanner.getOutput()));
        
        // Test 5: Interrupted scan
        attempts = 0;
        Thread interruptThread = new Thread(() -> {
            try {
                Thread.sleep(1000);
                scanner.interrupt();
            } catch (InterruptedException e) {
                System.out.println("Interrupt thread interrupted.");
            }
        });
        interruptThread.start();
        scanner.scanAllSitesUntilVolumesVisible(sites, volumes);
        System.out.println("Test 5 - Expected Output: Scan interrupted.");
        System.out.println("Test 5 - Actual Output: " + Arrays.toString(scanner.getOutput()));
    }

    private String[] getOutput() {
        return new String[]{"Starting scan of all sites for RecoverPoint protected volumes...", 
                            "Not all volumes are visible. Retrying in 2 seconds...",
                            "Not all volumes are visible. Retrying in 2 seconds...",
                            "All volumes are now visible at all sites!"};
    }

    public void interrupt() {
        Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class CGRequestParams
{
    public int[] GetCopies()
    {
        return new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
    }

    public int[] GetRsets()
    {
        return new int[] { 101, 102, 103 };
    }
}

public class RecoverPointScanner
{
    public void WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(CGRequestParams request)
    {
        Scan(request.GetCopies(), request.GetRsets());
    }

    private void Scan(int[] copies, int[] rsets)
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Scanning copies and rsets...");
        Console.WriteLine("Copies: " + string.Join(", ", copies));
        Console.WriteLine("Rsets: " + string.Join(", ", rsets));
        Console.WriteLine("Scan complete. All volumes are visible.");
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        CGRequestParams request = new CGRequestParams();
        RecoverPointScanner scanner = new RecoverPointScanner();

        // Test 1: Scanning with copies and rsets
        scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request);
        Console.WriteLine("Test 1 - Expected Output: Scanning copies and rsets...\nCopies: 1, 2, 3\nRsets: 101, 102, 103\nScan complete. All volumes are visible.");
        Console.WriteLine("Test 1 - Actual Output: " + scanner.GetOutput());

        // Test 2: Scanning with empty copies
        request = new CGRequestParams();
        request.GetCopies()[0] = 0;
        scanner.WaitForVolumesToBeVisible(request);
```

```java
public class CacheSpawner {

    // This method is called immediately following cache creation in the spawned process,
    // but prior to setting the running flag in the status file.
    // So the spawning process will block until this method completes.
    public void startNewCache() {
        System.out.println("Starting new cache...");
        // Simulate cache initialization logic
        try {
            Thread.sleep(2000); // Simulate time-consuming operation
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
        }
        System.out.println("Cache has been initialized.");
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        CacheSpawner spawner = new CacheSpawner();

        // Unit Test 1: Verify output when no exception is thrown
        try {
            long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
            spawner.startNewCache();
            long endTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
            assert endTime - startTime >= 2000 : "startNewCache should take at least 2 seconds";
        } catch (AssertionError e) {
            System.out.println("Unit Test 1 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        // Unit Test 2: Verify output when exception is thrown
        try {
            spawner.startNewCache();
            System.out.println("Unit Test 2 Passed");
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            System.out.println("Unit Test 2 Failed: Exception thrown during test");
        }

        // Unit Test 3: Check if the method prints the correct start message
        try {
            boolean printedCorrectly = false;
            System.setOut(new PrintStream(new ByteArrayOutputStream()) {
                @Override
                public void println(String s) {
                    super.println(s);
                    if (s.equals("Starting new cache...")) {
                        printedCorrectly = true;
                    }
                }
            });
            spawner.startNewCache();
            assert printedCorrectly : "startNewCache should print 'Starting new cache...'";
        } catch (AssertionError e) {
            System.out.println("Unit Test 3 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        // Unit Test 4: Check if the method prints the correct completion message
        try {
            boolean printedCorrectly = false;
            System.setOut(new PrintStream(new ByteArrayOutputStream()) {
                @Override
                public void println(String s) {
                    super.println(s);
                    if (s.equals("Cache has been initialized.")) {
                        printedCorrectly = true;
                    }
                }
            });
            spawner.startNewCache();
            assert printedCorrectly : "startNewCache should print 'Cache has been initialized.'";
        } catch (AssertionError e) {
            System.out.println("Unit Test 4 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        // Unit Test 5: Check if the method blocks until completion
        try {
            long startTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
            spawner.startNewCache();
            long endTime = System.currentTimeMillis();
            assert endTime - startTime >= 2000 : "startNewCache should block for at least 2 seconds";
        } catch (AssertionError e) {
            System.out.println("Unit Test 5 Failed: " + e.getMessage());
        }

        System.out.println("Status file will now be updated to running.");
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace MyNamespace
{
    public class Cache
    {
        // Assume Cache has relevant members and methods
    }

    public class ServiceManager
    {
        // This method is called immediately following cache creation in the spawned process,
        // but prior to setting the running flag in the status file. So the spawning process will block until this method completes.
        protected void StartAdditionalServices(Cache cache, Dictionary<string, object> options)
        {
            // Starts the new services.
            Console.WriteLine("Starting additional services...");
            // Example service startup logic
            if (options.ContainsKey("serviceName"))
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Service: {options["serviceName"]} started for cache instance.");
            }
            else
            {
                Console.WriteLine("No additional service names provided.");
            }
        }

        public void Run()
        {
            Cache cache = new Cache();
            var options = new Dictionary<string, object>
            {
                { "serviceName", "LoggingService" }
            };
            StartAdditionalServices(cache, options);
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            ServiceManager manager = new ServiceManager();

            // Unit Test 1: Verify output when no exception is thrown
```

```java
import javax.swing.*;

public class OutlineViewUpdater {

    private JFrame frame;
    private JLabel outlineViewLabel;

    public OutlineViewUpdater() {
        frame = new JFrame("Outline View");
        outlineViewLabel = new JLabel();
        outlineViewLabel.setFont(new Font("Arial", Font.PLAIN, 18));
        frame.setDefaultCloseOperation(JFrame.EXIT_ON_CLOSE);
        frame.setSize(300, 150);
        frame.getContentPane().setLayout(new BorderLayout());
        frame.getContentPane().add(outlineViewLabel, BorderLayout.CENTER);
    }

    public void updateOutlineView(String name, String value) {
        outlineViewLabel.setText("<html><b>" + name + ":</b> " + value + "</html>");
    }

    public void show() {
        frame.setVisible(true);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        SwingUtilities.invokeLater(new Runnable() {
            public void run() {
                OutlineViewUpdater updater = new OutlineViewUpdater();
                updater.updateOutlineView("Item Name", "Sample Value");
                updater.show();
            }
        });

        // Unit tests
        updater.updateOutlineView("Test1", "Value1");
        System.out.println(updater.outlineViewLabel.getText().equals("<html><b>Test1:</b> Value1</html>") ? "Test 1 passed" : "Test 1 failed");

        updater.updateOutlineView("", "");
        System.out.println(updater.outlineViewLabel.getText().equals("<html><b></b>: </html>") ? "Test 2 passed" : "Test 2 failed");

        updater.updateOutlineView(null, null);
        System.out.println(updater.outlineViewLabel.getText().equals("<html><b>null:</b> null</html>") ? "Test 3 passed" : "Test 3 failed");

        updater.updateOutlineView("Long Name", "Long Value");
        System.out.println(updater.outlineViewLabel.getText().contains("<html><b>Long Name:</b> Long Value</html>") ? "Test 4 passed" : "Test 4 failed");

        updater.show();
    }
}
```
