### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;

namespace NCNameValidation
{
    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            string[] testNames = { "abc", "_validName", ":invalid", "1stName", "name-with-hyphen", "name.with.dot", "name_with_underscore", "" };
            foreach (string name in testNames)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"\"{name}\" is valid NCName? {IsValidNCName(name)}");
            }
        }

        public static bool IsValidNCName(string ncName)
        {
            if (ncName.Length == 0)
                return false;

            char ch = ncName[0];
            if (!IsNCNameStart(ch))
                return false;

            for (int i = 1; i < ncName.Length; i++)
            {
                ch = ncName[i];
                if (!IsNCName(ch))
                    return false;
            }
            return true;
        }

        public static bool IsNCNameStart(char ch)
        {
            return (char.IsLetter(ch) || ch == '_');
        }

        public static bool IsNCName(char ch)
        {
            return (IsNCNameStart(ch) ||
                    char.IsDigit(ch) ||
                    ch == '.' ||
                    ch == '-');
        }

        // Unit Tests
        public static void runTests()
        {
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("ValidName") == true);
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("_validName") == true);
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("valid.name-123") == true);
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("1InvalidStart") == false);
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("invalid:name") == false);
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("Invalid Name") == false);
            Debug.Assert(IsValidNCName("") == false);

            Console.WriteLine("All C# unit tests passed.");
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace GeneratedCode
{
    public class RepeatedFieldBuilder<MType, BType, IType>
    {
        private List<MType> messages = new List<MType>();
        private List<object> builders = null;
        private int modCount = 0;

        // Ensures the internal message list is mutable (for the sake of this example, do nothing)
        private void EnsureMutableMessageList()
        {
            // Placeholder for actual implementation
        }

        // Called when the list has changed
        private void OnChanged()
        {
            // Placeholder for change notification logic
        }

        // Increment modification counts or version
        private void IncrementModCounts()
        {
            modCount++;
        }

        public RepeatedFieldBuilder<MType, BType, IType> AddMessage(MType message)
        {
            if (message == null)
            {
                throw new ArgumentNullException(nameof(message));
            }
            EnsureMutableMessageList();
            messages.Add(message);
            if (builders != null)
            {
                builders.Add(null);
            }
            OnChanged();
            IncrementModCounts();
            return this;
        }
    }

    public class RepeatedFieldBuilderTest
    {
        public static void Main()
        {
            RepeatedFieldBuilder<string, object, int> builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<string, object, int>();
            builder.AddMessage("Element1");
            if (builder.messages.Count != 1 || builder.messages[0] != "Element1") throw new AssertionError("Failed test 1");

            builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<string, object, int>();
            builder.AddMessage("Element2");
            builder.AddMessage("Element3");
            if (builder.messages.Count != 2 || builder.messages[0] != "Element2" || builder.messages[1] != "Element3") throw new AssertionError("Failed test 2");

            builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<string, object, int>();
            for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
            {
                builder.AddMessage($"Element{i}");
            }
            if (builder.messages.Count != 10) throw new AssertionError("Failed test 3");

            builder = new RepeatedFieldBuilder<string, object, int>();
            try
            {
                builder.AddMessage(null);
                throw new AssertionError("Failed test 4");
            }
            catch (ArgumentNullException e) { }

            Console.WriteLine("All tests passed!");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.Arrays;

public class ServiceRegistry {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Test case 1: Empty proxyRegSet
        Set<ProxyReg> emptyProxyRegSet = Set.of();
        assert Arrays.equals(builds(emptyProxyRegSet), new ServiceRegistrar[0]);

        // Test case 2: Single element in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg singleReg = new ProxyReg("Single");
        Set<ProxyReg> singleProxyRegSet = Set.of(singleReg);
        ServiceRegistrar[] expectedSingle = {new ServiceRegistrar("Single")};
        assert Arrays.equals(builds(singleProxyRegSet), expectedSingle);

        // Test case 3: Multiple elements in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg reg1 = new ProxyReg("First");
        ProxyReg reg2 = new ProxyReg("Second");
        ProxyReg reg3 = new ProxyReg("Third");
        Set<ProxyReg> multipleProxyRegSet = Set.of(reg1, reg2, reg3);
        ServiceRegistrar[] expectedMultiple = {new ServiceRegistrar("First"), new ServiceRegistrar("Second"), new ServiceRegistrar("Third")};
        assert Arrays.equals(builds(multipleProxyRegSet), expectedMultiple);

        // Test case 4: Null elements in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg nullReg = new ProxyReg(null);
        Set<ProxyReg> nullProxyRegSet = Set.of(singleReg, nullReg, reg3);
        ServiceRegistrar[] expectedNull = {new ServiceRegistrar("Single"), null, new ServiceRegistrar("Third")};
        assert Arrays.equals(builds(nullProxyRegSet), expectedNull);

        // Test case 5: Duplicate elements in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg duplicateReg = new ProxyReg("Duplicate");
        Set<ProxyReg> duplicateProxyRegSet = Set.of(duplicateReg, duplicateReg);
        ServiceRegistrar[] expectedDuplicate = {new ServiceRegistrar("Duplicate")};
        assert Arrays.equals(builds(duplicateProxyRegSet), expectedDuplicate);

        System.out.println("All unit tests passed.");
    }

    // Simulating a proxy registration set as a static field
    private static Set<ProxyReg> proxyRegSet = Set.of(
            new ProxyReg("RegistryA"), new ProxyReg("RegistryB")
    );

    // Builds method: returns array of ServiceRegistrar created from the proxyRegSet
    public static ServiceRegistrar[] builds(Set<ProxyReg> proxyRegSet) {
        return proxyRegSet.stream()
                .map(ProxyReg::toServiceRegistrar)
                .toArray(ServiceRegistrar[]::new);
    }
}

// Dummy class representing a proxy registration
class ProxyReg {
    private final String name;

    public ProxyReg(String name) {
        this.name = name;
    }

    public ServiceRegistrar toServiceRegistrar() {
        return new ServiceRegistrar(name);
    }
}

// Dummy class representing a service registrar
class ServiceRegistrar {
    private final String name;

    public ServiceRegistrar(String name) {
        this.name = name;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "ServiceRegistrar{" +
                "name='" + name + '\'' +
                '}';
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;

public class ServiceRegistrar
{
    public string Name { get; set; }

    public ServiceRegistrar(string name)
    {
        Name = name;
    }
}

public class ProxyReg
{
    public ServiceRegistrar proxy;

    public ProxyReg(ServiceRegistrar proxy)
    {
        this.proxy = proxy;
    }
}

public class ServiceRegistrarBuilder
{
    private int _NUM;
    private HashSet<ProxyReg> proxyRegSet;

    public ServiceRegistrarBuilder(int num, HashSet<ProxyReg> proxyRegSet)
    {
        this._NUM = num;
        this.proxyRegSet = proxyRegSet;
    }

    // returns array of serviceregistrar created from the proxyregset
    private ServiceRegistrar[] BuildServiceRegistrar()
    {
        int k = _NUM;
        ServiceRegistrar[] proxys = new ServiceRegistrar[proxyRegSet.Count];
        IEnumerator<ProxyReg> iter = proxyRegSet.GetEnumerator();
        while (iter.MoveNext())
        {
            ProxyReg reg = iter.Current;
            proxys[k++] = reg.proxy;
        }
        return proxys;
    }

    // Exposing BuildServiceRegistrar for demonstration
    public ServiceRegistrar[] Build()
    {
        return BuildServiceRegistrar();
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        var proxyRegSet = new HashSet<ProxyReg>
        {
            new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("A")),
            new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("B")),
            new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("C"))
        };

        int num = 0;
        var builder = new ServiceRegistrarBuilder(num, proxyRegSet);
        ServiceRegistrar[] result = builder.Build();

        Console.WriteLine("ServiceRegistrar array:");
        for (int i = 0; i < result.Length; i++)
        {
            if (result[i] != null)
                Console.WriteLine($"{i}: {result[i].Name}");
            else
                Console.WriteLine($"{i}: null");
        }

        // Test case 1: Empty proxyRegSet
        HashSet<ProxyReg> emptyProxyRegSet = new HashSet<ProxyReg>();
        var builderEmpty = new ServiceRegistrarBuilder(num, emptyProxyRegSet);
        var resultEmpty = builderEmpty.Build();
        AssertEqual(resultEmpty, new ServiceRegistrar[0]);

        // Test case 2: Single element in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg singleReg = new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("Single"));
        HashSet<ProxyReg> singleProxyRegSet = new HashSet<ProxyReg> { singleReg };
        var builderSingle = new ServiceRegistrarBuilder(num, singleProxyRegSet);
        var resultSingle = builderSingle.Build();
        AssertEqual(resultSingle, new[] { new ServiceRegistrar("Single") });

        // Test case 3: Multiple elements in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg reg1 = new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("First"));
        ProxyReg reg2 = new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("Second"));
        ProxyReg reg3 = new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("Third"));
        HashSet<ProxyReg> multipleProxyRegSet = new HashSet<ProxyReg> { reg1, reg2, reg3 };
        var builderMultiple = new ServiceRegistrarBuilder(num, multipleProxyRegSet);
        var resultMultiple = builderMultiple.Build();
        AssertEqual(resultMultiple, new[] { new ServiceRegistrar("First"), new ServiceRegistrar("Second"), new ServiceRegistrar("Third") });

        // Test case 4: Null elements in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg nullReg = new ProxyReg(null);
        HashSet<ProxyReg> nullProxyRegSet = new HashSet<ProxyReg> { singleReg, nullReg, reg3 };
        var builderNull = new ServiceRegistrarBuilder(num, nullProxyRegSet);
        var resultNull = builderNull.Build();
        AssertEqual(resultNull, new[] { new ServiceRegistrar("Single"), null, new ServiceRegistrar("Third") });

        // Test case 5: Duplicate elements in proxyRegSet
        ProxyReg duplicateReg = new ProxyReg(new ServiceRegistrar("Duplicate"));
        HashSet<ProxyReg> duplicateProxyRegSet = new HashSet<ProxyReg> { duplicateReg, duplicateReg };
        var builderDuplicate = new ServiceRegistrarBuilder(num, duplicateProxyRegSet);
        var resultDuplicate = builderDuplicate.Build();
        AssertEqual(resultDuplicate, new[] { new ServiceRegistrar("Duplicate") });
    }

    public static void AssertEqual(ServiceRegistrar[] a1, ServiceRegistrar[] a2)
    {
        if (!a1.SequenceEqual(a2))
            throw new Exception("Arrays are not equal.");
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;

public class Program
{
    private const int _NUM = 3;
    private const string _STR = "Invalid character: ";
    private const bool _BOOL = true;

    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        try
        {
            string testVal1 = "abc";
            string testVal2 = "abcd";
            string testVal3 = null;
            string testVal4 = "ab1";
            Console.WriteLine($"CheckValue(\"{testVal1}\") = {CheckValue(testVal1)}"); // true
            Console.WriteLine($"CheckValue(\"{testVal2}\") = {CheckValue(testVal2)}"); // throws MalformedObjectNameException
            Console.WriteLine($"CheckValue(null) = {CheckValue(testVal3)}"); // throws NullReferenceException
            Console.WriteLine($"CheckValue(\"{testVal4}\") = {CheckValue(testVal4)}"); // throws MalformedObjectNameException

            // Unit tests
            Assert.IsTrue(CheckValue("abc"), "Should be true");
            Assert.Throws<NullReferenceException>(() => CheckValue(null));
            Assert.IsFalse(CheckValue("Hello"));
        }
        catch (Exception ex)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"Exception: {ex.Message}");
        }
    }

    private static bool CheckValue(string val)
    {
        if (val == null)
            throw new NullReferenceException(_STR);

        int len = val.Length;
        if (len == _NUM)
            return _BOOL;

        char[] s = val.ToCharArray();
        int[] result = ParseValue(s, _NUM);
        int endValue = result[_NUM];
        bool valuePattern = result[_NUM] == _NUM;
        if (endValue < len)
            throw new MalformedObjectNameException(_STR + s[endValue] + _STR);
        return valuePattern;
    }

    // Example implementation: parse value up to _NUM characters,
    // if there's a non-alphabet character or length mismatch, error.
    // result[_NUM] is index where parsing stopped.
    private static int[] ParseValue(char[] s, int maxLength)
    {
        int[] result = new int[maxLength + 1];
        int i = 0;
        for (; i < s.Length && i < maxLength; i++)
        {
            if (!Char.IsLetter(s[i]))
            {
                break;
            }
            result[i] = 1;
        }
        result[maxLength] = i; // endValue (index where parsing stopped)
        return result;
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

public class ServerSelector {
    private List<String> backupServers = new ArrayList<>();
    private List<String> oldServers = new ArrayList<>();
    private Set<String> excludedServers = new HashSet<>();
    private String primaryServer = null;

    public ServerSelector(List<String> backups, List<String> olds) {
        this.backupServers.addAll(backups);
        this.oldServers.addAll(olds);
    }

    public boolean findNewPrimary() {
        Iterator<String> iter = backupServers.iterator();
        while (iter.hasNext()) {
            String server = iter.next();
            if (!excludedServers.contains(server)) {
                if (isServerHealthy(server)) {
                    primaryServer = server;
                    iter.remove();
                    return true;
                } else {
                    excludedServers.add(server);
                }
            }
        }

        iter = oldServers.iterator();
        while (iter.hasNext()) {
            String server = iter.next();
            if (!excludedServers.contains(server)) {
                if (isServerHealthy(server)) {
                    primaryServer = server;
                    iter.remove();
                    return true;
                } else {
                    excludedServers.add(server);
                }
            }
        }

        return false;
    }

    private boolean isServerHealthy(String server) {
        return !server.contains("fail");
    }

    public String getPrimaryServer() {
        return primaryServer;
    }

    public List<String> getBackupServers() {
        return backupServers;
    }

    public List<String> getOldServers() {
        return oldServers;
    }

    public Set<String> getExcludedServers() {
        return excludedServers;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<String> backups = new ArrayList<>();
        backups.add("backup1");
        backups.add("backup_fail1");
        backups.add("backup2");

        List<String> olds = new ArrayList<>();
        olds.add("old_fail1");
        olds.add("old1");
        olds.add("old2");

        ServerSelector selector = new ServerSelector(backups, olds);

        boolean found = selector.findNewPrimary();

        if (found) {
            System.out.println("New primary server: " + selector.getPrimaryServer());
        } else {
            System.out.println("No primary server found.");
        }
        System.out.println("Excluded servers: " + selector.getExcludedServers());
        System.out.println("Backup servers remaining: " + selector.getBackupServers());
        System.out.println("Old servers remaining: " + selector.getOldServers());

        // Unit Tests
        backups.clear();
        olds.clear();

        backups.add("backup1");
        olds.add("old2");

        selector = new ServerSelector(backups, olds);
        found = selector.findNewPrimary();
        assert found : "Test 1 failed. Expected primary server to be found.";
        assert selector.getPrimaryServer().equals("backup1") : "Test 1 failed. Incorrect primary server.";

        backups.clear();
        olds.clear();

        backups.add("backup_fail1");
        olds.add("old2");

        selector = new ServerSelector(backups, olds);
        found = selector.findNewPrimary();
        assert !found : "Test 2 failed. Expected no primary server to be found.";
        assert selector.getExcludedServers().contains("backup_fail1") : "Test 2 failed. Backup server not excluded.";

        backups.clear();
        olds.clear();

        backups.add("backup1");
        olds.add("old_fail1");

        selector = new ServerSelector(backups, olds);
        found = selector.findNewPrimary();
        assert found : "Test 3 failed. Expected primary server to be found.";
        assert selector.getPrimaryServer().equals("backup1") : "Test 3 failed. Incorrect primary server.";

        backups.clear();
        olds.clear();

        selector = new ServerSelector(new ArrayList<>(), new ArrayList<>());
        found = selector.findNewPrimary();
        assert !found : "Test 4 failed. Expected no primary server to be found.";
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ServerRecoveryApp
{
    public class Server { public string Name { get; set; } }

    public class Pool
    {
        public object GetPoolOrCacheCancelInProgress()
        {
            return null;
        }
    }

    public class Logger
    {
        public bool IsDebugEnabled() => true;
        public void Debug(string msg) => Console.WriteLine("DEBUG: " + msg);
        public void Info(string msg) => Console.WriteLine("INFO: " + msg);
    }

    public static class LocalizedStrings
    {
        public const string QueueManagerImpl_SUBSCRIPTION_MANAGER_REDUNDANCY_SATISFIER_PRIMARY_ENDPOINT_HAS_BEEN_LOST_ATTEMPTIMG_TO_RECOVER =
            "Primary endpoint lost. Attempting to recover...";
    }

    public class LocalizedMessage
    {
        public static string Create(string msg) => msg;
    }

    public class QueueConnectionImpl
    {
        public Server Server { get; set; }
        public QueueConnectionImpl(Server server) { Server = server; }
    }

    public class QueueConnections
    {
        private QueueConnectionImpl _primary;
        private List<QueueConnectionImpl> _backups = new List<QueueConnectionImpl>();

        public QueueConnectionImpl GetPrimary() => _primary;

        public List<QueueConnectionImpl> GetBackups() => new List<QueueConnectionImpl>(_backups);

        public void SetPrimary(QueueConnectionImpl conn) => _primary = conn;

        public void AddBackup(QueueConnectionImpl backup) => _backups.Add(backup);

        public void RemoveBackup(QueueConnectionImpl backup) => _backups.Remove(backup);
    }

    public class QueueManagerImpl
    {
        protected Pool pool = new Pool();
        protected Logger logger = new Logger();
        protected QueueConnections queueConnections = new QueueConnections();

        protected const string _STR = "Processing primary recovery.";
        protected const bool _BOOL = true;

        protected bool printRecoveringPrimary = true;

        protected bool AddToConnectionList(QueueConnectionImpl conn)
        {
            logger.Debug($"Added server {conn.Server.Name} to connection list as primary.");
            queueConnections.SetPrimary(conn);
            return true;
        }

        protected QueueConnectionImpl PromoteBackupToPrimary(List<QueueConnectionImpl> backups)
        {
            if (backups.Count > 0)
            {
                QueueConnectionImpl promoted = backups[0];
                queueConnections.RemoveBackup(promoted);
                return promoted;
            }
            return null;
        }

        protected QueueConnectionImpl FindNewBackup(HashSet<Server> excludedServers)
        {
            string[] allServerNames = { "A", "B", "C", "D" };
            foreach (var name in allServerNames)
            {
                Server s = new Server { Name = name };
                if (!excludedServers.Contains(s))
                    return new QueueConnectionImpl(s);
            }
            return null;
        }

        protected void RecoverPrimary(HashSet<Server> excludedServers)
        {
            if (pool.GetPoolOrCacheCancelInProgress() != null)
            {
                return;
            }

            bool isDebugEnabled = logger.IsDebugEnabled();

            if (queueConnections.GetPrimary() != null)
            {
                if (isDebugEnabled)
                {
                    logger.Debug(_STR);
                }
                return;
            }

            if (isDebugEnabled)
            {
                logger.Debug(_STR);
            }

            if (printRecoveringPrimary)
            {
                logger.Info(LocalizedMessage.Create(
                    LocalizedStrings.QueueManagerImpl_SUBSCRIPTION_MANAGER_REDUNDANCY_SATISFIER_PRIMARY_ENDPOINT_HAS_BEEN_LOST_ATTEMPTIMG_TO_RECOVER));
                printRecoveringPrimary = _BOOL;
            }

            QueueConnectionImpl newPrimary = null;
            while (newPrimary == null && pool.GetPoolOrCacheCancelInProgress() == null)
            {
                List<QueueConnectionImpl> backups = queueConnections.GetBackups();
                newPrimary = PromoteBackupToPrimary(backups);

                if (newPrimary == null)
                {
                    newPrimary = FindNewBackup(excludedServers);
                    if (newPrimary == null)
                    {
                        logger.Info("No servers available for primary promotion.");
                        break;
                    }
                }

                if (!AddToConnectionList(newPrimary))
                {
                    excludedServers.Add(newPrimary.Server);
                    logger.Debug($"Failed to add {newPrimary.Server.Name} to connection list as primary.");
                }
            }
        }

        public void RunTests()
        {
            List<QueueConnectionImpl> backups = new List<QueueConnectionImpl>();
            QueueConnectionImpl old2 = new QueueConnectionImpl(new Server { Name = "old2" });
            backups.Add(old2);

            HashSet<Server> excludedServers = new HashSet<Server>();

            RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
            Assert.IsTrue(queueConnections.GetPrimary() != null, "Test 1 failed. Expected primary server to be found.");
            Assert.AreEqual("old2", queueConnections.GetPrimary().Server.Name, "Test 1 failed. Incorrect primary server.");

            backups.Clear();
            old2 = new QueueConnectionImpl(new Server { Name = "backup_fail1" });
            backups.Add(old2);

            RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
            Assert.IsTrue(queueConnections.GetPrimary() == null, "Test 2 failed. Expected no primary server to be found.");
            Assert.IsTrue(excludedServers.Contains(old2.Server), "Test 2 failed. Backup server not excluded.");

            backups.Clear();
            old2 = new QueueConnectionImpl(new Server { Name = "backup1" });
            backups.Add(old2);

            RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
            Assert.IsTrue(queueConnections.GetPrimary() != null, "Test 3 failed. Expected primary server to be found.");
            Assert.AreEqual("backup1", queueConnections.GetPrimary().Server.Name, "Test 3 failed. Incorrect primary server.");

            backups.Clear();
            excludedServers = new HashSet<Server>();
            RecoverPrimary(excludedServers);
            Assert.IsTrue(queueConnections.GetPrimary() == null, "Test 4 failed. Expected no primary server to be found.");
        }
    }

    public static class Program
    {
        public static void Main()
        {
            QueueManagerImpl queueManager = new QueueManagerImpl();
            queueManager.RunTests();
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
namespace JournalApp
{
    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            JournalManager manager = new JournalManager();
            manager.RebuildJournal();
            Console.WriteLine("Journal rebuilt.");

            // Unit tests
            List<string> testEntries1 = new List<string> { "First entry" };
            manager.lruEntries.Clear();
            foreach (var entry in testEntries1)
                manager.lruEntries.Add(entry, new Entry(entry));
            manager.RebuildJournal();
            Assert.AreEqual(string.Join("\n", testEntries1), File.ReadAllText("journal.log"), "Test 1 Failed");

            List<string> testEntries2 = new List<string> { "Entry with duplicate", "Entry with duplicate", "New Entry" };
            manager.lruEntries.Clear();
            foreach (var entry in testEntries2)
                manager.lruEntries.Add(entry, new Entry(entry));
            manager.RebuildJournal();
            Assert.AreEqual("JOURNAL_MAGIC\n1\n42\n2\n\nDIRTY\nEntry with duplicate\nDIRTY\nNew Entry", File.ReadAllText("journal.log"), "Test 2 Failed");

            List<string> testEntries3 = new List<string>();
            manager.lruEntries.Clear();
            manager.RebuildJournal();
            Assert.AreEqual("", File.ReadAllText("journal.log"), "Test 3 Failed");

            List<string> testEntries4 = new List<string> { "Single entry" };
            manager.lruEntries.Clear();
            foreach (var entry in testEntries4)
                manager.lruEntries.Add(entry, new Entry(entry));
            manager.RebuildJournal();
            Assert.AreEqual("JOURNAL_MAGIC\n1\n42\n2\n\nCLEAN\nSingle entry", File.ReadAllText("journal.log"), "Test 4 Failed");

            try
            {
                manager.createNewJournalWithEntries(null);
            }
            catch (NullReferenceException)
            {
                // Expected exception
            }

            Console.WriteLine("All unit tests passed.");
        }
    }

    public class Entry
    {
        public object currentEditor;
        public string key;
        private long[] lengths;

        public Entry(string key, object currentEditor = null, params long[] lengths)
        {
            this.key = key;
            this.currentEditor = currentEditor;
            this.lengths = lengths.Length > 0 ? lengths : new long[] { 0, 1, 2 };
        }

        public string GetLengths()
        {
            StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
            foreach (var len in lengths)
            {
                sb.Append(" ").Append(len);
            }
            return sb.ToString();
        }
    }

    public class JournalManager
    {
        // ... (original code)

        public void RebuildJournal()
        {
            lock (ThisLock)
            {
                if (journalWriter != null)
                {
                    journalWriter.Close();
                }
                using (var writer = new StreamWriter(new FileStream(journalFileTmp, FileMode.Create),
                    new UTF8Encoding(), IO_BUFFER_SIZE))
                {
                    writer.Write(MAGIC);
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    writer.Write(VERSION_1);
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    writer.Write(appVersion.ToString());
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    writer.Write(valueCount.ToString());
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    writer.Write(_STR);
                    foreach (var entry in lruEntries.Values)
                    {
                        if (entry.currentEditor != null)
                        {
                            writer.Write(DIRTY + _STR + entry.key + _STR);
                        }
                        else
                        {
                            writer.Write(CLEAN + _STR + entry.key + entry.GetLengths() + _STR);
                        }
                    }
                }
                File.Copy(journalFileTmp, journalFile, true);
                journalWriter = new StreamWriter(new FileStream(journalFile, FileMode.OpenOrCreate, FileAccess.Write),
                    new UTF8Encoding(), IO_BUFFER_SIZE);
            }
        }

        private object ThisLock => this;

        private StreamWriter OpenWriter(string fileName)
        {
            return new StreamWriter(
                new FileStream(fileName, FileMode.OpenOrCreate, FileAccess.Write, FileShare.None),
                new UTF8Encoding(),
                IO_BUFFER_SIZE
            );
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class RuleManager {
    private List<String> rules;

    public RuleManager() {
        rules = new ArrayList<>();
        rules.add("Rule 1");
        rules.add("Rule 2");
        rules.add("Rule 3");
    }

    public void removeLastRule() {
        if (!rules.isEmpty()) {
            rules.remove(rules.size() - 1);
            System.out.println("Last rule removed from persistent store.");
        } else {
            System.out.println("No rules to remove.");
        }
    }

    public void printRules() {
        System.out.println("Current rules: " + rules);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        RuleManager manager = new RuleManager();
        manager.printRules(); // Test 1: Check initial state
        manager.removeLastRule();
        manager.printRules(); // Test 2: Remove one rule
        manager.removeLastRule();
        manager.printRules(); // Test 3: Remove another rule
        manager.removeLastRule();
        manager.printRules(); // Test 4: Remove last remaining rule
        manager.removeLastRule(); // Test 5: Try to remove when no rules left
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace RecurrenceExample
{
    public class RuleManager
    {
        private List<RecurrenceRule> rRulesList = new List<RecurrenceRule>();
        private List<RecurrenceRule> eRulesList = new List<RecurrenceRule>();

        public RuleManager()
        {
            rRulesList.Add(new RecurrenceRule());
            eRulesList.Add(new RecurrenceRule());
        }

        public void Remove()
        {
            List<RecurrenceRule> rulesList = new List<RecurrenceRule>();
            rulesList.AddRange(rRulesList);
            rulesList.AddRange(eRulesList);
            foreach (var rule in rulesList)
            {
                rule.Remove();
            }
        }

        public static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            RuleManager manager = new RuleManager();
            try
            {
                manager.Remove(); // Test 1: Check basic removal logic
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("Exception caught: " + ex.Message);
            }

            try
            {
                foreach(var rule in manager.rRulesList)
                    rule.Remove();
                
                foreach(var rule in manager.eRulesList)
                    rule.Remove();
                
                manager.Remove(); // Test 2: Try to remove when no rules left
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("Exception caught: " + ex.Message);
            }
        }
    }

    public class RecurrenceRule
    {
        public void Remove()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("RecurrenceRule removed.");
        }
    }
}
```

### Java Code

```java
import org.w3c.dom.Node;
import org.w3c.dom.NodeList;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;

public class CustomNodeList {

    private List<Node> nodes = new ArrayList<>();

    public void copyNodeListMembersInDocumentOrder(NodeList nodeList) {
        List<Node> toAdd = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = 0; i < nodeList.getLength(); i++) {
            Node n = nodeList.item(i);
            if (n != null) {
                toAdd.add(n);
            }
        }
        Collections.sort(toAdd, new DocumentOrderComparator());
        nodes.addAll(toAdd);
    }

    private static class DocumentOrderComparator implements Comparator<Node> {
        @Override
        public int compare(Node n1, Node n2) {
            try {
                short pos = (Short) n1.getClass().getMethod("compareDocumentPosition", Node.class).invoke(n1, n2);
                if ((pos & 0x04) != 0) return -1;
                if ((pos & 0x02) != 0) return 1;
                return 0;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                return Integer.compare(System.identityHashCode(n1), System.identityHashCode(n2));
            }
        }
    }

    public List<Node> getNodes() {
        return nodes;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) throws Exception {
        javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory dbf = javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();
        javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilder db = dbf.newDocumentBuilder();
        org.w3c.dom.Document doc = db.newDocument();

        Node node1 = doc.createElement("node1");
        Node node2 = doc.createElement("node2");
        Node node3 = doc.createElement("node3");

        doc.appendChild(node1);
        doc.appendChild(node2);
        doc.appendChild(node3);

        NodeList nodeList = new NodeList() {
            private Node[] nodes = new Node[] { node2, null, node1, node3 };

            @Override
            public Node item(int index) {
                return nodes[index];
            }

            @Override
            public int getLength() {
                return nodes.length;
            }
        };

        CustomNodeList customNodeList = new CustomNodeList();
        customNodeList.copyNodeListMembersInDocumentOrder(nodeList);

        for (Node n : customNodeList.getNodes()) {
            System.out.println(n.getNodeName());
        }
    }
}
```

### C# Code

```csharp
using System;

namespace XPathNodeListExample
{
    public static class DTM
    {
        public const int NULL = -1;
    }

    public static class XPATHErrorResources
    {
        public const string ER_NODESETDTM_NOT_MUTABLE = "NodeSetDTM is not mutable.";
    }

    public static class XSLMessages
    {
        public static string CreateXPathMessage(string msg, object[] args)
        {
            return msg;
        }
    }

    public class XPathContext
    {
    }

    public abstract class DTMIterator
    {
        public abstract int NextNode();
    }

    public class ArrayDTMIterator : DTMIterator
    {
        private readonly int[] _nodes;
        private int _index = 0;

        public ArrayDTMIterator(int[] nodes)
        {
            _nodes = nodes ?? Array.Empty<int>();
        }

        public override int NextNode()
        {
            if (_index < _nodes.Length)
            {
                return _nodes[_index++];
            }
            return DTM.NULL;
        }
    }

    public class NodeList
    {
        private List<int> nodes = new List<int>();
        private bool m_mutable;

        public NodeList(bool mutable = true)
        {
            m_mutable = mutable;
        }

        public void AddNodeInDocOrder(int node, XPathContext support)
        {
            if (node == DTM.NULL) return;
            int i = nodes.BinarySearch(node);
            if (i < 0)
            {
                nodes.Insert(~i, node);
            }
        }

        public void AddNodesInDocOrder(DTMIterator iterator, XPathContext support)
        {
            if (!m_mutable)
                throw new InvalidOperationException(XSLMessages.CreateXPathMessage(XPATHErrorResources.ER_NODESETDTM_NOT_MUTABLE, null));
            int node;
            while ((node = iterator.NextNode()) != DTM.NULL)
            {
                AddNodeInDocOrder(node, support);
            }
        }

        public void PrintNodes()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Nodes in document order: " + string.Join(", ", nodes));
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main()
        {
            int[] nodeArray = { 10, 5, 8, 5, DTM.NULL, 20 };
            DTMIterator iterator = new ArrayDTMIterator(nodeArray);
            XPathContext context = new XPathContext();
            NodeList nodeList = new NodeList();

            nodeList.AddNodesInDocOrder(iterator, context);

            nodeList.PrintNodes(); // Output: Nodes in document order: 5, 8, 10, 20
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.time.Instant;

public class DataFetcherTest {

    public static void main(String[] args) throws InterruptedException {
        DataFetcher fetcher = new DataFetcher();

        // First check, should fetch data
        boolean result1 = fetcher.isDataValidAtCurrentTime();
        System.out.println("First Check Result: " + result1);

        // Second check within the refresh interval, should not fetch data
        boolean result2 = fetcher.isDataValidAtCurrentTime();
        System.out.println("Second Check Result: " + result2);

        // Wait for a period longer than refresh interval
        Thread.sleep(600000); // 10 minutes

        // Third check after refresh interval, should fetch data again
        boolean result3 = fetcher.isDataValidAtCurrentTime();
        System.out.println("Third Check Result: " + result3);

        // Fourth check within the refresh interval of second call, should not fetch data
        boolean result4 = fetcher.isDataValidAtCurrentTime();
        System.out.println("Fourth Check Result: " + result4);
    }
}
```

```java
import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilder;
import javax.xml.parsers.DocumentBuilderFactory;
import javax.xml.parsers.ParserConfigurationException;
import org.w3c.dom.DOMImplementation;
import org.w3c.dom.DocumentType;

public class CreateEmptyDocumentType {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        try {
            // Create a DocumentBuilderFactory
            DocumentBuilderFactory factory = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();
            // Create a DocumentBuilder
            DocumentBuilder builder = factory.newDocumentBuilder();
            // Get DOMImplementation from builder
            DOMImplementation domImpl = builder.getDOMImplementation();
            // Create a new empty DocumentType node
            // Empty name, publicId, and systemId
            DocumentType doctype = domImpl.createDocumentType("", "", "");
            // Print the DocumentType node's properties
            System.out.println("DocumentType node created:");
            System.out.println("Name: '" + doctype.getName() + "'");
            System.out.println("PublicId: '" + doctype.getPublicId() + "'");
            System.out.println("SystemId: '" + doctype.getSystemId() + "'");
        } catch (ParserConfigurationException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit tests
        try {
            DocumentBuilderFactory factory = DocumentBuilderFactory.newInstance();
            DocumentBuilder builder = factory.newDocumentBuilder();
            DOMImplementation domImpl = builder.getDOMImplementation();

            // Test case 1: Create an empty DocumentType node
            DocumentType doctype1 = domImpl.createDocumentType("", "", "");
            assert " ".equals(doctype1.getName()) : "Test case 1 failed";
            assert "".equals(doctype1.getPublicId()) : "Test case 1 failed";
            assert "".equals(doctype1.getSystemId()) : "Test case 1 failed";

            // Test case 2: Create a DocumentType node with non-empty values
            DocumentType doctype2 = domImpl.createDocumentType("note", "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN", "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd");
            assert "note".equals(doctype2.getName()) : "Test case 2 failed";
            assert "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN".equals(doctype2.getPublicId()) : "Test case 2 failed";
            assert "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd".equals(doctype2.getSystemId()) : "Test case 2 failed";

            // Test case 3: Create a DocumentType node with publicId but no systemId
            DocumentType doctype3 = domImpl.createDocumentType("note", "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN", null);

            assert "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN".equals(doctype3.getPublicId()) : "Test case 3 failed";
            assert "".equals(doctype3.getSystemId()) : "Test case 3 failed";
```

### C# Code

```csharp
using System;

namespace DomSample
{
    // Simulate DOM Level 2 DocumentType and related structures

    public class DocumentType
    {
        public string Name { get; }
        public string PublicId { get; }
        public string SystemId { get; }

        public DocumentType(string name, string publicId, string systemId)
        {
            Name = name;
            PublicId = publicId;
            SystemId = systemId;
        }

        public override string ToString()
        {
            return $"DocumentType(Name='{Name}', PublicId='{PublicId}', SystemId='{SystemId}')";
        }
    }

    public class DocumentTypeImpl : DocumentType
    {
        public object OwnerDocument { get; }

        public DocumentTypeImpl(object ownerDocument, string qualifiedName, string publicID, string systemID)
            : base(qualifiedName, publicID, systemID)
        {
            OwnerDocument = ownerDocument;
        }
    }

    public class DomImplementation
    {
        protected void CheckQName(string qualifiedName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(qualifiedName))
                throw new ArgumentException("Qualified name cannot be null or empty.");

            // Simple check: must be a valid XML name (very simplified)
            if (!char.IsLetter(qualifiedName[0]))
                throw new ArgumentException("Qualified name must start with a letter.");
            // For demonstration, not a comprehensive check
        }

        // introduced in dom level 2.
        // creates an empty documenttype node.
        public virtual DocumentType CreateDocumentType(string qualifiedName, string publicID, string systemID)
        {
            CheckQName(qualifiedName);
            return new DocumentTypeImpl(null, qualifiedName, publicID, systemID);
        }
    }

    public class CustomDomImplementation : DomImplementation
    {
        public override DocumentType CreateDocumentType(string qualifiedName, string publicID, string systemID)
        {
            CheckQName(qualifiedName);
            return new DocumentTypeImpl(null, qualifiedName, publicID, systemID);
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            var domImpl = new CustomDomImplementation();
            try
            {
                // Example usage
                var docType = domImpl.CreateDocumentType("note", "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN", "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd");
                Console.WriteLine(docType);

                // Uncomment this to test invalid qualified name
                //var badDocType = domImpl.CreateDocumentType("1note", "public", "system");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Exception: {ex.Message}");
            }

            // Unit tests
            try
            {
                var domImpl2 = new CustomDomImplementation();

                // Test case 1: Create an empty DocumentType node
                var doctype1 = domImpl2.CreateDocumentType("", "", "");
                Assert(" ".Equals(doctype1.Name), "Test case 1 failed");
                Assert(string.IsNullOrEmpty(doctype1.PublicId), "Test case 1 failed");
                Assert(string.IsNullOrEmpty(doctype1.SystemId), "Test case 1 failed");

                // Test case 2: Create a DocumentType node with non-empty values
                var doctype2 = domImpl2.CreateDocumentType("note", "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN", "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd");
                Assert("note".Equals(doctype2.Name), "Test case 2 failed");
                Assert("//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN".Equals(doctype2.PublicId), "Test case 2 failed");
                Assert("http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd".Equals(doctype2.SystemId), "Test case 2 failed");

                // Test case 3: Create a DocumentType node with publicId but no systemId
                var doctype3 = domImpl2.CreateDocumentType("note", "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN", null);
                Assert("note".Equals(doctype3.Name), "Test case 3 failed");
                Assert("//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN".Equals(doctype3.PublicId), "Test case 3 failed");
                Assert(string.IsNullOrEmpty(doctype3.SystemId), "Test case 3 failed");

                // Test case 4: Create a DocumentType node with systemId but no publicId
                var doctype4 = domImpl2.CreateDocumentType("note", null, "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd");
                Assert("note".Equals(doctype4.Name), "Test case 4 failed");
                Assert(string.IsNullOrEmpty(doctype4.PublicId), "Test case 4 failed");
                Assert("http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd".Equals(doctype4.SystemId), "Test case 4 failed");

                // Test case 5: Create a DocumentType node with both publicId and systemId
                var doctype5 = domImpl2.CreateDocumentType("note", "-//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN", "http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd");
                Assert("note".Equals(doctype5.Name), "Test case 5 failed");
                Assert("//W3C//DTD XHTML 1.0 Strict//EN".Equals(doctype5.PublicId), "Test case 5 failed");
                Assert("http://www.w3.org/TR/xhtml1/DTD/xhtml1-strict.dtd".Equals(doctype5.SystemId), "Test case 5 failed");

                Console.WriteLine("All test cases passed!");
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"Exception: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        private static void Assert(bool condition, string message)
        {
            if (!condition) throw new Exception(message);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.Arrays;

public class CommandLineService {
    public void service(String[] args) {
        // Services a commandline request.
        if (args == null || args.length == 0) {
            System.out.println("No command provided.");
            return;
        }
        String command = args[0];
        switch(command) {
            case "greet":
                System.out.println("Hello, " + (args.length > 1 ? args[1] : "World") + "!");
                break;
            case "sum":
                int sum = 0;
                for (int i = 1; i < args.length; i++) {
                    try {
                        sum += Integer.parseInt(args[i]);
                    } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                        System.out.println("Invalid number: " + args[i]);
                        return;
                    }
                }
                System.out.println("Sum is: " + sum);
                break;
            default:
                System.out.println("Unknown command: " + command);
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        CommandLineService service = new CommandLineService();
        
        // Unit Test 1
        service.service(new String[]{"greet", "Alice"});
        // Expected Output: Hello, Alice!
        
        // Unit Test 2
        service.service(new String[]{"sum", "10", "20", "30"});
        // Expected Output: Sum is: 60
        
        // Unit Test 3
        service.service(new String[]{"greet"});
        // Expected Output: Hello, World!
        
        // Unit Test 4
        service.service(new String[]{"sum", "10", "invalid", "20"});
        // Expected Output: Invalid number: invalid
        
        // Unit Test 5
        service.service(new String[]{});
        // Expected Output: No command provided.
```

```csharp
using System;

namespace CommandLineService
{
    public class CommandLineServiceHandler : BaseHandler
    {
        private const string _STR = "*";

        private Dictionary<string, string> _options = new Dictionary<string, string>
        {
            { IArgument.REALM_NAME, "realm1" },
            { IArgument.AGENT_TYPE, null },
            { IArgument.FILTER, null }
        };

        public override void HandleRequest(RequestContext rc)
        {
            base.HandleRequest(rc);
            LdapLogin();
            SSOToken adminSSOToken = GetAdminSSOToken();
            IOutput outputWriter = GetOutputWriter();
            string realm = GetStringOptionValue(IArgument.REALM_NAME);
            string patternType = GetStringOptionValue(IArgument.AGENT_TYPE);
            string filter = GetStringOptionValue(IArgument.FILTER);

            if (patternType == null)
            {
                patternType = _STR;
            }
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(filter))
            {
                filter = _STR;
            }
            string[] parameters = { realm, patternType, filter };
            WriteLog(LogWriter.LOG_ACCESS, LogLevel.INFO, _STR, parameters);

            try
            {
                AMIdentityRepository amir = new AMIdentityRepository(adminSSOToken, realm);
                IdSearchResults isr = amir.SearchIdentities(IdType.AGENTONLY, filter, new IdSearchControl());
                var results = isr.GetSearchResults();
                if (results != null && results.Count > 0)
                {
                    results.RemoveAll(am => !MatchType(am, patternType));
                }
                if (results != null)
                {
                    outputWriter.Write($"Found {results.Count} matching agents:");
                    foreach (var am in results)
                    {
                        outputWriter.Write($"Agent Type: {am.Type}");
                    }
                }
                else
                {
                    outputWriter.Write("No results found.");
                }
            }
            catch (Exception ex)
            {
                outputWriter.Write($"Exception: {ex.Message}");
            }
        }

        private void LdapLogin()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("LdapLogin called.");
        }

        private SSOToken GetAdminSSOToken()
        {
            return new SSOToken();
        }

        private IOutput GetOutputWriter()
        {
            return new ConsoleOutput();
        }

        private string GetStringOptionValue(string key)
        {
            if (_options.TryGetValue(key, out string value))
            {
                return value;
            }
            return null;
        }

        public static bool MatchType(AMIdentity am, string patternType)
        {
            return am.Type.Equals(patternType, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        }
    }

    public class AMIdentity
    {
        public string Type { get; set; }

        public AMIdentity(string type)
        {
            Type = type;
        }
    }

    public class IdSearchResults
    {
        private List<AMIdentity> searchResults;

        public IdSearchResults(List<AMIdentity> searchResults)
        {
            this.searchResults = searchResults;
        }

        public List<AMIdentity> GetSearchResults()
        {
            return searchResults;
        }
    }

    public class AMIdentityRepository
    {
        private SSOToken _token;
        private string _realm;

        public AMIdentityRepository(SSOToken token, string realm)
        {
            _token = token;
            _realm = realm;
        }

        public IdSearchResults SearchIdentities(IdType idType, string filter, IdSearchControl control)
        {
            var results = new List<AMIdentity>
            {
                new AMIdentity("TypeA"),
                new AMIdentity("TypeB"),
                new AMIdentity("TypeC")
            };

            if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(filter) && filter != "*")
            {
                results = results.FindAll(am => am.Type.Equals(filter, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
            }

            return new IdSearchResults(results);
        }
    }

    public class SSOToken { }

    public class IdSearchControl { }

    public enum LogLevel
    {
        INFO
    }

    public enum IdType
    {
        AGENTONLY
    }

    public interface IOutput
    {
        void Write(string message);
    }

    public class ConsoleOutput : IOutput
    {
        public void Write(string message)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(message);
        }
    }

    public static class LogWriter
    {
        public const string LOG_ACCESS = "LOG_ACCESS";
    }

    public static class IArgument
    {
        public const string REALM_NAME = "REALM_NAME";
        public const string AGENT_TYPE = "AGENT_TYPE";
        public const string FILTER = "FILTER";
    }
}

class Program
{
    static void Main()
    {
        CommandLineServiceHandler service = new CommandLineServiceHandler();
        
        // Unit Test 1
        service.HandleRequest(new RequestContext());
        // Expected Output: LdapLogin called.
        // Expected Output: No results found.
        
        // Unit Test 2
        service.HandleRequest(new RequestContext());
        // Expected Output: LdapLogin called.
        // Expected Output: Found 3 matching agents:
        // Agent Type: TypeA
        // Agent Type: TypeB
        // Agent Type: TypeC
        
        // Unit Test 3
        service.HandleRequest(new RequestContext());
        // Expected Output: LdapLogin called.
        // Expected Output: No results found.
        
        // Unit Test 4
        service.HandleRequest(new RequestContext());
        // Expected Output: LdapLogin called.
        // Expected Output: Found 1 matching agent:
        // Agent Type: TypeA
        
        // Unit Test 5
        service.HandleRequest(new RequestContext());
        // Expected Output: LdapLogin called.
        // Expected Output: No results found.
    }
}
```

```java
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.ObjectOutputStream;
import java.io.Serializable;

public class SerializeNestedObject {
    
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        try {
            NestedObject obj = new NestedObject("Example", 42, new InnerObject("Inner", 99));
            byte[] bytes = serializeToByteArray(obj);
            System.out.println("Serialized byte array length: " + bytes.length);

            // Unit tests
            assert bytes.length > 0 : "Serialized byte array should not be empty";
            assert bytes.length == 10 : "Serialized byte array length should be 10";
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }

    public static byte[] serializeToByteArray(Serializable obj) throws IOException {
        ByteArrayOutputStream byteStream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        ObjectOutputStream objectStream = new ObjectOutputStream(byteStream);
        objectStream.writeObject(obj);
        objectStream.flush();
        return byteStream.toByteArray();
    }

    static class NestedObject implements Serializable {
        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
        String name;
        int value;
        InnerObject inner;

        NestedObject(String name, int value, InnerObject inner) {
            this.name = name;
            this.value = value;
            this.inner = inner;
        }
    }

    static class InnerObject implements Serializable {
        private static final long serialVersionUID = 1L;
        String innerName;
        int innerValue;

        InnerObject(String innerName, int innerValue) {
            this.innerName = innerName;
            this.innerValue = innerValue;
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;

public interface IStorableField
{
    void Store(Stream stream, object context);
}

public class NestedObject
{
    private IStorableField _field;

    public NestedObject(IStorableField field)
    {
        _field = field;
    }

    public byte[] ToBytes()
    {
        try
        {
            using (var outStream = new MemoryStream())
            {
                _field.Store(outStream, null);
                return outStream.ToArray();
            }
        }
        catch (IOException e)
        {
            throw new InvalidOperationException(e.Message, e);
        }
    }
}

public class ExampleField : IStorableField
{
    private string _text;
    public ExampleField(string text)
    {
        _text = text;
    }

    public void Store(Stream stream, object context)
    {
        byte[] data = System.Text.Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(_text);
        stream.Write(data, 0, data.Length);
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        IStorableField field = new ExampleField("Hello, World!");
        NestedObject nestedObject = new NestedObject(field);

        byte[] byteArray = nestedObject.ToBytes();

        Console.WriteLine("Serialized bytes:");
        Console.WriteLine(BitConverter.ToString(byteArray));

        // Unit tests
        if (byteArray.Length == 0)
            throw new Exception("Serialized byte array should not be empty");
        if (byteArray.Length != 13)
            throw new Exception("Serialized byte array length should be 13");
    }
}
```

### Java Unit Tests

```java
public class StyleNameConverter {

    public enum Style {
        BOLD, ITALIC, UNDERLINE, STRIKETHROUGH
    }

    /**
     * Returns first enum constant found in a space-separated list of style names.
     */
    public static Style convertFromStyleName(String styleNames) {
        if (styleNames == null || styleNames.trim().isEmpty()) {
            return null;
        }
        String[] names = styleNames.trim().split("\\s+");
        for (String name : names) {
            try {
                return Style.valueOf(name.toUpperCase());
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                // Ignore and check next one
            }
        }
        return null;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit tests
        System.out.println(convertFromStyleName("italic underline").equals(Style.ITALIC)); // true
        System.out.println(convertFromStyleName("foo bold").equals(Style.BOLD)); // true
        System.out.println(convertFromStyleName("notAStyle") == null); // true
        System.out.println(convertFromStyleName("") == null); // true
        System.out.println(convertFromStyleName(null) == null); // true
    }
}
```

### C# Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Linq;

namespace StyleEnumExample
{
    public interface HasCssName
    {
        string GetCssName();
    }

    public static class Style
    {
        // Sample enum implementing HasCssName
        public enum SampleStyle : HasCssName
        {
            Bold,
            Italic,
            Underline
        }

        public static class SampleStyleExtensions
        {
            public static string GetCssName(this SampleStyle style)
            {
                switch (style)
                {
                    case SampleStyle.Bold:
                        return "bold";
                    case SampleStyle.Italic:
                        return "italic";
                    case SampleStyle.Underline:
                        return "underline";
                    default:
                        return null;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    public static class StyleHelper
    {
        public static bool ContainsStyle(string styleNames, string styleName)
        {
            if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(styleNames) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(styleName))
                return false;

            var styles = styleNames.Split(new[] { ' ' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
            return styles.Any(s => s.Equals(styleName, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase));
        }
    }

    public static class StyleParser
    {
        public static E FromStyleName<E>(string styleName, Type enumClass, E defaultValue) where E : Enum, HasCssName
        {
            if (styleName == null || enumClass == null)
            {
                return defaultValue;
            }

            foreach (E constant in Enum.GetValues(enumClass).Cast<E>())
            {
                var anEnum = constant as HasCssName;
                string cssClass = anEnum?.GetCssName();
                if (cssClass != null && StyleHelper.ContainsStyle(styleName, cssClass))
                {
                    return constant;
                }
            }
            return defaultValue;
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            // Unit tests
            Console.WriteLine(StyleParser.FromStyleName<Style.SampleStyle>("italic underline", typeof(Style.SampleStyle), default(Style.SampleStyle)) == Style.SampleStyle.Italic); // true
            Console.WriteLine(StyleParser.FromStyleName<Style.SampleStyle>("foo bold", typeof(Style.SampleStyle), default(Style.SampleStyle)) == Style.SampleStyle.Bold); // true
            Console.WriteLine(StyleParser.FromStyleName<Style.SampleStyle>("notAStyle", typeof(Style.SampleStyle), default(Style.SampleStyle)) == null); // true
            Console.WriteLine(StyleParser.FromStyleName<Style.SampleStyle>("", typeof(Style.SampleStyle), default(Style.SampleStyle)) == null); // true
            Console.WriteLine(StyleParser.FromStyleName<Style.SampleStyle>(null, typeof(Style.SampleStyle), default(Style.SampleStyle)) == null); // true
        }
    }
}
```

```java
public class ScempEgNextAvcrPau {
    // Method to get next number by "a"
    public static int getNextNumberByA(int current) {
        // "skip one video frame" is metaphorically represented as +2 (skip one)
        return current + 2;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        int currentFrame = 1;
        int nextFrame = getNextNumberByA(currentFrame);
        System.out.println("Current frame: " + currentFrame);
        System.out.println("Next frame (skipping one): " + nextFrame);

        // Unit tests
        assert getNextNumberByA(0) == 2 : "Test 1 failed";
        assert getNextNumberByA(5) == 7 : "Test 2 failed";
        assert getNextNumberByA(-3) == -1 : "Test 3 failed";
        assert getNextNumberByA(100) == 102 : "Test 4 failed";
        assert getNextNumberByA(-1) == 1 : "Test 5 failed";

        System.out.println("All tests passed!");
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;

namespace SceMpegExample
{
    // Simple logger class
    public class Logger
    {
        public bool IsDebugEnabled { get; set; } = true;

        public void Warn(string message)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("[WARN] " + message);
        }

        public void Debug(string message)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("[DEBUG] " + message);
        }
    }

    public class MpegHandler
    {
        private Dictionary<int, object> streamMap = new Dictionary<int, object>();
        private Logger log = new Logger();
        private int videoFrameCount = 0;
        private bool startedMpeg = false;

        // Dummy HleMpegGetAvcAu method
        private int HleMpegGetAvcAu(object obj)
        {
            // Just return a sample result for demonstration purposes
            // Simulate this returns a valid frame index or -1
            Random rnd = new Random();
            return rnd.Next(0, 2) == 0 ? -1 : rnd.Next(100, 200);
        }

        // scempegnextavcrpau - skip one video frame
        public int SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(int mpeg, int streamUid)
        {
            if (!streamMap.ContainsKey(streamUid))
            {
                log.Warn(string.Format("{0}", streamUid));
                return -1; // Replace _NUM with -1
            }
            int result = HleMpegGetAvcAu(null);
            if (result != -1) // Replace _NUM with -1
            {
                if (log.IsDebugEnabled)
                {
                    log.Debug(string.Format("{0}", result));
                }
                return result;
            }
            videoFrameCount++;
            startedMpeg = true; // Replace _BOOL with true
            return -1; // Replace _NUM with -1
        }

        // For demonstration: methods to interact with the class
        public void AddStream(int streamUid)
        {
            if (!streamMap.ContainsKey(streamUid))
            {
                streamMap[streamUid] = new object();
            }
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main()
        {
            MpegHandler handler = new MpegHandler();

            // Try with an unknown streamUid
            Console.WriteLine("Calling with unknown streamUid (42):");
            int res1 = handler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 42);
            Console.WriteLine("Result: " + res1);

            // Add streamUid and try again
            handler.AddStream(42);

            Console.WriteLine("\nCalling with known streamUid (42):");
            int res2 = handler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 42);
            Console.WriteLine("Result: " + res2);

            // Unit tests
            try
            {
                MpegHandler mpegHandler = new MpegHandler();
                assert(mpegHandler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 1) == -1) : "Test 1 failed";
                assert(mpegHandler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 2) != -1) : "Test 2 failed";
                mpegHandler.AddStream(3);
                assert(mpegHandler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 3) == -1 || mpegHandler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 3) > 99 && mpegHandler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, 3) < 200) : "Test 3 failed";
                assert(mpegHandler.SceMpegNextAvcRpAu(0, -1) == -1) : "Test 4 failed";
                Console.WriteLine("All tests passed!");
            }
            catch (AssertionException ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine(ex.Message);
            }
        }

        public static void assert(bool condition, string message)
        {
            if (!condition)
            {
                throw new AssertionException(message);
            }
        }
    }

    public class AssertionException : Exception
    {
        public AssertionException(string message) : base(message) { }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public class ExportMaskMapper {

    // Mock database of export masks
    private static final Map<String, ExportMask> EXPORT_MASK_DB = new HashMap<>();

    static {
        // Initialize some mock data
        EXPORT_MASK_DB.put("uri-1", new ExportMask("uri-1", "ExportMaskOne"));
        EXPORT_MASK_DB.put("uri-2", new ExportMask("uri-2", "ExportMaskTwo"));
        EXPORT_MASK_DB.put("uri-3", new ExportMask("uri-3", "ExportMaskThree"));
    }

    /**
     * Given a set of exportMaskUris, return a map of exportMaskUri to ExportMask object.
     */
    public static Map<String, ExportMask> getExportMasksForUris(Set<String> exportMaskUris) {
        Map<String, ExportMask> result = new HashMap<>();
        for (String uri : exportMaskUris) {
            ExportMask mask = EXPORT_MASK_DB.get(uri);
            if (mask != null) {
                result.put(uri, mask);
            }
        }
        return result;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Set<String> uris = Set.of("uri-1", "uri-3", "uri-nonexistent");
        Map<String, ExportMask> maskMap = getExportMasksForUris(uris);
        System.out.println("ExportMask map:");
        for (Map.Entry<String, ExportMask> entry : maskMap.entrySet()) {
            System.out.println(entry.getKey() + " -> " + entry.getValue());
        }
    }

    // Unit Tests
    public static void runTests() {
        Set<String> uris1 = Set.of("uri-1", "uri-3");
        Map<String, ExportMask> maskMap1 = getExportMasksForUris(uris1);
        System.out.println("Test 1: " + (maskMap1.containsKey("uri-1") && maskMap1.get("uri-1").getName().equals("ExportMaskOne")
                && maskMap1.containsKey("uri-3") && maskMap1.get("uri-3").getName().equals("ExportMaskThree")));

        Set<String> uris2 = Set.of("uri-nonexistent");
        Map<String, ExportMask> maskMap2 = getExportMasksForUris(uris2);
        System.out.println("Test 2: " + !maskMap2.containsKey("uri-nonexistent"));

        Set<String> uris3 = Set.of();
        Map<String, ExportMask> maskMap3 = getExportMasksForUris(uris3);
        System.out.println("Test 3: " + maskMap3.isEmpty());

        Set<String> uris4 = Set.of("uri-1", "uri-2", "uri-3");
        Map<String, ExportMask> maskMap4 = getExportMasksForUris(uris4);
        System.out.println("Test 4: " + (maskMap4.containsKey("uri-1") && maskMap4.get("uri-1").getName().equals("ExportMaskOne")
                && maskMap4.containsKey("uri-2") && maskMap4.get("uri-2").getName().equals("ExportMaskTwo")
                && maskMap4.containsKey("uri-3") && maskMap4.get("uri-3").getName().equals("ExportMaskThree")));

        Set<String> uris5 = Set.of("uri-1", "uri-1");
        Map<String, ExportMask> maskMap5 = getExportMasksForUris(uris5);
        System.out.println("Test 5: " + (maskMap5.containsKey("uri-1") && maskMap5.get("uri-1").getName().equals("ExportMaskOne")));
    }
}
```

### C# Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace ExportMaskExample
{
    // Stub definition for ExportMask
    public class ExportMask
    {
        private Uri _id;

        public ExportMask(Uri id)
        {
            _id = id;
        }

        public Uri GetId()
        {
            return _id;
        }
    }

    // Stub definition for DbClient with an iterator method
    public class DbClient
    {
        private Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask> _maskStorage;

        public DbClient(Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask> maskStorage)
        {
            _maskStorage = maskStorage;
        }

        public IEnumerator<ExportMask> QueryIterativeObjects<T>(HashSet<Uri> uris, bool whatever)
        {
            foreach (var uri in uris)
            {
                if (_maskStorage.TryGetValue(uri, out ExportMask mask))
                    yield return mask;
            }
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        private DbClient dbClient;

        public Program(DbClient dbClient)
        {
            this.dbClient = dbClient;
        }

        // given a set of exportmask uris, return a map of exportmask uri to exportmask object
        private Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask> CreateExportMaskMap(HashSet<Uri> placedMasks)
        {
            Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask> exportMaskMap = new Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask>();
            var exportMaskIterator = dbClient.QueryIterativeObjects<ExportMask>(placedMasks, true);
            while (exportMaskIterator.MoveNext())
            {
                ExportMask exportMask = exportMaskIterator.Current;
                exportMaskMap[exportMask.GetId()] = exportMask;
            }
            return exportMaskMap;
        }

        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            // Create some ExportMasks
            Uri uri1 = new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1");
            Uri uri2 = new Uri("http://example.com/mask/2");
            ExportMask mask1 = new ExportMask(uri1);
            ExportMask mask2 = new ExportMask(uri2);

            // Individually populate dummy database
            var maskStorage = new Dictionary<Uri, ExportMask>
            {
                { uri1, mask1 },
                { uri2, mask2 }
            };

            DbClient dbClient = new DbClient(maskStorage);
            Program program = new Program(dbClient);

            // Create set of uris to look up
            var urisToFind = new HashSet<Uri> { uri1, uri2 };

            var result = program.CreateExportMaskMap(urisToFind);

            // Print results
            foreach (var kvp in result)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"URI: {kvp.Key} -> ExportMask ID: {kvp.Value.GetId()}");
            }

            // Unit Tests
            RunTests();
        }

        public static void RunTests()
        {
            HashSet<Uri> uris1 = new HashSet<Uri> { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1"), new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3") };
            var maskMap1 = CreateExportMaskMap(uris1);
            Console.WriteLine("Test 1: " + (maskMap1.ContainsKey(uri1) && maskMap1[uri1].GetId().Equals(uri1)
                    && maskMap1.ContainsKey(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3")) && maskMap1[new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3")].GetId().Equals(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3"))));

            HashSet<Uri> uris2 = new HashSet<Uri> { new Uri("http://example.com/nonexistent") };
            var maskMap2 = CreateExportMaskMap(uris2);
            Console.WriteLine("Test 2: " + !maskMap2.ContainsKey(new Uri("http://example.com/nonexistent")));

            HashSet<Uri> uris3 = new HashSet<Uri>();
            var maskMap3 = CreateExportMaskMap(uris3);
            Console.WriteLine("Test 3: " + maskMap3.Count == 0);

            HashSet<Uri> uris4 = new HashSet<Uri> { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1"), new Uri("http://example.com/mask/2"), new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3") };
            var maskMap4 = CreateExportMaskMap(uris4);
            Console.WriteLine("Test 4: " + (maskMap4.ContainsKey(uri1) && maskMap4[uri1].GetId().Equals(uri1)
                    && maskMap4.ContainsKey(uri2) && maskMap4[uri2].GetId().Equals(uri2)
                    && maskMap4.ContainsKey(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3")) && maskMap4[new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3")].GetId().Equals(new Uri("http://example.com/mask/3"))));

            HashSet<Uri> uris5 = new HashSet<Uri> { new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1"), new Uri("http://example.com/mask/1") };
            var maskMap5 = CreateExportMaskMap(uris5);
            Console.WriteLine("Test 5: " + (maskMap5.ContainsKey(uri1) && maskMap5[uri1].GetId().Equals(uri1)));
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Threading;

public static class DataUtils
{
    // Copies from source to destination, leaving a gap at the specified index.
    // For simplicity, we copy [0,index) then [index,length) to [index+1,length+1)
    public static void CopyWithGap<K>(K[] source, K[] dest, int length, int gapIndex)
    {
        Array.Copy(source, 0, dest, 0, gapIndex);
        Array.Copy(source, gapIndex, dest, gapIndex + 1, length - gapIndex);
    }
}

public class ImmutableArray2<K>
{
    internal readonly K[] array;
    internal readonly int length;
    internal readonly bool extendable;

    // Simulate extendibility with an AtomicBoolean replacement
    internal AtomicBool? canExtend;

    public const int _NUM = 1;
    public const bool _BOOL = true;

    public ImmutableArray2(K[] array, int length, bool extendable)
    {
        this.array = array;
        this.length = length;
        this.extendable = extendable;
        this.canExtend = extendable ? new AtomicBool(false) : null;
    }

    // Insert an entry at this index.
    public ImmutableArray2<K> Insert(int index, K obj)
    {
        int len = length + _NUM;
        int newLen = len;
        bool extendable;

        if (index == len - _NUM)
        {
            var x = canExtend;
            if (x != null)
            {
                canExtend = null;
                if (array.Length > index && x.GetAndSet(_BOOL))
                {
                    array[index] = obj;
                    return new ImmutableArray2<K>(array, len, _BOOL);
                }
            }
            extendable = _BOOL;
            newLen = len + _NUM;
        }
        else
        {
            extendable = _BOOL;
        }
        K[] a2 = new K[newLen];
        DataUtils.CopyWithGap(array, a2, length, index);
        a2[index] = obj;
        return new ImmutableArray2<K>(a2, len, extendable);
    }

    public override string ToString()
    {
        return $"[{string.Join(", ", array.AsSpan(0, length))}]";
    }
}

public class AtomicBool
{
    private int value;

    public AtomicBool(bool initialValue)
    {
        value = initialValue ? 1 : 0;
    }

    // Returns old value, sets new value
    public bool GetAndSet(bool newValue)
    {
        int newInt = newValue ? 1 : 0;
        int old = Interlocked.Exchange(ref value, newInt);
        return old != 0;
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        int[] arr = new int[] { 1, 2, 3 };
        var immutable = new ImmutableArray2<int>((int[])arr.Clone(), arr.Length, true);
        Console.WriteLine("Original: " + immutable);

        var inserted = immutable.Insert(1, 99);
        Console.WriteLine("After Insert at index 1: " + inserted);

        var inserted2 = inserted.Insert(4, 88); // new entry at the end (simulate extended length)
        Console.WriteLine("After Insert at index 4: " + inserted2);

        // Unit tests
        testInsert(immutable, new int[]{5, 6}, 1);
        testInsert(immutable, new int[]{7, 8, 9}, immutable.length);
        try {
            immutable.Insert(-1, 10); // Should throw IndexOutOfRangeException
            Console.WriteLine("Test failed: Expected IndexOutOfRangeException");
        } catch (IndexOutOfRangeException e) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test passed: Received expected IndexOutOfRangeException");
        }
    }

    public static void testInsert<T>(ImmutableArray2<T> array, T[] element, int index) {
        var result = array.Insert(index, element[0]);
        Console.WriteLine("After Insert at index " + index + ": " + result);
    }
}
```
