```java
import java.awt.Graphics;
import java.awt.Image;
import java.awt.print.PageFormat;
import java.awt.print.Printable;
import java.awt.print.PrinterException;
import java.awt.print.PrinterJob;
import javax.swing.ImageIcon;

public class ImagePrintDemo implements Printable {

    private Image image;

    public ImagePrintDemo(Image image) {
        this.image = image;
    }

    @Override
    public int print(Graphics g, PageFormat pf, int pageIndex) throws PrinterException {
        if (pageIndex > 0) {
            return NO_SUCH_PAGE;
        }

        int imgWidth = image.getWidth(null);
        int imgHeight = image.getHeight(null);

        double paperWidth = pf.getImageableWidth();
        double paperHeight = pf.getImageableHeight();
        double paperX = pf.getImageableX();
        double paperY = pf.getImageableY();

        int x = (int) (paperX + paperWidth - imgWidth);
        int y = (int) paperY;

        g.drawImage(image, x, y, null);

        return PAGE_EXISTS;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Image image = new ImageIcon("example.jpg").getImage(); // Replace with your image path
        PrinterJob job = PrinterJob.getPrinterJob();
        job.setPrintable(new ImagePrintDemo(image));

        boolean doPrint = job.printDialog();
        if (doPrint) {
            try {
                job.print();
            } catch (PrinterException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }

        // Unit tests
        ImagePrintDemo demo1 = new ImagePrintDemo(null);
        try {
            demo1.print(null, null, 0); // Should throw NullPointerException
        } catch (NullPointerException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 1 Passed");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 1 Failed");
        }

        Image image2 = new ImageIcon("example.jpg").getImage();
        ImagePrintDemo demo2 = new ImagePrintDemo(image2);
        try {
            demo2.print(null, null, 0); // Should throw NullPointerException
        } catch (NullPointerException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 2 Failed");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 2 Passed");
        }

        PageFormat pf = new PageFormat();
        try {
            demo1.print(null, pf, 0); // Should throw NullPointerException
        } catch (NullPointerException e) {
            System.out.println("Test 3 Passed");
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 3 Failed");
        }

        Image image3 = new ImageIcon("example.jpg").getImage();
        ImagePrintDemo demo3 = new ImagePrintDemo(image3);
        try {
            demo2.print(null, pf, 1); // Should return NO_SUCH_PAGE
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 4 Failed");
        }
        if (demo3.print(null, pf, 0) == PAGE_EXISTS) {
            System.out.println("Test 4 Passed");
        }

        Image image4 = new ImageIcon("example.jpg").getImage();
        ImagePrintDemo demo4 = new ImagePrintDemo(image4);
        try {
            demo4.print(new Graphics() { // Mock Graphics
                @Override
                public void drawImage(Image img, int x, int y, ImageObserver observer) {}
                @Override
                public void dispose() {}
                @Override
                public boolean drawImage(Image img, int x, int y, int width, int height, ImageObserver observer) {
                    return true;
                }
                @Override
                public boolean drawImage(Image img, double x, double y, double width, double height, ImageObserver observer) {
                    return true;
                }
            }, pf, 0); // Should not throw any exceptions
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("Test 5 Failed");
        }
        if (demo4.print(new Graphics() { // Mock Graphics
            @Override
            public void drawImage(Image img, int x, int y, ImageObserver observer) {}
            @Override
            public void dispose() {}
            @Override
            public boolean drawImage(Image img, int x, int y, int width, int height, ImageObserver observer) {
                return true;
            }
            @Override
            public boolean drawImage(Image img, double x, double y, double width, double height, ImageObserver observer) {
                return true;
            }
        }, pf, 0) == PAGE_EXISTS) {
            System.out.println("Test 5 Passed");
        }
    }
```

```java
        DocumentCache docCache = new DocumentCache();

        // Test case 1: Putting a root node
        TreeNode root1 = new TreeNode(1);
        String docId1 = "doc1";
        docCache.putRootNode(docId1, root1);
        TreeNode retrievedRoot1 = docCache.getRootNode(docId1);
        System.out.println("Test Case 1 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot1 != null && retrievedRoot1.val == 1 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

        // Test case 2: Getting a non-existent node
        TreeNode retrievedRoot2 = docCache.getRootNode("nonExistentDocId");
        System.out.println("Test Case 2 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot2 == null ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

        // Test case 3: Replacing an existing node
        TreeNode root3 = new TreeNode(3);
        docCache.putRootNode(docId1, root3);
        TreeNode retrievedRoot3 = docCache.getRootNode(docId1);
        System.out.println("Test Case 3 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot3 != null && retrievedRoot3.val == 3 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

        // Test case 4: Putting multiple nodes
        String docId2 = "doc2";
        TreeNode root2 = new TreeNode(2);
        docCache.putRootNode(docId2, root2);
        TreeNode retrievedRoot2_1 = docCache.getRootNode(docId2);
        System.out.println("Test Case 4 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot2_1 != null && retrievedRoot2_1.val == 2 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

        // Test case 5: Getting a node after replacing
        TreeNode root4 = new TreeNode(4);
        docCache.putRootNode(docId1, root4);
        TreeNode retrievedRoot4 = docCache.getRootNode(docId1);
        System.out.println("Test Case 5 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot4 != null && retrievedRoot4.val == 4 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
```

### C# Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

namespace DocumentCacheExample
{
    public class Source
    {
        private string systemId;

        public Source(string systemId)
        {
            this.systemId = systemId;
        }

        public string GetSystemId()
        {
            return systemId;
        }
    }

    public class SourceTree
    {
        public int NodeId { get; }
        public string SystemId { get; }

        public SourceTree(int nodeId, string systemId)
        {
            NodeId = nodeId;
            SystemId = systemId;
        }
    }

    public static class DTM
    {
        public const int NULL = -1;
    }

    public class DocumentCache
    {
        private List<SourceTree> m_sourceTree = new List<SourceTree>();
        private Dictionary<string, int> cache = new Dictionary<string, int>();

        private string _STR = "Source Tree Exception: ";

        public void PutDocumentInCache(int n, Source source)
        {
            int cachedNode = GetNode(source);
            if (DTM.NULL != cachedNode)
            {
                if (cachedNode != n)
                {
                    throw new InvalidOperationException(_STR + _STR + source.GetSystemId());
                }
                return;
            }
            if (source.GetSystemId() != null)
            {
                m_sourceTree.Add(new SourceTree(n, source.GetSystemId()));
                cache[source.GetSystemId()] = n;
            }
        }

        // Simulates finding a node from the source using its system id
        private int GetNode(Source source)
        {
            string systemId = source.GetSystemId();
            if (systemId != null && cache.ContainsKey(systemId))
            {
                return cache[systemId];
            }
            return DTM.NULL;
        }

        // For demonstration
        public void PrintSourceTrees()
        {
            foreach (var tree in m_sourceTree)
            {
                Console.WriteLine($"NodeId: {tree.NodeId}, SystemId: {tree.SystemId}");
            }
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            DocumentCache cache = new DocumentCache();
            Source source1 = new Source("doc1.xml");
            Source source2 = new Source("doc2.xml");

            // Test case 1: Putting a root node
            cache.PutDocumentInCache(1, source1);
            int retrievedRoot1 = cache.GetNode(source1);
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 1 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot1 == 1 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

            // Test case 2: Getting a non-existent node
            int retrievedRoot2 = cache.GetNode(new Source("nonExistentDocId"));
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 2 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot2 == DTM.NULL ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

            // Test case 3: Replacing an existing node
            cache.PutDocumentInCache(1, source1);
            int retrievedRoot3 = cache.GetNode(source1);
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 3 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot3 == 1 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

            // Test case 4: Putting multiple nodes
            cache.PutDocumentInCache(2, source2);
            int retrievedRoot2_1 = cache.GetNode(source2);
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 4 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot2_1 == 2 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));

            // Test case 5: Getting a node after replacing
            cache.PutDocumentInCache(1, source1);
            int retrievedRoot4 = cache.GetNode(source1);
            Console.WriteLine("Test Case 5 - Result: " + (retrievedRoot4 == 1 ? "Pass" : "Fail"));
        }
    }
}
```

```java
public class ProgressUpdate {

    private String message;
    private int progress; // 0 to 100

    // Constructor
    public ProgressUpdate(String message, int progress) {
        this.message = message;
        this.progress = progress;
    }

    // Getter for message
    public String getMessage() {
        return message;
    }

    // Getter for progress
    public int getProgress() {
        return progress;
    }

    // Setter for message
    public void setMessage(String message) {
        this.message = message;
    }

    // Setter for progress
    public void setProgress(int progress) {
        this.progress = progress;
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        return "ProgressUpdate{" +
                "message='" + message + '\'' +
                ", progress=" + progress +
                '}';
    }

    // For demo purposes: simulate communication of progress updates
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Simulate a plugin tool sending progress updates
        ProgressUpdate update1 = new ProgressUpdate("Initializing...", 0);
        ProgressUpdate update2 = new ProgressUpdate("Processing data...", 50);
        ProgressUpdate update3 = new ProgressUpdate("Finalizing...", 100);

        // Simulate main whitebox user interface receiving updates
        displayProgress(update1);
        displayProgress(update2);
        displayProgress(update3);

        // Unit tests
        assert "Initializing... (0%)".equals(displayProgressToString(update1)) : "Test 1 Failed";
        assert "Processing data... (50%)".equals(displayProgressToString(update2)) : "Test 2 Failed";
        assert "Finalizing... (100%)".equals(displayProgressToString(update3)) : "Test 3 Failed";

        System.out.println("All Java unit tests passed.");
    }

    // Simulated UI display
    public static String displayProgressToString(ProgressUpdate update) {
        return "Received progress update: " + update.getMessage() + " (" + update.getProgress() + "%)";
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;

namespace PluginProgressUpdate
{
    public class ProgressHandler
    {
        private IHost myHost = null;
        private int previousProgress = -1;
        private string previousProgressLabel = "";

        public ProgressHandler(IHost host)
        {
            myHost = host;
        }

        public void UpdateProgress(string progressLabel, int progress)
        {
            if (myHost != null && (progress != previousProgress || !progressLabel.Equals(previousProgressLabel)))
            {
                myHost.UpdateProgress(progressLabel, progress);
            }
            previousProgress = progress;
            previousProgressLabel = progressLabel;
        }
    }

    public interface IHost
    {
        void UpdateProgress(string progressLabel, int progress);
    }

    public class MainHost : IHost
    {
        public void UpdateProgress(string progressLabel, int progress)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"{progressLabel}: {progress}%");
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            IHost host = new MainHost();
            ProgressHandler handler = new ProgressHandler(host);

            handler.UpdateProgress("Loading Data", 10);
            handler.UpdateProgress("Loading Data", 50);
            handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70);
            handler.UpdateProgress("Processing", 70); // No update, same label and progress
            handler.UpdateProgress("Complete", 100);

            // Unit tests
            assert "Loading Data: 10%" == MainHost.LoggedOutput[0] : "Test 1 Failed";
            assert "Loading Data: 50%" == MainHost.LoggedOutput[1] : "Test 2 Failed";
            assert "Processing: 70%" == MainHost.LoggedOutput[2] : "Test 3 Failed";
            assert "Complete: 100%" == MainHost.LoggedOutput[4] : "Test 5 Failed";

            Console.WriteLine("All C# unit tests passed.");
        }
    }

    public static class MainHost
    {
        public static List<string> LoggedOutput { get; private set; } = new List<string>();

        public void UpdateProgress(string progressLabel, int progress)
        {
            LoggedOutput.Add($"{progressLabel}: {progress}%");
            Console.WriteLine($"{progressLabel}: {progress}%");
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.io.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

public class ResourceCopyExample {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        String inputFilePath = "input.txt";
        String outputFilePath = "output.txt";

        try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(inputFilePath);
             OutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(outputFilePath)) {
            byte[] buffer = new byte[1024];
            int bytesRead;
            while ((bytesRead = in.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                out.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead);
            }
            System.out.println("Resource copied successfully.");
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // Unit Tests
        @Test
        public void testCopy() throws IOException {
            String input = "test.txt";
            String output = "output_test.txt";
            try (InputStream in = new ByteArrayInputStream(input.getBytes());
                 OutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(output)) {
                CopyUtils.copy(in, out);
                assertTrue(new File(output).exists());
            } finally {
                new File(output).delete();
            }
        }

        @Test
        public void testCopyLargeFile() throws IOException {
            String input = "large_test.txt";
            String output = "output_large_test.txt";
            try (InputStream in = new BufferedInputStream(new FileInputStream(input));
                 OutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(output)) {
                CopyUtils.copy(in, out);
                assertTrue(new File(output).exists());
            } finally {
                new File(output).delete();
            }
        }

        @Test
        public void testCopyWithException() throws IOException {
            String input = "non_existent.txt";
            try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(input);
                 OutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream()) {
                CopyUtils.copy(in, out);
                fail("Expected exception not thrown");
            } catch (FileNotFoundException e) {
                // Expected
            }
        }

        @Test
        public void testCopyWithNullOutputStream() throws IOException {
            String input = "test.txt";
            try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(input)) {
                CopyUtils.copy(in, null);
                fail("Expected exception not thrown");
            } catch (NullPointerException e) {
                // Expected
            }
        }

        @Test
        public void testCopyWithCloseInputStream() throws IOException {
            String input = "test.txt";
            String output = "output_test_close.txt";
            try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(input);
                 OutputStream out = new FileOutputStream(output)) {
                CopyUtils.copy(in, out, true);
                assertTrue(new File(output).exists());
                in.close();
            } finally {
                new File(output).delete();
            }
        }
    }

    private static class CopyUtils {
        public static void copy(InputStream inputStream, OutputStream outputStream) throws IOException {
            byte[] buffer = new byte[1024];
            int bytesRead;
            while ((bytesRead = inputStream.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                outputStream.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead);
            }
        }

        public static void copy(InputStream inputStream, OutputStream outputStream, boolean closeInputStream) throws IOException {
            try (OutputStream os = outputStream) {
                byte[] buffer = new byte[1024];
                int bytesRead;
                while ((bytesRead = inputStream.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                    os.write(buffer, 0, bytesRead);
                }
            } finally {
                if (closeInputStream && inputStream != null) {
                    inputStream.close();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;
using Xunit;

public class ResourceCopyExample
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        string inputFilePath = "input.txt";
        string outputFilePath = "output.txt";

        using (FileStream inputStream = new FileStream(inputFilePath, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read))
        {
            Resource outputResource = new Resource(outputFilePath);
            Copier.Copy(inputStream, outputResource, true);
        }
        Console.WriteLine("Copy completed.");

        // Unit Tests
        [Fact]
        public void TestCopy()
        {
            string input = "test.txt";
            string output = "output_test.txt";
            using (FileStream inputStream = new FileStream(input, FileMode.Open))
            {
                using (FileStream outputStream = new FileStream(output, FileMode.Create))
                {
                    Copier.Copy(inputStream, outputStream);
                    Assert.True(File.Exists(output));
                }
                File.Delete(output);
            }
        }

        [Fact]
        public void TestCopyLargeFile()
        {
            string input = "large_test.txt";
            string output = "output_large_test.txt";
            using (FileStream inputStream = new FileStream(input, FileMode.Open))
            {
                using (FileStream outputStream = new FileStream(output, FileMode.Create))
                {
                    Copier.Copy(inputStream, outputStream);
                    Assert.True(File.Exists(output));
                }
                File.Delete(output);
            }
        }

        [Fact]
        public void TestCopyWithException()
        {
            string input = "non_existent.txt";
            try
            {
                using (FileStream inputStream = new FileStream(input, FileMode.Open))
                {
                    using (FileStream outputStream = new MemoryStream())
                    {
                        Copier.Copy(inputStream, outputStream);
                    }
                }
                Assert.Fail("Expected exception not thrown");
            }
            catch (FileNotFoundException)
            {
                // Expected
            }
        }

        [Fact]
        public void TestCopyWithNullOutputStream()
        {
            string input = "test.txt";
            try
            {
                using (FileStream inputStream = new FileStream(input, FileMode.Open))
                {
                    Copier.Copy(inputStream, null);
                }
                Assert.Fail("Expected exception not thrown");
            }
            catch (ArgumentNullException)
            {
                // Expected
            }
        }

        [Fact]
        public void TestCopyWithCloseInputStream()
        {
            string input = "test.txt";
            string output = "output_test_close.txt";
            using (FileStream inputStream = new FileStream(input, FileMode.Open))
            {
                using (FileStream outputStream = new FileStream(output, FileMode.Create))
                {
                    Copier.Copy(inputStream, outputStream, true);
                    Assert.True(File.Exists(output));
                    inputStream.Close();
                }
                File.Delete(output);
            }
        }
    }

    public class Resource
    {
        private readonly string _path;

        public Resource(string path)
        {
            _path = path;
        }

        public Stream GetOutputStream()
        {
            return new FileStream(_path, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.Write);
        }
    }

    public static class Copier
    {
        private const bool _BOOL = true;

        public static void Copy(Stream inputStream, Resource outputResource, bool closeInputStream)
        {
            using (Stream outputStream = ToBufferedOutputStream(outputResource.GetOutputStream()))
            {
                Copy(inputStream, outputStream, closeInputStream, _BOOL);
            }
        }

        public static Stream ToBufferedOutputStream(Stream outputStream)
        {
            return new BufferedStream(outputStream, 8192);
        }

        public static void Copy(Stream inputStream, Stream outputStream, bool closeInputStream, bool closeOutputStream)
        {
            try
            {
                byte[] buffer = new byte[8192];
                int read;
                while ((read = inputStream.Read(buffer, 0, buffer.Length)) > 0)
                {
                    outputStream.Write(buffer, 0, read);
                }
            }
            finally
            {
                if (closeInputStream && inputStream != null)
                {
                    inputStream.Close();
                }
                if (closeOutputStream && outputStream != null)
                {
                    outputStream.Close();
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

### Java Code with Unit Tests

```java
import java.io.IOException;

public class FadeAudioWithSox {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Example usage:
        String inputPath = "input.wav";
        String outputPath = "output.wav";
        String type = "t";            // type 't' for linear fade (or 'q' for quadratic)
        String fadeInLength = "3";    // 3 seconds fade in
        String stopTime = "10";       // total audio duration (10 seconds)
        String fadeOutLength = "3";   // 3 seconds fade out

        String[] command = {
            "sox",
            inputPath,
            outputPath,
            "fade",
            type,
            fadeInLength,
            stopTime,
            fadeOutLength
        };

        try {
            Process process = new ProcessBuilder(command)
                .inheritIO()
                .start();

            int exitCode = process.waitFor();
            if (exitCode == 0) {
                System.out.println("Audio faded successfully.");
            } else {
                System.out.println("Error: Sox process exited with code " + exitCode);
            }
        } catch (IOException | InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // Unit Tests
        testFadeAudioWithSox("input.wav", "output.wav", "t", "3", "10", "3");
    }

    public static void testFadeAudioWithSox(String inputPath, String outputPath, String type, String fadeInLength, String stopTime, String fadeOutLength) {
        try {
            ProcessBuilder processBuilder = new ProcessBuilder(
                "sox",
                inputPath,
                outputPath,
                "fade",
                type,
                fadeInLength,
                stopTime,
                fadeOutLength
            );
            Process process = processBuilder.start();
            int exitCode = process.waitFor();
            assert exitCode == 0 : "Audio fading failed.";
        } catch (IOException | InterruptedException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.IO;

namespace AudioFadeApp
{
    class Program
    {
        // Stand-ins for obfuscated constants
        private const string _STR = "FADE";
        private const int _NUM = -1;
        private static string soxBin = "sox";

        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            // Example Usage
            string inputPath = "input.wav";
            string type = "FADE"; // Must be in curves
            double fadeInLength = 1.5;
            double stopTime = 5.0;
            double fadeOutLength = 2.0;

            Program p = new Program();
            string result = p.FadeAudio(inputPath, type, fadeInLength, stopTime, fadeOutLength);

            Console.WriteLine("Output file: " + result);
            
            // Unit Tests
            testFadeAudio("input.wav", "output.wav", "FADE", 1.5, 5.0, 2.0);
        }

        public string FadeAudio(string path, string type, double fadeInLength, double stopTime, double fadeOutLength)
        {
            var curves = new List<string> { _STR, _STR, _STR, _STR, _STR };
            if (!curves.Contains(type))
            {
                throw new InvalidOperationException(_STR + type);
            }
            var file = new FileInfo(path);
            string outFile = file.FullName + _STR;
            var cmd = new List<string> { soxBin, path, outFile, _STR, type, fadeInLength.ToString() };
            if (stopTime != _NUM)
                cmd.Add(stopTime.ToString());
            if (fadeOutLength != _NUM)
                cmd.Add(fadeOutLength.ToString());
            try
            {
                int rc = ExecSox(cmd, Callback);
                if (rc != _NUM)
                {
                    outFile = null;
                }
            }
            catch (IOException e)
            {
                PrintStackTrace(e);
            }
            catch (Exception e)
            {
                PrintStackTrace(e);
            }
            return outFile;
        }

        // Dummy implementation of callback and ExecSox
        private static void Callback(string data)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Callback: " + data);
        }

        private static int ExecSox(List<string> cmd, Action<string> callback)
        {
            // For demonstration, just print command and call callback
            Console.WriteLine("Executing: " + string.Join(" ", cmd));
            callback("Processing done.");
            return 0; // success
        }

        private static void PrintStackTrace(Exception e)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("Exception caught: " + e.ToString());
        }
        
        public static void testFadeAudio(string inputPath, string outputPath, string type, double fadeInLength, double stopTime, double fadeOutLength) {
            try {
                var p = new Program();
                string result = p.FadeAudio(inputPath, type, fadeInLength, stopTime, fadeOutLength);
                Console.WriteLine("Output file: " + result);
                assert !string.IsNullOrEmpty(result) : "Audio fading failed.";
            } catch (Exception e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import javax.swing.*;
import java.awt.*;

public class CanvasOutlineDemo extends JPanel {

    @Override
    protected void paintComponent(Graphics g) {
        super.paintComponent(g);
        // Draw the outline of the canvas (the panel itself)
        g.setColor(Color.BLACK);
        g.drawRect(0, 0, getWidth()-1, getHeight()-1);
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        JFrame frame = new JFrame("Canvas Outline Demo");
        CanvasOutlineDemo panel = new CanvasOutlineDemo() {
            @Override
            protected void paintComponent(Graphics g) {
                super.paintComponent(g);
                // Mock the Graphics object to check if drawRect was called
                g.setColor(Color.BLACK);
                ((Graphics2D)g).setStroke(new BasicStroke(10));
                g.drawRect(0, 0, getWidth()-1, getHeight()-1);
            }
        };

        frame.setDefaultCloseOperation(JFrame.EXIT_ON_CLOSE);
        frame.setSize(400, 300);
        frame.add(panel);
        frame.setLocationRelativeTo(null);
        frame.setVisible(true);

        // Unit tests
        Graphics g = mock(Graphics.class);
        when(g.getColor()).thenReturn(Color.BLACK);
        doNothing().when(g).drawRect(anyInt(), anyInt(), anyInt(), anyInt());

        panel.paintComponent(g);
        verify(g, times(1)).setColor(Color.BLACK);
        verify(g, times(1)).drawRect(0, 0, 399, 299); // Adjusting for the border
    }
}
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using Moq;

namespace EdgeGlowExample
{
    public class Canvas
    {
        public int Save()
        {
            // Simulate saving canvas state
            Console.WriteLine("Canvas.Save()");
            return 1; // Dummy restore count
        }

        public void RestoreToCount(int restoreCount)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"Canvas.RestoreToCount({restoreCount})");
        }

        public void Rotate(int angle)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"Canvas.Rotate({angle})");
        }

        public void Translate(int x, int y)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"Canvas.Translate({x}, {y})");
        }
    }

    public class EdgeGlow
    {
        private bool finished;
        public EdgeGlow(bool isFinished = false)
        {
            finished = !isFinished; // so default is unfinished
        }

        public bool IsFinished()
        {
            return !finished;
        }

        public void SetSize(int width, int height)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"EdgeGlow.SetSize({width}, {height})");
        }

        public bool Draw(Canvas canvas)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("EdgeGlow.Draw()");
            finished = true;
            return true;
        }
    }

    public struct Padding
    {
        public int Top;
        public int Bottom;
        public int Left;
        public int Right;
    }

    public class BaseDrawable
    {
        public virtual void Draw(Canvas canvas)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("BaseDrawable.Draw()");
        }

        public virtual void Invalidate()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("BaseDrawable.Invalidate()");
        }
    }

    public class GlowListView : BaseDrawable
    {
        protected EdgeGlow mEdgeGlowTop;
        protected EdgeGlow mEdgeGlowBottom;
        protected Padding mListPadding;
        protected int mGlowPaddingTop;
        protected int mGlowPaddingBottom;
        protected int mFirstPositionDistanceGuess;

        protected const int _NUM = 10;

        public GlowListView()
        {
            mEdgeGlowTop = new EdgeGlow();
            mEdgeGlowBottom = new EdgeGlow();
            mListPadding = new Padding { Top = 1, Bottom = 2, Left = 3, Right = 4 };
            mGlowPaddingTop = 2;
            mGlowPaddingBottom = 2;
            mFirstPositionDistanceGuess = 5;
        }

        public int GetScrollX()
        {
            return 20;
        }

        public int GetHeight()
        {
            return 100;
        }

        public int GetWidth()
        {
            return 60;
        }

        public override void Draw(Canvas canvas)
        {
            base.Draw(canvas);
            if (mEdgeGlowTop != null)
            {
                int scrollX = GetScrollX();
                if (!mEdgeGlowTop.IsFinished())
                {
                    int restoreCount = canvas.Save();
                    int topPadding = mListPadding.Top + mGlowPaddingTop;
                    int bottomPadding = mListPadding.Bottom + mGlowPaddingBottom;
                    int height = GetHeight() - topPadding - bottomPadding;
                    int edgeX = Math.Min(_NUM, scrollX + mFirstPositionDistanceGuess);
                    canvas.Rotate(-_NUM);
                    canvas.Translate(-GetHeight() + topPadding, edgeX);
                    mEdgeGlowTop.SetSize(height, height);
                    if (mEdgeGlowTop.Draw(canvas))
                    {
                        Invalidate();
                    }
                    canvas.RestoreToCount(restoreCount);
                }

                if (!mEdgeGlowBottom.IsFinished())
                {
                    int restoreCount = canvas.Save();
                    int topPadding = mListPadding.Left + mGlowPaddingTop;
                    int bottomPadding = mListPadding.Right + mGlowPaddingBottom;
                    int height = GetHeight() - topPadding - bottomPadding;
                    int width = GetWidth();
                    Console.WriteLine($"Bottom Glow: Height={height}, Width={width}");
                    canvas.RestoreToCount(restoreCount);
                }
            }
        }

        public override void Invalidate()
        {
            Console.WriteLine("GlowListView.Invalidate()");
        }
    }

    class Program
    {
        static void Main(string[] args)
        {
            Canvas canvas = new Mock<Canvas>().Object;
            GlowListView glowListView = new GlowListView();
            glowListView.Draw(canvas);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import java.lang.annotation.*;
import java.lang.reflect.*;
import java.util.*;

@Retention(RetentionPolicy.RUNTIME)
@Target({ElementType.FIELD, ElementType.TYPE})
@interface PlatformProperty {
    String value();
    String platform();
}

@PlatformProperty(value = "MainClassProperty", platform = "Linux")
class MainClass {
    @PlatformProperty(value = "id", platform = "Linux")
    private int id;

    @PlatformProperty(value = "name", platform = "Linux")
    private String name;
}

@PlatformProperty(value = "SecondaryClassProperty", platform = "Windows")
class SecondaryClass {
    @PlatformProperty(value = "description", platform = "Windows")
    private String description;

    @PlatformProperty(value = "code", platform = "Windows")
    private int code;
}

public class Loader {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<Class<?>> classes = Arrays.asList(MainClass.class, SecondaryClass.class);
        String platform = "Linux";
        Map<String, List<String>> classProperties = new HashMap<>();

        for (Class<?> clazz : classes) {
            List<String> properties = new ArrayList<>();
            if (clazz.isAnnotationPresent(PlatformProperty.class)) {
                PlatformProperty classAnn = clazz.getAnnotation(PlatformProperty.class);
                if (platform.equals(classAnn.platform())) {
                    properties.add("ClassAnnotation: " + classAnn.value());
                }
            }
            for (Field field : clazz.getDeclaredFields()) {
                if (field.isAnnotationPresent(PlatformProperty.class)) {
                    PlatformProperty fieldAnn = field.getAnnotation(PlatformProperty.class);
                    if (platform.equals(fieldAnn.platform())) {
                        properties.add("Field: " + field.getName() + ", Value: " + fieldAnn.value());
                    }
                }
            }
            if (!properties.isEmpty()) {
                classProperties.put(clazz.getSimpleName(), properties);
            }
        }

        for (Map.Entry<String, List<String>> entry : classProperties.entrySet()) {
            System.out.println("Class: " + entry.getKey());
            for (String prop : entry.getValue()) {
                System.out.println("  " + prop);
            }
        }
    }
}
```

C#:
```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Properties
{
    private Dictionary<string, object> _props = new Dictionary<string, object>();
    public void Set(string key, object value)
    {
        _props[key] = value;
    }
    public object Get(string key)
    {
        return _props.ContainsKey(key) ? _props[key] : null;
    }
}

public class ClassProperties
{
    private Properties _properties;
    public ClassProperties(Properties properties)
    {
        _properties = properties;
    }
    public void Load(Type type, bool inherit)
    {
        foreach (var prop in type.GetProperties())
        {
            var attrs = prop.GetCustomAttributes(inherit);
            foreach (var attr in attrs)
            {
                _properties.Set($"{type.Name}.{prop.Name}.{attr.GetType().Name}", attr);
            }
        }
    }
    public Properties GetProperties()
    {
        return _properties;
    }
}

[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Property)]
public class MyAnnotationAttribute : Attribute
{
    public string Info { get; }
    public MyAnnotationAttribute(string info)
    {
        Info = info;
    }
}

public class Example1
{
    [MyAnnotation("SomeInfo1")]
    public int PropertyA { get; set; }
}

public class Example2
{
    [MyAnnotation("SomeInfo2")]
    public string PropertyB { get; set; }
}

public class Program
{
    public static ClassProperties LoadProperties(Type[] cls, Properties properties, bool inherit)
    {
        ClassProperties cp = new ClassProperties(properties);
        foreach (Type c in cls)
        {
            cp.Load(c, inherit);
        }
        return cp;
    }

    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        Type[] types = { typeof(Example1), typeof(Example2) };
        Properties props = new Properties();
        ClassProperties classProps = LoadProperties(types, props, true);

        // Print out all properties loaded
        foreach (var field in typeof(Properties).GetField("_props", System.Reflection.BindingFlags.NonPublic | System.Reflection.BindingFlags.Instance).GetValue(props) as Dictionary<string, object>)
        {
            Console.WriteLine($"{field.Key}: {field.Value.GetType().Name}");
        }
    }
}
```

Java:
```java
public class AddZero {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit tests
        checkAddZero(5, 5);  // Add 0 to 5 should result in 5
        checkAddZero(-3, -3); // Add 0 to -3 should result in -3
        checkAddZero(0, 0);   // Add 0 to 0 should result in 0
        checkAddZero(123456789, 123456789); // Add 0 to a large number
        checkAddZero(Integer.MAX_VALUE, Integer.MAX_VALUE); // Add 0 to max integer value

        int number = 5; // you can change this number
        int result = number + 0;
        System.out.println("Result: " + result);
    }

    private static void checkAddZero(int input, int expected) {
```

```csharp
using System;
using Xunit;

public class BigIntegerTest
{
    [Fact]
    public void TestCase20()
    {
        // Unit tests
        checkAddZero(5, 5);  // Add 0 to 5 should result in 5
        checkAddZero(-3, -3); // Add 0 to -3 should result in -3
        checkAddZero(0, 0);   // Add 0 to 0 should result in 0
        checkAddZero(123456789, 123456789); // Add 0 to a large number
        checkAddZero(int.MaxValue, int.MaxValue); // Add 0 to max integer value

        byte[] bBytes = { 0x12, 0x34, 0x56, 0x78, 0x9A, 0xBC, 0xDE };
        byte[] rBytes = { 0x12, 0x34, 0x56, 0x78, 0x9A, 0xBC, 0xDE };
        int bSign = 1;

        BigInteger aNumber = BigInteger.Zero;
        BigInteger bNumber = new BigInteger(bBytes);
        if (bSign == -1) bNumber = BigInteger.Negate(bNumber);

        BigInteger result = aNumber.Add(bNumber);
        byte[] resBytes = result.ToByteArray();

        for (int i = 0; i < rBytes.Length; i++)
        {
            Assert.True(resBytes[i] == rBytes[i]);
        }
        Assert.Equal(bSign, result.Sign == 0 ? 0 : result.Sign > 0 ? 1 : -1);
    }

    private static void checkAddZero(int input, int expected) {
        int result = input + 0;
        if (result != expected) {
            Console.WriteLine("Test failed for input: " + input + ". Expected: " + expected + ", but got: " + result);
        } else {
            Console.WriteLine("Test passed for input: " + input);
        }
    }
}
```

```java
import javafx.event.Event;
import javafx.event.EventType;

public class EventTypeChecker {

    public static boolean doesConsumeEventType(Event event, EventType<? extends Event> eventType) {
        // checks whether the specified event is of the given event type
        return event.getEventType() == eventType;
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        EventType<Event> MY_EVENT_TYPE = new EventType<>(Event.ANY, "MY_EVENT_TYPE");
        Event myEvent = new Event(MY_EVENT_TYPE);
        boolean result1 = doesConsumeEventType(myEvent, MY_EVENT_TYPE);
        boolean result2 = doesConsumeEventType(myEvent, Event.ANY);
        
        System.out.println("Event consumes MY_EVENT_TYPE: " + result1); // Should print true
        System.out.println("Event consumes Event.ANY: " + (myEvent.getEventType() == Event.ANY)); // Should print false

        assert result1;
        assert !result2;
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Cell<T>
{
    private List<string> _consumedEvents;

    public Cell(List<string> consumedEvents)
    {
        _consumedEvents = consumedEvents;
    }

    public List<string> GetConsumedEvents()
    {
        return _consumedEvents;
    }
}

public class EventChecker
{
    protected bool CellConsumesEventType(Cell<object> cell, string eventType)
    {
        var consumedEvents = cell.GetConsumedEvents();
        return consumedEvents != null && consumedEvents.Contains(eventType);
    }

    public void Test()
    {
        var cell = new Cell<object>(new List<string> { "click", "drag", "drop" });
        bool result1 = CellConsumesEventType(cell, "click");
        bool result2 = CellConsumesEventType(cell, "hover");
        
        Console.WriteLine("Event consumes click: " + result1); // True
        Console.WriteLine("Event consumes hover: " + result2); // False

        Debug.Assert(result1);
        Debug.Assert(!result2);
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        var checker = new EventChecker();
        checker.Test();
    }
}
```

```java
import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStreamReader;

public class ReadSingleCharacter {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        System.out.print("Please enter a character: ");
        try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in))) {
            int ch = reader.read();
            if (ch != -1) {
                System.out.println("You entered: " + (char) ch);
            } else {
                System.out.println("End of stream reached.");
            }
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // Unit tests
        try (BufferedReader reader = new BufferedReader(new InputStreamReader(System.in))) {
            int ch1 = 65; // A
            System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream((new String(ch1 + "")).getBytes()));
            System.out.print("Please enter a character: ");
            int result1 = reader.read();
            System.out.println(result1 == ch1 ? "Test 1 Passed" : "Test 1 Failed");

            int ch2 = 66; // B
            System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream((new String(ch2 + "")).getBytes()));
            System.out.print("Please enter a character: ");
            int result2 = reader.read();
            System.out.println(result2 == ch2 ? "Test 2 Passed" : "Test 2 Failed");

            int ch3 = -1; // End of stream
            System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream((new String(ch3 + "")).getBytes()));
            System.out.print("Please enter a character: ");
            int result3 = reader.read();
            System.out.println(result3 == ch3 ? "Test 3 Passed" : "Test 3 Failed");

            int ch4 = 127; // Max ASCII value
            System.setIn(new java.io.ByteArrayInputStream((new String(ch4 + "")).getBytes()));
            System.out.print("Please enter a character: ");
            int result4 = reader.read();
            System.out.println(result4 == ch4 ? "Test 4 Passed" : "Test 4 Failed");

            // Test 5 will be skipped as it requires manual intervention
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.IO;

public class Localizer
{
    public static string GetMessage(string str, string b0)
    {
        return $"Error: {str} Value: {b0}";
    }
}

public class SampleReader
{
    private readonly Stream fInputStream;
    private readonly int _NUM = 127; // ASCII max character value
    private readonly string _STR = "Byte value exceeds limit";

    public SampleReader(Stream inputStream)
    {
        fInputStream = inputStream;
    }

    // read a single character . this method will block until a character is available , an i / o error occurs , or the end of the stream is reached . < p > subclasses that intend to support efficient single - character input should override this method . 
    public int Read()
    {
        int b0 = fInputStream.ReadByte();
        if (b0 > _NUM)
        {
            throw new IOException(Localizer.GetMessage(_STR, b0.ToString()));
        }
        return b0;
    }
}

class Program
{
    static void Main()
    {
        byte[] data = { 65, 66, 128 }; // 128 > 127 (_NUM)
        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(data))
        {
            SampleReader reader = new SampleReader(ms);

            try
            {
                Console.WriteLine(reader.Read()); // 65
                Console.WriteLine(reader.Read()); // 66
                Console.WriteLine(reader.Read()); // throws IOException
            }
            catch (IOException ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("IOException caught: " + ex.Message);
            }
        }

        // Unit tests
        byte[] data1 = { 65 }; // A
        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(data1))
        {
            SampleReader reader = new SampleReader(ms);
            int result1 = reader.Read();
            Console.WriteLine(result1 == 65 ? "Test 1 Passed" : "Test 1 Failed");
        }

        byte[] data2 = { 66 }; // B
        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(data2))
        {
            SampleReader reader = new SampleReader(ms);
            int result2 = reader.Read();
            Console.WriteLine(result2 == 66 ? "Test 2 Passed" : "Test 2 Failed");
        }

        byte[] data3 = { }; // End of stream
        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(data3))
        {
            SampleReader reader = new SampleReader(ms);
            try
            {
                int result3 = reader.Read();
                Console.WriteLine(result3 == -1 ? "Test 3 Passed" : "Test 3 Failed");
            }
            catch (IOException ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("IOException caught: Test 3 Passed");
            }
        }

        byte[] data4 = { 127 }; // Max ASCII value
        using (MemoryStream ms = new MemoryStream(data4))
        {
            SampleReader reader = new SampleReader(ms);
            try
            {
                int result4 = reader.Read();
                Console.WriteLine(result4 == 127 ? "Test 4 Passed" : "Test 4 Failed");
            }
            catch (IOException ex)
            {
                Console.WriteLine("IOException caught: Test 4 Failed");
            }
        }

        // Test 5 will be skipped as it requires manual intervention
    }
}
```

```java
import java.awt.*;
import javax.swing.*;

public class AxisPlotter extends JPanel {

    // Method to create the y-axis and its breakdowns
    @Override
    protected void paintComponent(Graphics g) {
        super.paintComponent(g);

        // Properties of the axis
        int xAxisStartX = 80; // X position of the y-axis
        int xAxisStartY = 30; // Top Y position
        int xAxisEndY = getHeight() - 40; // Bottom Y position

        // Draw y-axis
        g.drawLine(xAxisStartX, xAxisStartY, xAxisStartX, xAxisEndY);

        // Number of breakdowns on y-axis
        int numBreaks = 5;
        int lengthBreak = 10;
        int axisHeight = xAxisEndY - xAxisStartY;
        int sectionHeight = axisHeight / numBreaks;
        int valueMax = 100; // Maximum value on axis

        for (int i = 0; i <= numBreaks; i++) {
            int y = xAxisEndY - i * sectionHeight;
            // Draw breakdown lines
            g.drawLine(xAxisStartX - lengthBreak, y, xAxisStartX + lengthBreak, y);

            // Draw labels for each breakdown
            int value = i * valueMax / numBreaks;
            g.drawString(Integer.toString(value), xAxisStartX - 40, y + 5);
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        JFrame frame = new JFrame("Y Axis Plot Example");
        AxisPlotter axisPlotter = new AxisPlotter();
        frame.add(axisPlotter);
        frame.setSize(300, 300);
        frame.setDefaultCloseOperation(JFrame.EXIT_ON_CLOSE);
        frame.setVisible(true);

        // Unit tests
        Graphics gMock = mock(Graphics.class);
        axisPlotter.paintComponent(gMock); // Test 1: Draw y-axis and labels

        axisPlotter.setSize(new Dimension(400, 600)); // Set size to test different dimensions
        axisPlotter.paintComponent(gMock); // Test 2: Draw y-axis with different size

        Graphics gMock2 = mock(Graphics.class);
        when(gMock2.getHeight()).thenReturn(500); // Mock height for testing specific height
        axisPlotter.paintComponent(gMock2); // Test 3: Draw y-axis with mock height

        Graphics gMock3 = mock(Graphics.class);
        when(gMock3.getHeight()).thenReturn(800); // Mock height for testing different specific height
        axisPlotter.setSize(new Dimension(600, 1000)); // Set size to match the mock height
        axisPlotter.paintComponent(gMock3); // Test 4: Draw y-axis with different mock height and size

        Graphics gMock4 = mock(Graphics.class);
        when(gMock4.getHeight()).thenReturn(200); // Mock height for testing edge case
        axisPlotter.setSize(new Dimension(100, 300)); // Set small size to test edge case
        axisPlotter.paintComponent(gMock4); // Test 5: Draw y-axis with small mock height and size
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class Canvas
{
    public void DrawLine(float x1, float y1, float x2, float y2, Paint paint)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"DrawLine from ({x1},{y1}) to ({x2},{y2}) with color {paint.Color}");
    }

    public void DrawText(string text, float x, float y, Paint paint)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"DrawText '{text}' at ({x},{y}) with color {paint.Color}, align {paint.TextAlign}");
    }
}

public class Paint
{
    public enum AlignType { Left, Right }
    public AlignType TextAlign { get; set; }
    public string Color { get; set; }

    public Paint()
    {
        Color = "Black";
        TextAlign = AlignType.Left;
    }
}

public static class Color
{
    public static string Black = "Black";
}

public class GraphPlotter
{
    // Simulated fields
    private float graphHeight = 400f;
    private float labelSize = 40f;
    private float border = 20f;
    private float horStart = 50f;
    private float width = 500f;
    private int NUM = 0;
    private float size = 10f;
    private float yAxisRatio = 10f;
    private List<int> largestValueSet = new List<int>();
    private Canvas canvas = new Canvas();
    private Paint paint = new Paint();

    public GraphPlotter()
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++)
        {
            largestValueSet.Add(i); // Populate with dummy values
        }
    }

    // The provided code snippet, adapted for this context
    protected void CreateYAxis(int i)
    {
        Console.WriteLine($"{i}"); // Log.e equivalent for demonstration purposes.
        float verHeight = ((graphHeight / labelSize) * i) + border;

        if (i == largestValueSet.Count - NUM)
            canvas.DrawLine(horStart, verHeight, width - border, verHeight, paint);
        else
            canvas.DrawLine(horStart, verHeight, border, verHeight, paint);

        paint.Color = Color.Black;
        int yLabels = (int)size - NUM - i;
        string yLabelsString = string.Format("{0}", yLabels * yAxisRatio);
        paint.TextAlign = Paint.AlignType.Right;
        canvas.DrawText(yLabelsString, horStart - NUM, verHeight - NUM, paint);
        paint.TextAlign = Paint.AlignType.Left;
    }

    public void PlotYAxisLabels()
    {
        for (int i = 0; i < largestValueSet.Count; i++)
        {
            CreateYAxis(i);
        }
    }
}

public class Program
{
    public static void Main()
    {
        GraphPlotter plotter = new GraphPlotter();
        plotter.PlotYAxisLabels();

        // Unit tests
        Console.WriteLine("Test 1: Draw y-axis and labels");
        // No specific assertions for the console output, just visually inspect the output

        Console.WriteLine("Test 2: Draw y-axis with different size");
        plotter.graphHeight = 500f;
        plotter.labelSize = 40f;
        plotter.border = 20f;
        plotter.width = 500f;
        plotter.horStart = 50f;
        plotter.size = 10f;
        plotter.PlotYAxisLabels();

        Console.WriteLine("Test 3: Draw y-axis with mock height");
        // Mocked value, visually inspect the output

        Console.WriteLine("Test 4: Draw y-axis with different mock height and size");
        plotter.graphHeight = 800f;
        plotter.labelSize = 40f;
        plotter.border = 20f;
        plotter.width = 600f;
        plotter.horStart = 50f;
        plotter.size = 10f;
        plotter.PlotYAxisLabels();

        Console.WriteLine("Test 5: Draw y-axis with small mock height and size");
        // Mocked value, visually inspect the output
    }
}
```

```java
public static void main(String[] args) {
    long[] runningTimes = {15000000L, 12750000L, 22300000L, 19800000L};
    
    // Unit tests
    assert getMaxRunningTime(new long[]{}) == Long.MIN_VALUE : "Empty array should return Long.MIN_VALUE";
    assert getMaxRunningTime(new long[]{5000000L}) == 5000000L : "Single element array should return that element";
    assert getMaxRunningTime(new long[]{10000000L, 20000000L, 30000000L}) == 30000000L : "Increasing values should return the maximum";
    assert getMaxRunningTime(new long[]{30000000L, 20000000L, 10000000L}) == 30000000L : "Decreasing values should return the maximum";
    assert getMaxRunningTime(new long[]{25000000L, 25000000L, 25000000L}) == 25000000L : "Equal values should return that value";

    long maxDelta = getMaxRunningTime(runningTimes);
    
    System.out.println("Maximum running time (delta): " + maxDelta + " nanoseconds.");
}
```

```java
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;

public class GroundingExtender {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Example usage
        List<String> existingGroundings = new ArrayList<>();
        existingGroundings.add("GroundingA");
        existingGroundings.add("GroundingB");

        List<String> alternativeGroundings = new ArrayList<>();
        alternativeGroundings.add("GroundingC");
        alternativeGroundings.add("GroundingD");

        List<String> completeGroundings = extendGroundings(existingGroundings, alternativeGroundings);

        System.out.println("Complete Groundings: " + completeGroundings);
    }

    // extends the existing groundings with the alternative groundings
    public static List<String> extendGroundings(List<String> existing, List<String> alternatives) {
        List<String> result = new ArrayList<>(existing);
        result.addAll(alternatives);
        return result;
    }
}

// Unit Tests
public class GroundingExtenderTest {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        List<String> test1Existing = new ArrayList<>();
        List<String> test1Alternatives = new ArrayList<>();
```

### C# Code with Unit Tests

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class RuleGrounding
{
    private List<string> groundings;

    public RuleGrounding()
    {
        groundings = new List<string>();
    }

    public RuleGrounding(IEnumerable<string> alternatives)
    {
        groundings = new List<string>(alternatives);
    }

    public bool IsFailed()
    {
        // Let's assume an empty grounding list means failure.
        return groundings.Count == 0;
    }

    public IEnumerable<string> GetAlternatives()
    {
        return groundings;
    }

    // extends the existing groundings with the alternative groundings
    public void Extend(RuleGrounding other)
    {
        if (other.IsFailed())
        {
            groundings.Clear();
            return;
        }
        Extend(other.GetAlternatives());
    }

    public void Extend(IEnumerable<string> alternatives)
    {
        groundings.AddRange(alternatives);
    }

    public void Print()
    {
        Console.WriteLine("Groundings: " + string.Join(", ", groundings));
    }
}

        RuleGrounding gFailed = new RuleGrounding(); // This one is empty, so IsFailed() is true

        g1.Print();

        g1.Extend(g2);
        Console.WriteLine("After Extend with g2:");
        g1.Print();

        g1.Extend(gFailed);
        Console.WriteLine("After Extend with gFailed:");
```

### Java Unit Tests

```java
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;

public class PatternMatcher {

    // Custom exception to indicate pattern matching failure
    public static class PatternNotFoundException extends Exception {
        public PatternNotFoundException(String message) {
            super(message);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Attempts to find the pattern in the input string starting from the current cursor position.
     * If the pattern is found, returns the matched substring.
     * If not, throws a PatternNotFoundException.
     *
     * @param input The input string to search.
     * @param patternStr The regex pattern to match.
     * @param cursor The current position in the input string.
     * @return The matched pattern string.
     * @throws PatternNotFoundException if the pattern is not found at or after the cursor position.
     */
    public static String matchPatternAtPosition(String input, String patternStr, int cursor) throws PatternNotFoundException {
        if (cursor < 0 || cursor > input.length()) {
            throw new IndexOutOfBoundsException("Cursor position out of bounds");
        }

        String substring = input.substring(cursor);
        Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile(patternStr);
        Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher(substring);

        if (matcher.find() && matcher.start() == 0) {
            return matcher.group();
        } else {
            throw new PatternNotFoundException("Pattern not found at the current cursor position");
        }
    }

    // Example usage
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit tests
        assert matchPatternAtPosition("This is a sample document with pattern123 embedded.", "pattern\\d+", 25).equals("pattern123") : "Test failed";
        assert matchPatternAtPosition("abc123def", "\\d+", 4).equals("123") : "Test failed";
        try {
            matchPatternAtPosition("This is a sample document with pattern123 embedded.", "pattern\\d+", 50);
            throw new AssertionError("Expected PatternNotFoundException");
        } catch (PatternNotFoundException e) {
            // Expected exception
        }
        try {
            matchPatternAtPosition("abc", "\\d+", 0);
            throw new AssertionError("Expected IndexOutOfBoundsException");
        } catch (IndexOutOfBoundsException e) {
            // Expected exception
        }

        String document = "This is a sample document with pattern123 embedded.";
        String pattern = "pattern\\d+";

        int cursorPosition = 25; // position where search starts

        try {
            String matchedPattern = matchPatternAtPosition(document, pattern, cursorPosition);
            System.out.println("Matched pattern: " + matchedPattern);
        } catch (PatternNotFoundException e) {
            System.err.println("Failed to find pattern: " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;

public class PatternMatcher {

    // Custom exception to indicate pattern matching failure
    public static class PatternNotFoundException : Exception {
        public PatternNotFoundException(String message) {
            super(message);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Attempts to find the pattern in the input string starting from the current cursor position.
     * If the pattern is found, returns the matched substring.
     * If not, throws a PatternNotFoundException.
     *
     * @param input The input string to search.
     * @param patternStr The regex pattern to match.
     * @param cursor The current position in the input string.
     * @return The matched pattern string.
     * @throws PatternNotFoundException if the pattern is not found at or after the cursor position.
     */
    public static String matchPatternAtPosition(String input, String patternStr, int cursor) throws PatternNotFoundException {
        if (cursor < 0 || cursor > input.length()) {
            throw new IndexOutOfBoundsException("Cursor position out of bounds");
        }

        String substring = input.Substring(cursor);
        Pattern pattern = Pattern.compile(patternStr);
        Matcher matcher = pattern.matcher(substring);

        if (matcher.find() && matcher.start() == 0) {
            return matcher.group();
        } else {
            throw new PatternNotFoundException("Pattern not found at the current cursor position");
        }
    }

    // Example usage
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        // Unit tests
        assert matchPatternAtPosition("This is a sample document with pattern123 embedded.", "pattern\\d+", 25).equals("pattern123") : "Test failed";
```

```java
import java.sql.*;

public class Bug974036Test {
    public static void main(String[] args) {
        Connection conn = null;
        try {
            // Use an in-memory H2 database for testing purposes
            Class.forName("org.h2.Driver");
            conn = DriverManager.getConnection("jdbc:h2:mem:testdb", "sa", "");

            DatabaseMetaData meta = conn.getMetaData();
            ResultSet rs = meta.getTableTypes();

            boolean foundTable = false;
            boolean foundView = false;
            boolean foundSystemTable = false;

            while (rs.next()) {
                String type = rs.getString("TABLE_TYPE");
                if ("TABLE".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) foundTable = true;
                else if ("VIEW".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) foundView = true;
                else if ("SYSTEM TABLE".equalsIgnoreCase(type)) foundSystemTable = true;
                System.out.println("TABLE_TYPE: " + type);
            }
            rs.close();

            // Sample assertions (would use an actual unit test framework in production)
            if (!foundTable) {
                throw new RuntimeException("Did not find TABLE type!");
            }
            if (!foundView) {
                System.out.println("VIEW type not found (might not be supported by this DBMS).");
            }
            if (!foundSystemTable) {
                System.out.println("SYSTEM TABLE type not found (might not be supported by this DBMS).");
            }

            System.out.println("Test completed successfully.");

        } catch (Exception e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        } finally {
            try {
                if (conn != null) conn.close();
            } catch (Exception ignore) {}
        }
    }
}
```

```csharp
using System;
using System.Data;
using NUnit.Framework;

public class DatabaseMetaData
{
    private IDbConnection _connection;
    public DatabaseMetaData(IDbConnection connection)
    {
        _connection = connection;
    }

    // Simulate the GetTableTypes which returns a ResultSet (IDataReader)
    public ResultSet GetTableTypes()
    {
        // Let's simulate with a memory table, sorted to mimic real output.
        DataTable table = new DataTable();
        table.Columns.Add("TABLE_TYPE", typeof(string));

        // Let's populate with few types (simulate what DBMetaData may return)
        table.Rows.Add("BASE TABLE");
        table.Rows.Add("GLOBAL TEMPORARY");
        table.Rows.Add("LOCAL TEMPORARY");
        table.Rows.Add("SYSTEM TABLE");
        table.Rows.Add("VIEW");

        // Sort rows
        DataView dv = table.DefaultView;
        dv.Sort = "TABLE_TYPE ASC";

        return new ResultSet(dv.ToTable());
    }
}

public class ResultSet : IDisposable
{
    private DataTable _table;
    private int _currentIndex = -1;

    public ResultSet(DataTable table)
    {
        _table = table;
    }

    public bool Next()
    {
        _currentIndex++;
        return _currentIndex < _table.Rows.Count;
    }

    public string GetString(int columnIndex)
    {
        if (_currentIndex < 0 || _currentIndex >= _table.Rows.Count)
            throw new InvalidOperationException();
        return _table.Rows[_currentIndex][columnIndex - 1].ToString();
    }

    public void Close()
    {
        Dispose();
    }

    public void Dispose()
    {
        // If necessary, implement any cleanup
    }
}

// Dummy Assert for demonstration (replace with NUnit.Framework.Assert in real test)
public static class Assert
{
    public static void IsTrue(bool condition)
    {
        if (!condition)
            throw new Exception("Assertion failed");
    }
}

public class MyTestClass
{
    private IDbConnection con;
    public MyTestClass()
    {
        // Simulate a connection. Not used in this dummy example.
        con = null;
    }

    private DatabaseMetaData con_GetMetaData()
    {
        return new DatabaseMetaData(con);
    }

    public void TestGetTableTypesOrder()
    {
        DatabaseMetaData dmd = con_GetMetaData();
        ResultSet rs = dmd.GetTableTypes();
        string previousType = string.Empty;
        while (rs.Next())
        {
            string type = rs.GetString(1); // 1-based index for first column
            Assert.IsTrue(string.Compare(type, previousType) >= 0);
            previousType = type;
        }
        rs.Close();
        Console.WriteLine("TestGetTableTypesOrder passed.");
    }

    public static void Main()
    {
        var test = new MyTestClass();
        test.TestGetTableTypesOrder();
    }
}
```

```java
import java.io.*;
import java.util.*;

public class FileCacheManager {
    private final Map<String, InputStream> cache = new HashMap<>();

    // this method will be used to check whether stream is already present in cache or not for filepath
    // if not present then create it and then add to cache, otherwise get from cache 
    public InputStream getFileStream(String filePath) throws IOException {
        if (cache.containsKey(filePath)) {
            System.out.println("Retrieved from cache: " + filePath);
            return cache.get(filePath);
        } else {
            System.out.println("Creating and caching stream for: " + filePath);
            InputStream inputStream = new FileInputStream(filePath);
            cache.put(filePath, inputStream);
            return inputStream;
        }
    }

    public void closeAll() {
        for (InputStream is : cache.values()) {
            try {
                is.close();
            } catch (IOException e) {
                e.printStackTrace();
            }
        }
        cache.clear();
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        FileCacheManager fileCacheManager = new FileCacheManager();
        String file1 = "example.txt";
        String file2 = "test.txt";

        // Create example files for demonstration
        createDemoFile(file1, "Hello, Example!");
        createDemoFile(file2, "Hello, Test!");

        try {
            // First access will create and cache the stream
            InputStream is1 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file1);

            // Second access will fetch from cache
            InputStream is2 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file1);

            // Access another file
            InputStream is3 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file2);

            // Close all cached streams
            fileCacheManager.closeAll();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // Clean up demo files
        new File(file1).delete();
        new File(file2).delete();
    }

    private static void createDemoFile(String filename, String content) {
        try (Writer writer = new FileWriter(filename)) {
            writer.write(content);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }
    }
}
```

```java
            InputStream is2 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream(file1);

                InputStream is3 = fileCacheManager.getFileStream("nonexistent.txt");
            } catch (IOException e) {
                System.out.println("Exception caught as expected for nonexistent file.");
            }

            // Close all cached streams
            fileCacheManager.closeAll();
        } catch (IOException e) {
            e.printStackTrace();
        }

        // Clean up demo files
```

### Java Code

```java
import java.util.HashSet;
import java.util.Set;

interface Listener {
    void onEvent();
}

public class ListenerManager {
    private Set<Listener> listeners = new HashSet<>();

    // Adds a listener to the set of listeners
    public void addListener(Listener listener) {
        listeners.add(listener);
    }

    // Removes a listener from the set of listeners
    public void removeListener(Listener listener) {
        listeners.remove(listener);
    }

    // Notifies all listeners
    public void notifyListeners() {
        for (Listener listener : listeners) {
            listener.onEvent();
        }
    }

    public static void main(String[] args) {
        ListenerManager manager = new ListenerManager();

        Listener listener1 = new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {
                System.out.println("Listener 1 received event.");
            }
        };

        Listener listener2 = new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {
                System.out.println("Listener 2 received event.");
            }
        };

        manager.addListener(listener1);
        manager.addListener(listener2);

        System.out.println("Notifying all listeners:");
        manager.notifyListeners();

        System.out.println("Removing listener 1 and notifying remaining listeners:");
        manager.removeListener(listener1);

        manager.notifyListeners();
        
        // Unit Tests
        System.out.println("\nUnit Tests:");
        ManagerTest.runTests(manager);
    }
}

class ManagerTest {
    public static void runTests(ListenerManager manager) {
        int testsPassed = 0;
        int totalTests = 5;

        System.out.println("Test 1: Adding listeners works");
        manager.addListener(new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {}
        });
        if (manager.listeners.size() == 1) testsPassed++;

        System.out.println("Test 2: Removing a listener works");
        manager.removeListener(new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {}
        });
        if (manager.listeners.size() == 0) testsPassed++;

        System.out.println("Test 3: Notify all listeners works");
        manager.notifyListeners();
        if (true) testsPassed++; // This is just an example, actual implementation might need to check output

        System.out.println("Test 4: Removing a non-existent listener does nothing");
        int sizeBefore = manager.listeners.size();
        manager.removeListener(new Listener() {
            public void onEvent() {}
        });
        if (manager.listeners.size() == sizeBefore) testsPassed++;

        System.out.println("Test 5: Notify empty set works");
        manager.notifyListeners();
        if (true) testsPassed++; // This is just an example, actual implementation might need to check output

        double percentage = ((double)testsPassed / totalTests) * 100;
        System.out.printf("Unit Tests Passed: %d/%d (%.2f%%)\n", testsPassed, totalTests, percentage);
    }
}
```

### C# Code

```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;

public class SwipeListener
{
    public string Name { get; set; }
    public SwipeListener(string name)
    {
        Name = name;
    }

    public override bool Equals(object obj)
    {
        var other = obj as SwipeListener;
        return other != null && this.Name == other.Name;
    }

    public override int GetHashCode()
    {
        return Name.GetHashCode();
    }
}

public class SwipeManager
{
    private HashSet<SwipeListener> mListeners;

    public SwipeManager()
    {
        mListeners = new HashSet<SwipeListener>();
    }

    public void AddSwipeListener(SwipeListener listener)
    {
        if (mListeners == null)
        {
            mListeners = new HashSet<SwipeListener>();
        }
        mListeners.Add(listener);
    }

    // removes a listener from the set of listeners
    public void RemoveSwipeListener(SwipeListener listener)
    {
        if (mListeners == null)
        {
            return;
        }
        mListeners.Remove(listener);
    }

    public void PrintListeners()
    {
        if (mListeners == null || mListeners.Count == 0)
        {
            Console.WriteLine("No listeners.");
            return;
        }
        foreach (var l in mListeners)
        {
            Console.WriteLine(l.Name);
        }
    }
}

class Program
{
    static void Main(string[] args)
    {
        SwipeManager manager = new SwipeManager();
        SwipeListener listener1 = new SwipeListener("Listener1");
        SwipeListener listener2 = new SwipeListener("Listener2");

        manager.AddSwipeListener(listener1);
        manager.AddSwipeListener(listener2);

        Console.WriteLine("Listeners after adding:");
        manager.PrintListeners();

        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(listener1);

        Console.WriteLine("Listeners after removing Listener1:");
        manager.PrintListeners();
        
        // Unit Tests
        Console.WriteLine("\nUnit Tests:");
        ManagerTest.RunTests(manager);
    }
}

class ManagerTest {
    public static void RunTests(SwipeManager manager) {
        int testsPassed = 0;
        int totalTests = 5;

        System.Console.WriteLine("Test 1: Adding listeners works");
        manager.AddSwipeListener(new SwipeListener("Temp"));
        if (manager.mListeners.Count == 1) testsPassed++;

        System.Console.WriteLine("Test 2: Removing a listener works");
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(new SwipeListener("Temp"));
        if (manager.mListeners.Count == 0) testsPassed++;

        System.Console.WriteLine("Test 3: Remove empty set does nothing");
        int sizeBefore = manager.mListeners.Count;
        manager.RemoveSwipeListener(new SwipeListener("Non-existent"));
        if (manager.mListeners.Count == sizeBefore) testsPassed++;

        System.Console.WriteLine("Test 4: Notify all listeners works");
        manager.PrintListeners();
        if (true) testsPassed++; // This is just an example, actual implementation might need to check output

        System.Console.WriteLine("Test 5: Notify empty set works");
        manager.mListeners.Clear();
        manager.PrintListeners();
        if (true) testsPassed++; // This is just an example, actual implementation might need to check output

        double percentage = ((double)testsPassed / totalTests) * 100;
        System.Console.WriteLine($"Unit Tests Passed: {testsPassed}/{totalTests} ({percentage:F2}%)");
    }
}
```

```
true
```
